# Aṭṭhakanipāta (AN 8)

> **Nikāya:** Aṅguttaranikāya
> **Nipāta:** AN 8 (Aṭṭhakanipāta)
> **PTS Volume:** Aṅguttara Nikāya Vol. 4
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

##### *[PTS Page 156]*

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na appiyapasaṃsī ca hoti na piyagarahī ca na lābhakāmo ca[^156-1] na sakkārakāmo ca[^156-1] hirimā ca hoti[^156-2] ottappī ca appiccho ca sammādiṭṭhi ca. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo cā ti. IV.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo ca hoti amanāpo ca agaru ca abhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu lābhakāmo ca hoti sakkārakāmo ca anavaññattikāmo[^156-3] ca[^156-3] akālaññū ca amattaññū ca asuci ca[^156-3] bahubhāṇī ca akkosakaparibhāsako ca sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho[^156-1] bhikkhave[^156-4] aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyo[^156-5] ca hoti amanāpo[^156-6] ca agaru[^156-7] ca abhāvanīyo[^156-8] ca[^156-9].

3. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo ca. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na lābhakāmo ca hoti na sakkārakāmo ca na anavaññattikāmo ca kālaññū ca mattaññū ca suci ca na bahubhāṇī ca[^156-1] na[^156-10] akkosakaparibhāsako[^156-10] ca sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyo ca hoti manāpo ca garu ca bhāvanīyo cā ti. V.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave lokadhammā lokaṃ anuparivattanti, loko ca aṭṭha lokadhamme anuparivattati. Katame aṭṭha?


<!-- PTS Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 157]*

2. Lābho ca alābho ca yaso[^157-1] ca[^157-2] ayaso ca[^157-2] nindā ca pasaṃsā ca sukhañ ca dukkhañ ca. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha lokadhammā lokaṃ anuparivattanti, loko ca ime[^157-2] aṭṭha[^157-3] lokadhamme anuparivattatī ti. Lābho alābho ca[^157-4] yaso[^157-5] ayaso[^157-5] ca nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhañ ca[^157-6] dukkhaṃ[^157-7]: ete aniccā manujesu dhammā asassatā vipariṇāmadhammā, ete ca ñatvā[^157-8] satimā sumedho avekkhati[^157-9] vipariṇāmadhamme. Iṭṭhassa dhammā na[^157-8] mathenti cittaṃ aniṭṭhato no[^157-10] paṭighātam eti, tassānurodhā atha vā virodhā vidhūpitā[^157-11] atthagatā[^157-12] na santi, padañ ca ñatvā virajaṃ asokaṃ sammappajānāti bhavassa pāragū ti. VI.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave lokadhammā lokaṃ anuparivattanti, loko ca aṭṭha lokadhamme anuparivattati. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Lābho ca alābho ca yaso[^157-1] ca ayaso ca nindā ca pasaṃsā ca sukhañ ca dukkhañ ca. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha lokadhammā lokaṃ anuparivattanti, loko ca ime aṭṭha lokadhamme anuparivattati.

3. Assutavato bhikkhave puthujjanassa uppajjati lābho pi alābho pi yaso[^157-1] pi ayaso pi nindā pi pasaṃsā pi sukhaṃ pi dukkhaṃ pi. Sutavato pi[^157-13] bhikkhave ariyasāvakassa uppajjati lābho pi alābho pi yaso[^157-1] pi ayaso pi nindā pi pasaṃsā pi sukhaṃ pi dukkhaṃ pi. Tatra bhikkhave ko


<!-- PTS Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 158]*

viseso, ko adhippāyoso[^158-1], kin nānākaraṇaṃ sutavato ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthujjanenā ti? ‘Bhagavaṃmūlakā[^158-2] no bhante dhammā Bhagavaṃnettikā[^158-3] Bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā[^158-2]; sādhu vata bhante Bhagavantaṃ yeva paṭibhātu etassa bhāsitassa attho, Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī'; ti. Tena hi bhikkhave suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

4. Assutavato bhikkhave puthujjanassa uppajjati lābho. So na iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘uppanno kho me ayaṃ lābho, so ca kho anicco dukkho vipariṇāmadhammo'; ti yathābhūtaṃ na ppajānāti . . . uppajjati alābho . . .[^158-4] uppajjati yaso . . . uppajjati ayaso . . . uppajjati nindā . . . uppajjati pasaṃsā . . . uppajjati sukhaṃ . . . uppajjati dukkhaṃ. So na iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘uppannaṃ kho me idaṃ dukkhaṃ, tañ ca kho aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhamman'; ti yathābhūtaṃ na[^158-5] ppajānāti. Tassa lābho pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, alābho pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, yaso pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, ayaso pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, nindā pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, pasaṃsā pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, sukhaṃ pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, dukkhaṃ pi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. So uppannaṃ lābhaṃ anurujjhati, alābhe paṭivirujjhati, uppannaṃ yasaṃ anurujjhati, ayase paṭivirujjhati, uppannaṃ pasaṃsaṃ anurujjhati, nindāya paṭivirujjhati, uppannaṃ sukhaṃ anurujjhati, dukkhe paṭivirujjhati. So evaṃ anurodhavirodhasamāpanno na parimuccati jātiyā jarāya[^158-6] maraṇena[^158-6] sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi, na parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi.

5. Sutavato ca kho bhikkhave ariyasāvakassa uppajjati lābho. So iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘uppanno kho me ayaṃ lābho, so ca kho anicco dukkho vipariṇāmadhammo'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^158-7] . . .uppajjati alābho . . .[^158-8] uppajjati yaso . . .


<!-- PTS Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 159]*

uppajjati ayaso . . . uppajjati nindā[^159-1] . . . uppajjati pasaṃsā . . . uppajjati sukhaṃ . . . uppajjati dukkhaṃ. So iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘uppannaṃ kho me idaṃ dukkhaṃ, tañ ca kho aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhamman'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti[^159-2]. Tassa lābho pi cittaṃ na[^159-3] pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, alābho pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, yaso pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, ayaso pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, nindā pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, pasaṃsā pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, sukhaṃ pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, dukkhaṃ pi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. So[^159-4] uppannaṃ lābhaṃ nānurujjhati, alābhe na ppaṭivirujjhati, uppannaṃ[^159-5] yasaṃ nānurujjhati, ayase na ppaṭivirujjhati, uppannaṃ pasaṃsaṃ nānurujjhati, nindāya na ppaṭivirujjhati, uppannaṃ sukhaṃ nānurujjhati, dukkhe na ppaṭivirujjhati. So evaṃ anurodhavirodhavippahīno[^159-6] parimuccati jātiyā jarāya[^159-7] maraṇena[^159-7] sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi[^159-3], parimuccati dukkhasmā ti vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho bhikkhave viseso, ayaṃ adhippāyoso[^159-8], idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavato ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthujjanenā ti. Lābho alābho ca[^159-9] yaso[^159-10] ayaso[^159-10] ca nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhañ ca[^159-11] dukkhaṃ[^159-12]: ete aniccā manujesu dhammā asassatā vipariṇāmadhammā, ete ca ñatvā satimā sumedho avekkhati vipariṇāmadhamme. Iṭṭhassa[^159-13] dhammā na mathenti[^159-14] cittaṃ aniṭṭhato no paṭighātam eti,


<!-- PTS Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 160]*

tassānurodhā atha vā virodhā vidhūpitā[^160-1] atthagatā[^160-2] na santi, padañ ca ñatvā virajaṃ asokaṃ sammappajānāti bhavassa pāragū ti. VII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate acirapakkante Devadatte. Tatra kho[^160-3] Bhagavā Devadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantesi:--

2. Sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attavipattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ paravipattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ parasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave asaddhammehi abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto[^160-4] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

3. Lābhena bhikkhave abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto[^160-5] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho . . . alābhena bhikkhave . . .[^160-6] yasena bhikkhave . . . ayasena bhikkhave . . . sakkārena bhikkhave . . . asakkārena[^160-7] bhikkhave[^160-7] . . . pāpicchatāya bhikkhave . . . pāpamittatāya bhikkhave abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto[^160-8] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi asaddhammehi abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto[^160-9] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho.

4. Sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^160-10] vihareyya, uppannam alābhaṃ . . .[^160-6] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ[^160-11] ayasaṃ[^160-11] . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ


<!-- PTS Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 161]*

. . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^161-1] vihareyya. Kathañ[^161-2] ca bhikkhave bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭicca uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^161-3] vihareyya . . . pe[^161-4] . . .? Yaṃ hi'; ssa bhikkhave uppannaṃ lābhaṃ anabhibhuyya[^161-5] viharato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^161-5] viharato evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Yaṃ hi'; ssa[^161-6] bhikkhave uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^161-7] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ anabhibhuyya[^161-5] viharato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^161-5] viharato evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭicca uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^161-8] vihareyya[^161-8], uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^161-9] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^161-10] vihareyya. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--

5. Uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^161-11] viharissāma[^161-12], uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^161-13] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^161-10] viharissāmā[^161-14] ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabban ti.


<!-- PTS Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 162]*

VIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Uttaro Mahisavatthusmiṃ viharati Saṃkheyyake pabbate Dhavajālikāyaṃ[^162-1]. Tatra kho āyasmā Uttaro bhikkhū āmantesi . . . pe[^162-2] . . . [^162-2]. Sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attavipattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ paravipattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ parasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hotī ti.

3. Tena kho pana samayena Vessavaṇo[^162-3] mahārājā uttarāya[^162-4] disāya[^162-4] dakkhiṇaṃ[^162-5] disaṃ gacchati kenaci-d-eva karaṇīyena. Assosi kho Vessavaṇo mahārājā āyasmato Uttarassa Mahisavatthusmiṃ Saṃkheyyake pabbate Dhavajālikāyaṃ bhikkhunaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ desentassa: sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attavipattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ paravipattiṃ[^162-6] paccavekkhitā hoti; sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ parasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hotī ti.

4. Atha kho Vessavaṇo mahārājā seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^162-7] vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^162-8], evam eva Mahisavatthusmiṃ Saṃkheyyake pabbate Dhavajālikāyaṃ antarahito devesu Tāvatiṃsesu pāturahosi. Atha kho Vessavaṇo mahārājā yena Sakko devānam indo ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Sakkaṃ devānam indaṃ etad avoca ‘yagghe[^162-9] mārisa[^162-9] jāneyyāsi eso āyasmā Uttaro Mahisavatthusmiṃ Saṃ-


<!-- PTS Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 163]*

kheyyake pabbate Dhavajālikāyaṃ[^163-1] bhikkhūnaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti[^163-2]: sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attavipattiṃ paccavekkhitā[^163-3] hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ paravippattiṃ . . .[^163-4] attasampattiṃ[^163-5] . . . parasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hotī'; ti.

5. Atha kho Sakko devānam indo seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya, evam eva devesu Tāvatiṃsesu antarahito Mahisavatthusmiṃ Saṃkheyyake pabbate Dhavajālikāyaṃ[^163-6] āyasmato Uttarassa sammukhe[^163-7] pāturahosi. Atha kho Sakko devānam indo yenāyasmā Uttaro ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Uttaraṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhito kho Sakko devānam indo āyasmantaṃ Uttaraṃ etad avoca ‘saccaṃ kira bhante āyasmā Uttaro bhikkhūnaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ desesi: sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attavipattiṃ paccavekkhitā[^163-3] hoti, sādhāvuso bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ paravippattiṃ . . .[^163-8] attasampattiṃ . . . parasampattiṃ paccavekkhitā hotī'; ti? ‘Evaṃ devānam indā'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pan'; idaṃ[^163-9] bhante āyasmato Uttarassa sakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ[^163-10] udāhu tassa Bhagavato vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassā'; ti?

6. ‘Tena hi devānam inda upaman te karissāmi, upamāya[^163-11] pi[^163-12] idh'; ekacce viññū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti[^163-13]. Seyyathā pi devānam inda gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūre mahādhaññarāsi[^163-14], tato mahājanakāyo dhaññaṃ[^163-15] āhareyya kācehi[^163-16] pi piṭakehi[^163-17] pi ucchaṅgehi[^163-18]


<!-- PTS Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 164]*

pi añjalīhi[^164-1] pi. Yo nu kho devānam inda taṃ[^164-2] mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā evaṃ puccheyya: kuto imaṃ dhaññaṃ[^164-3] āharathā[^164-4] ti? Kathaṃ vyākaramāno nu kho devānam inda so[^164-5] mahājanakāyo sammā vyākaramāno[^164-6] vyākareyyā'; ti? ‘Amumhā mahādhaññarāsimhā[^164-7] āharāmā ti kho bhante so mahājanakāyo sammā vyākaramāno[^164-6] vyākareyyā'; ti. ‘Evam eva kho devānam inda yaṃ[^164-8] kiñci subhāsitaṃ, sabban taṃ tassa Bhagavato vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, tato upādāy'; upādāya mayañ[^164-9] c'; aññe[^164-10] ca bhaṇāmā'; ti.

7. Acchariyaṃ bhante abbhutaṃ bhante, yāva[^164-11] subhāsitam[^164-12] idaṃ[^164-12] āyasmatā Uttarena: yaṃ kiñci subhāsitaṃ, sabban taṃ tassa[^164-13] Bhagavato vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, tato upādāy'; upādāya[^164-14] mayaṃ c'; aññe[^164-10] ca bhaṇāmā ti[^164-15]. Ekam[^164-16] idaṃ bhante Uttara[^164-17] samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe[^164-18] pabbate acirapakkante Devadatte. Tatra kho[^164-19] Bhagavā Devadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantesi: Sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ attavipattiṃ paccavekkhitā hoti, sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu kālena kālaṃ paravipattiṃ . . .[^164-20] attasampattiṃ . . . parasampattiṃ[^164-9] paccavekkhitā hoti. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave asaddhammehi abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto[^164-21] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho. Katamehi aṭṭhahi? Lābhena bhikkhave abhibhūto . . . pe[^164-22] . . .


<!-- PTS Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 165]*

pāpamittatāya bhikkhave abhibhūto pariyādinnacitto[^165-1] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi asaddhammehi abhibhūto pariyādinna citto[^165-2] Devadatto āpāyiko nerayiko kappaṭṭho atekiccho. Sādhu bhikkhave bhikkhu uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^165-3] vihareyya, uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^165-4] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^165-5] vihareyya.

8. Kathañ[^165-6] ca[^165-6] bhikkhave bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭicca uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^165-7] vihareyya, uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^165-8]uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^165-9] vihareyya? Yaṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhave uppannaṃ lābhaṃ anabhibhuyya[^165-10] viharato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^165-11] viharato evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Yaṃ hi'; ssa bhikkhave uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^165-4] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ anabhibhuyya[^165-10] viharato uppajjeyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^165-11] viharato evaṃsa te āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na honti. Idaṃ kho[^165-12] bhikkhave bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭicca uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^165-13] vihareyya[^165-14], uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^165-4] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^165-13] vihareyya. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--


<!-- PTS Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 166]*

9. Uppannaṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya[^166-1] viharissāma, uppannaṃ alābhaṃ . . .[^166-2] uppannaṃ yasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ ayasaṃ . . . uppannaṃ sakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ asakkāraṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpicchataṃ . . . uppannaṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya[^166-1] viharissāmā ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabban ti.

10. Yāvatā[^166-3] bhante Uttara manussesu catasso parisā: bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo, nāyaṃ dhammapariyāyo kismiñci[^166-4] patiṭṭhito[^166-5]. Uggaṇhātu bhante āyasmā Uttaro imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ, pariyāpuṇātu bhante āyasmā Uttaro imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ, dhāretu[^166-6] bhante āyasmā Uttaro imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ. Atthasaṃhito[^166-7] ayaṃ bhante dhammapariyāyo ādibrahmacariyiko[^166-8] ti. IX.

1. ‘Kulaputto'; ti bhikkhave Nandaṃ sammā vadamāno vadeyya, ‘balavā[^166-9]'; ti bhikkhave Nandaṃ sammā vadamāno vadeyya, ‘pāsādiko'; ti bhikkhave Nandaṃ sammā[^166-10] vadamāno[^166-10] vadeyya, ‘tibbarāgo[^166-11]'; ti bhikkhave Nandaṃ sammā vadamāno vadeyya. Kim[^166-12] aññatra[^166-13] bhikkhave Nando indriyesu guttadvāro bhojane[^166-14] mattaññū jāgariyaṃ anuyutto satisampajaññena samannāgato, yena[^166-15] Nando sakkoti paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ[^166-16]?

2. Tatr'; idaṃ[^166-17] bhikkhave Nandassa indriyesu guttadvāratāya[^166-18] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 167]*

Sace bhikkhave Nandassa puratthimā disā āloketabbā hoti, sabbañ[^167-1] cetaso[^167-2] samannāharitvā Nando puratthimaṃ disaṃ āloketi ‘evaṃ me[^167-1] puratthimaṃ[^167-3] disaṃ[^167-4] ālokayato nābhijjhā domanassā pāpakā[^167-5] akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī[^167-6]'; ti[^167-7]. Iti ha tattha[^167-8] sampajāno hoti. Sace bhikkhave Nandassa pacchimā disā āloketabbā hoti . . .[^167-9] uttarā disā[^167-10] āloketabbā[^167-10] hoti . . . dakkhiṇā disā[^167-10] āloketabbā[^167-10] hoti . . . uddhaṃ ulloketabbaṃ[^167-11] hoti[^167-12] . . . adho oloketabbaṃ[^167-13] hoti . . . anudisā anuviloketabbā[^167-14] hoti, sabbañ cetaso[^167-15] samannāharitvā Nando anudisaṃ[^167-16] anuviloketi ‘evaṃ me anudisaṃ anuvilokayato nābhijjhā domanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī[^167-6]'; ti[^167-17]. Iti ha tattha[^167-8] sampajāno hoti. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave Nandassa indriyesu guttadvāratāya hoti.

3. Tatr'; idaṃ bhikkhave Nandassa bhojane mattaññutāya hoti[^167-18]. Idha bhikkhave Nando paṭisaṅkhā yoniso āhāraṃ[^167-19] āhāreti[^167-20] neva davāya na madāya[^167-21] na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya yāva-d-eva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya ‘iti purāṇañ ca vedanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi navañ ca vedanaṃ na uppādessāmi, yātrā ca me bhavissati anavajjatā ca phāsuvihāro cā'; ti. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave Nandassa bhojane mattaññutāya hoti.

4. Tatr'; idaṃ bhikkhave Nandassa jāgariyānuyogasmiṃ[^167-22] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 168]*

Idha bhikkhave Nando divasaṃ caṅkamena nisajjāya āvaraṇiyehi dhammehi cittaṃ parisodheti, rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ[^168-1] caṅkamena nisajjāya āvaraṇiyehi dhammehi cittaṃ parisodheti, rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ[^168-2] dakkhiṇena passena[^168-3] sīhaseyyaṃ kappeti[^168-4] pāde[^168-5] pādaṃ accādhāya[^168-6] sato sampajāno uṭṭhānasaññaṃ manasikaritvā, rattiyā[^168-7] pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ paccuṭṭhāya caṅkamena nisajjāya āvaraṇiyehi dhammehi cittaṃ parisodheti. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave Nandassa jāgariyānuyogasmiṃ hoti.

5. Tatr'; idaṃ bhikkhave Nandassa satisampajaññasmiṃ hoti. Idha bhikkhave Nandassa viditā vedanā[^168-1] uppajjanti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti[^168-8], viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā saññā . . .[^168-9] viditā vitakkā[^168-10] uppajjanti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave Nandassa satisampajaññasmiṃ hoti[^168-11]. Kim aññatra[^168-12] bhikkhave Nando indriyesu guttadvāro bhojane mattaññū jāgariyam anuyutto satisampajaññena samannāgato, yena[^168-13] Nando sakkoti paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritun ti? X.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Campāyaṃ[^168-1] viharati Gaggarāya[^168-14] pokkharaṇiyā[^168-14] tīre. Tena kho pana samayena bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno aññen'; aññaṃ[^168-15] paṭicarati[^168-16], bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmeti[^168-17], kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti. Atha kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--


<!-- PTS Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 169]*

2. Dhamath[^169-1]'; etaṃ bhikkhave puggalaṃ, niddhamath' etaṃ bhikkhave puggalaṃ. Apaneyyo[^169-2] so[^169-3] bhikkhave puggalo. Kiṃ vo[^169-4] paraputtā[^169-5] viheṭheti[^169-6]? Idha bhikkhave ekaccassa puggalassa tādisaṃ yeva hoti abhikkantaṃ paṭikkantaṃ ālokitaṃ vilokitaṃ[^169-7] sammiñjitaṃ[^169-8] pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, seyyathā pi aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yāv'; assa bhikkhū[^169-9] āpattiṃ na passanti; yato ca khvāssa[^169-10] bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tam enaṃ evaṃ jānanti ‘samaṇadūsī[^169-11]'; vāyaṃ[^169-12] samaṇapalāpo[^169-13] samaṇakāraṇḍavo[^169-14]'; ti. Tam enaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsenti[^169-15]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā aññe bhaddake bhikkhū dūsesī[^169-16] ti.

3. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave sampanne[^169-17] yavakaraṇe[^169-18] yavadūsī[^169-19] jāyetha yavapalāpo[^169-13] yavakāraṇḍavo[^169-20], tassa tādisaṃ yeva mūlaṃ hoti, seyyathā pi aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ yavānaṃ. Tādisaṃ yeva nālaṃ[^169-21] hoti, seyyathā pi aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ yavānaṃ, tādisaṃ yeva pattaṃ[^169-22] hoti, seyyathā pi aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ yavānaṃ, yāv'; assa sīsaṃ na[^169-23] nibbattati; yato ca khvāssa sīsaṃ nibbattati, tam enaṃ evaṃ jānanti ‘yavadūsī[^169-24] 'vāyaṃ[^169-25] yavapalāpo[^169-13]


<!-- PTS Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 170]*

yavakāraṇḍavo[^170-1]'; ti. Tam enaṃ iti viditvā samūlaṃ uppāṭetvā[^170-2] bahiddhā yavakaraṇassa chaḍḍenti[^170-3]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā[^170-4] aññe[^170-4] bhaddake yave dūsesī[^170-5] ti. Evaṃ eva kho bhikkhave idh'; ekaccassa puggalassa tādisaṃ yeva hoti abhikkantaṃ paṭikkantaṃ ālokitaṃ vilokitaṃ sammiñjitaṃ[^170-6] pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, seyyathā pi aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yāv'; assa bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti; yato ca khvāssa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tam enaṃ evaṃ jānanti ‘samaṇadūsī[^170-7] 'vāyaṃ[^170-8] samaṇapalāpo[^170-9] samaṇakāraṇḍavo[^170-10]'; ti. Tam enaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā[^170-4] aññe[^170-4] bhaddake bhikkhū dūsesī[^170-11] ti.

4. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave mahato dhaññarāsissa vuvahyamānassa[^170-12] tattha yāni[^170-13] tāni[^170-14] dhaññāni daḷhāni sāravantāni, tāni[^170-15] ekamantaṃ puñjo[^170-16] hoti[^170-17]; yāni pana tāni dhaññāni dubbalāni palāpāni[^170-18], tāni vāto ekamantaṃ apavahati[^170-19]. Tam enaṃ sāmikā sammajjaniṃ[^170-20] gahetvā bhiyyosomattāya apasammajjanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā[^170-4] aññe[^170-4] bhaddake[^170-21] dhaññe dūsesī ti. Evam eva kho bhikkhave idh'; ekaccassa puggalassa tādisaṃ yeva hoti abhikkantaṃ paṭikkantaṃ ālokitaṃ vilokitaṃ sammiñjitaṃ[^170-22] pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, seyyathā pi[^170-23] aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yāv'; assa bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti; yato ca khvāssa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tam enaṃ evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 171]*

jānanti ‘samaṇadūsī[^171-1] 'vāyaṃ[^171-2] samaṇapalāpo[^171-3] samaṇakāraṇḍavo[^171-4]'; ti. Tam enaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā[^171-5] aññe[^171-5] bhaddake[^171-6] bhikkhū dūsesī ti.

5. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave puriso udapānapanāliyā[^171-7] atthiko[^171-7] tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ[^171-8] ādāya vanaṃ paviseyya, so taṃ[^171-9] tad[^171-10] eva rukkhaṃ kuṭhāripāsena[^171-11] ākoṭeti[^171-12]. Tattha yāni[^171-13] tāni rukkhāni daḷhāni sāravantāni, tāni[^171-14] kuṭhāripāsena[^171-8] ākoṭitāni kakkhaḷaṃ[^171-15] paṭinadanti; yāni pana tāni rukkhāni antopūtīni avassutāni kasambujātāni, tāni[^171-14] kuṭhāripāsena[^171-8] ākoṭitāni daddaraṃ paṭinadanti. Tam[^171-16] enaṃ[^171-16] mūle[^171-17] chindati[^171-16], mūle[^171-18] chinditvā[^171-19] agge chindati, agge chinditvā[^171-20] anto suvisodhitaṃ[^171-21] visodheti, anto suvisodhitaṃ visodhetvā udapānapanāliṃ[^171-22] yojeti. Evam eva kho bhikkhave idh' ekaccassa puggalassa tādisaṃ yeva hoti abhikkantaṃ paṭikkantaṃ ālokitaṃ vilokitaṃ sammiñjitaṃ[^171-23] pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, seyyathā pi aññesaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yāv'; assa bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti; yato ca khvāssa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tam enaṃ evaṃ jānanti ‘samaṇadūsī[^171-1] 'vāyaṃ[^171-24] samaṇapalāpo[^171-3] samaṇakāraṇḍavo[^171-4]'; ti. Tam enaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsenti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā[^171-5] aññe[^171-5] bhaddake bhikkhū dūsesī ti.


<!-- PTS Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 172]*

Saṃvāsāyaṃ[^172-1] vijānātha[^172-2] pāpiccho[^172-3] kodhano iti makkhī thambhī palāsī ca issukī maccharī saṭho saṇhavāco[^172-4] janavati samaṇo[^172-5] viya bhāsati raho karoti karaṇaṃ[^172-6] pāpadiṭṭhi anādaro saṃsappī[^172-7] ca musāvādī taṃ[^172-8] viditvā yathā kathaṃ? Sabbe samaggā hutvāna abhinibbajjayātha[^172-9] naṃ kāraṇḍavaṃ[^172-10] niddhamatha[^172-11] kasambuṃ[^172-12] apakassatha[^172-13]. Tato palāpe[^172-14] vāhetha assamaṇe[^172-15] samaṇamānino[^172-16] niddhamitvāna[^172-17] pāpicche[^172-18] pāpa-ācāragocare[^172-19] {suddhā suddhehi} saṃvāsaṃ kappayavho patissatā[^172-20], tato samaggā nipakā dukkhass'; antaṃ karissathā ti. Mettāvaggo[^172-21] paṭhamo. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^172-22]: Mettaṃ[^172-23] paññā[^172-24] ca dve[^172-25] piyā[^172-26] dve ca lokavipattiyo Devadatto ca Uttaro Nando kārandavena[^172-27] cā ti. XI[^172-28].

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Verañjāyaṃ viharati Naḷerupucimandamūle[^172-29]. Atha kho Verañjo


<!-- PTS Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 173]*

brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^173-1] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Verañjo brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sutaṃ me[^173-2] taṃ[^173-3] bho[^173-4] Gotama: na[^173-5] samaṇo Gotamo brāhmaṇe jiṇṇe vuddhe[^173-6] mahallake addhagate vayo-anuppatte abhivādeti vā paccuṭṭheti vā āsanena vā nimantetī ti[^173-7]. Tayidaṃ[^173-8] bho Gotama tath'; eva, na hi bhavaṃ Gotamo brāhmaṇe jiṇṇe vuddhe[^173-9] mahallake addhagate vayo-anuppatte abhivādeti vā paccuṭṭheti vā āsanena vā nimanteti. Tayidaṃ bho Gotama na sampannam evā'; ti. ‘Nāhan taṃ brāhmaṇa passāmi sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya, yam ahaṃ abhivādeyyaṃ vā paccuṭṭheyyaṃ vā āsanena vā nimanteyyaṃ. Yaṃ hi brāhmaṇa Tathāgato abhivādeyya vā paccuṭṭheyya vā āsanena vā nimanteyya, muddhā pi tassa vipateyyā'; ti.

2. ‘Arasarūpo bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi khv esa[^173-10] brāhmaṇa[^173-11] pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: arasarūpo samaṇo Gotamo ti. Ye te brāhmaṇa[^173-12] rūparasā saddarasā gandharasā rasarasā phoṭṭhabbarasā, te Tathāgatassa pahīnā[^173-13] ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā[^173-14] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: arasarūpo samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho[^173-15] yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^173-16].


<!-- PTS Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 174]*

3. ‘Nibbhogo bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi khv esa[^174-1] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: nibbhogo samaṇo Gotamo ti. Ye te brāhmaṇa rūpabhogā saddabhogā gandhabhogā rasabhogā phoṭṭhabbabhogā, te Tathāgatassa pahīnā[^174-2] ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: nibbhogo samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ[^174-3] sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^174-4].

4. ‘Akiriyavādo bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi khv esa[^174-5] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo ti. Ahaṃ hi[^174-6] brāhmaṇa akiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyaduccaritassa vacīduccaritassa manoduccaritassa, anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ akiriyaṃ vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^174-4].

5. ‘Ucchedavādo bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi khv esa[^174-5] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: ucchedavādo samaṇo Gotamo ti. Ahaṃ hi brāhmaṇa ucchedaṃ vadāmi rāgassa dosassa mohassa, anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ucchedaṃ vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: ucchedavādo samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^174-4].

6. ‘Jegucchī bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi khv esa[^174-5] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ[^174-7] pariyāyena[^174-7] sammā vadamāno vadeyya: jegucchī samaṇo Gotamo ti. Ahaṃ hi brāhmaṇa jigucchāmi[^174-8] kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena[^174-7] manoduccaritena[^174-7], jigucchāmi[^174-9] anekavihitānaṃ[^174-10] papakānaṃ akusalānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 175]*

dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā[^175-1]. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: jegucchī samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^175-2].

7. ‘Venayiko bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi[^175-3] khv esa[^175-4] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: venayiko samaṇo Gotamo ti. Ahaṃ hi brāhmaṇa vinayāya[^175-5] dhammaṃ desemi rāgassa dosassa mohassa, anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vinayāya dhammaṃ desemi. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya[^175-6]: venayiko samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^175-2].

8. ‘Tapassī bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi khv esa[^175-7] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: tapassī samaṇo Gotamo ti. Tapanīyāhaṃ brāhmaṇa pāpake akusale dhamme vadāmi, kāyaduccaritaṃ vacīduccaritaṃ manoduccaritaṃ. yassa kho brāhmaṇa tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tam ahaṃ tapassī ti vadāmi. Tathāgatassa kho brāhmaṇa tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: tapassī samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesī'; ti[^175-2].

9. ‘Apagabbho bhavaṃ Gotamo'; ti. ‘Atthi[^175-3] khv esa[^175-8] brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: apagabbho samaṇo Gotamo ti. Yassa kho brāhmaṇa āyatiṃ gabbhaseyyā[^175-9] punabbhavābhinibbatti[^175-10] pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ


<!-- PTS Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 176]*

anuppādadhammā, tam ahaṃ apagabbho ti vadāmi. Tathāgatassa kho brāhmaṇa āyatiṃ gabbhaseyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: apagabbho samaṇo Gotamo ti; no ca kho yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadesi. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa kukkuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā, tān'; assu kukkuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni sammā pariseditāni[^176-1] sammā paribhāvitāni; yo nu kho tesaṃ kukkuṭapotakānaṃ[^176-2] paṭhamataraṃ pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakena vā aṇḍakosaṃ padāletvā[^176-3] sotthinā[^176-4] abhinibbijjheyya, kinti svāssa[^176-5] vacanīyo[^176-6] jeṭṭho vā kaniṭṭho vā'; ti? ‘Jeṭṭho ti 'ssa bho Gotama vacanīyo[^176-7], so hi nesaṃ bho[^176-8] Gotama[^176-8] jeṭṭho hotī'; ti.

10. ‘Evam eva kho ahaṃ brāhmaṇa avijjāgatāya pajāya aṇḍabhūtāya pariyonaddhāya avijjaṇḍakosaṃ padāletvā eko 'va[^176-9] loke anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambuddho. Ahaṃ hi[^176-10] brāhmaṇa jeṭṭho[^176-8] seṭṭho lokassa. Āraddhaṃ kho pana me brāhmaṇa viriyaṃ ahosi asallīnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati apamuṭṭhā[^176-11], passaddho kāyo asāraddho, samāhitaṃ[^176-12] cittaṃ ekaggaṃ.

11. ‘So kho ahaṃ brāhmaṇa vivicc'; eva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ[^176-13] jhānaṃ[^176-13] upasampajja vihāsiṃ[^176-14]; vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā[^176-15] ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ[^176-8] pītisukhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 177]*

dutiyaṃ[^177-1] jhānaṃ[^177-1] upasampajja vihāsiṃ; pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca vihāsiṃ sato ca sampajāno[^177-2], sukhañ ca kāyena paṭisaṃvedesiṃ[^177-3], yan taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti: upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī ti, tatiyaṃ[^177-1] jhānaṃ[^177-1] upasampajja vihāsiṃ; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubb'; eva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^177-4] adukkhamasukhaṃ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ[^177-5] jhānaṃ[^177-5] upasampajja vihāsiṃ.

12. ‘So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte[^177-6] pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ[^177-7]. So[^177-8] anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarāmi, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo[^177-9] tisso pi jātiyo catasso pi jātiyo pañca pi jātiyo dasa pi jātiyo vīsam pi jātiyo tiṃsam pi jātiyo cattāḷīsam pi jātiyo paññāsam pi jātiyo, jātisatam pi jātisahassam pi jātisatasahassam[^177-10] pi[^177-10], aneke pi saṃvaṭṭakappe aneke pi vivaṭṭakappe aneke pi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe <<amutrāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto[^177-10] amutra udapādiṃ, tatrāpāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto idhūpapanno>> ti. Iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarāmi. Ayaṃ kho me brāhmaṇa rattiyā paṭhame yāme paṭhamā vijjā adhigatā, avijjā vihatā[^177-11] vijjā uppannā, tamo vihato[^177-12] āloko uppanno yathātaṃ appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato. Ayaṃ kho me brāhmaṇa paṭhamā abhinibbidhā[^177-13] ahosi kukkuṭacchāpakass'; eva[^177-14] aṇḍakosamhā.


<!-- PTS Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 178]*

13. ‘So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte[^178-1] sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ[^178-2]. So dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^178-3] satte passāmi cavamāne upapajjamāne, hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage [^178-4] satte pajānāmi[^178-5] <<ime vata bhonto sattā kāyaduccaritena samannāgatā vacīduccaritena samannāgatā manoduccaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapannā; ime vā pana bhonto sattā kāyasucaritena samannāgatā vacīsucaritena samannāgatā manosucaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapannā>> ti. Iti dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passāmi cavamāne upapajjamāne, hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāmi. Ayaṃ kho me brāhmaṇa rattiyā majjhime yāme dutiyā vijjā adhigatā, avijjā vihatā[^178-6] vijjā uppannā, tamo vihato[^178-7] āloko uppanno yathātaṃ appamattassa ātapino pahitattassa viharato. Ayaṃ kho me brāhmaṇa dutiyā abhinibbidhā[^178-8] ahosi kukkuṭacchāpakass' eva aṇḍakosamhā.

14. ‘So evaṃ samāhite citte parisuddhe pariyodāte anaṅgaṇe vigatūpakkilese mudubhūte kammaniye ṭhite āneñjappatte[^178-1] āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya[^178-9] cittaṃ abhininnāmesiṃ[^178-10]. So <<idaṃ dukkhan>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, <<ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, <<ayaṃ[^178-11] dukkhanirodho>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, <<ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ; <<ime āsavā>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ,


<!-- PTS Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 179]*

<<ayaṃ[^179-1] āsavasamudayo>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, <<ayaṃ āsavanirodho>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ, <<ayaṃ āsavanirodhagāminī paṭipadā>> ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhaññāsiṃ. Tassa me evaṃ jānato evaṃ passato kāmāsavā pi cittaṃ vimuccittha, bhavāsavā pi cittaṃ vimuccittha, avijjāsavā pi cittaṃ vimuccittha, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttam[^179-2] iti[^179-2] ñāṇaṃ ahosi. <<Khīṇā[^179-3] jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā>> ti abbhaññāsiṃ. Ayaṃ kho me brāhmaṇa rattiyā pacchime yāme tatiyā vijjā adhigatā, avijjā vihatā[^179-4] vijjā uppannā, tamo vihato[^179-5] āloko uppanno yathātaṃ appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato. Ayaṃ kho me brāhmaṇa tatiyā abhinibbidhā[^179-6] ahosi kukkuṭacchāpakass'; eva aṇḍakosamhā'; ti.

15. Evaṃ vutte[^179-7] Verañjo brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: Jeṭṭho bhavaṃ Gotamo, seṭṭho bhavaṃ Gotamo[^179-8]. Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama abhikkantaṃ[^179-9] bho Gotama, seyyathā pi bho Gotama nikkujjitaṃ vā ukkujjeyya, paṭicchannaṃ vā vivareyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkheyya, andhakāre vā telapajjotaṃ dhāreyya ‘cakkhumanto rūpāni dakkhantī'; ti: evam eva[^179-10] bhotā Gotamena anekapariyāyena dhammo pakāsito. Esāhaṃ bhavantaṃ Gotamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ ca bhikkhusaṅghañ ca, upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti. XII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā abhiññātā abhiññātā Licchavī santhāgāre[^179-11] sannisinnā sannipatitā anekapariyāyena buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti[^179-12], dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti[^179-12], saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti[^179-12].


<!-- PTS Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 180]*

2. Tena kho pana samayena Sīho senāpati Nigaṇṭhasāvako[^180-1] tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisinno hoti. Atha kho Sīhassa senāpatissa etad ahosi ‘nissaṃsayaṃ[^180-2] kho so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho bhavissati, tathā h'; ime sambahulā abhiññātā[^180-3] abhiññātā[^180-3] Licchavī santhāgāre sannisinnā sannipatitā anekapariyāyena buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti; yan nūnāhaṃ taṃ Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkameyyaṃ[^180-4] arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhan'; ti.

3. Atha kho Sīho senāpati yena Nigaṇṭho[^180-5] Nāṭaputto[^180-6] ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Nigaṇṭhaṃ[^180-7] Nāṭaputtaṃ[^180-6] etad avoca ‘icchām'; ahaṃ bhante samaṇaṃ Gotamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pana tvaṃ Sīha kiriyavādo samāno akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ Gotamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? Samaṇo hi Sīha Gotamo akiriyavādo akiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī'; ti. Atha kho Sīhassa senāpatissa yo ahosi gamiyābhisaṅkhāro[^180-8] Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, so paṭippassambhi.

4. Dutiyam pi kho sambahulā abhiññātā[^180-9] abhiññātā[^180-9] Licchavī santhāgāre sannisinnā sannipatitā anekapariyāyena buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ[^180-10] bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti. Dutiyaṃ pi kho Sīhassa senāpatissa etad ahosi ‘nissaṃsayaṃ kho so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho bhavissati, tathā h'; ime sambahulā abhiññātā[^180-11] abhiññātā[^180-11] Licchavī santhāgāre sannisinnā sannipatitā anekapariyāyena buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ[^180-12] bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ[^180-13] bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti; yan nūnāhaṃ taṃ Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkameyyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 181 -->
<a id="page-181"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 181]*

arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhan'; ti. Atha kho Sīho senāpati yena Nigaṇṭho[^181-1] Nāṭaputto ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Nigaṇṭhaṃ[^181-2] Nāṭaputtaṃ etad avoca ‘icchām'; ahaṃ bhante samaṇaṃ Gotamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitun'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pana tvaṃ Sīha kiriyavādo samāno akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ Gotamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? Samaṇo hi Sīha Gotamo akiriyavādo akiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī'; ti. Dutiyam pi kho Sīhassa senāpatissa yo ahosi gamiyābhisaṅkhāro[^181-3] Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, so paṭippassambhi.

5. Tatiyam pi kho sambahulā abhiññātā[^181-4] abhiññātā[^181-4] Licchavī santhāgāre sannisinnā sannipatitā anekapariyāyena buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ[^181-5] bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti. Tatiyam pi kho Sīhassa senāpatissa etad ahosi ‘nissaṃsayaṃ kho so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho bhavissati, tathā h'; ime sambahulā abhiññātā[^181-4] abhiññātā[^181-4] Licchavī santhāgāre sannisinnā sannipatitā anekapariyāyena buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti; kiṃ hi 'me[^181-6] karissanti Nigaṇṭhā[^181-7] apalokitā vā anapalokitā vā; yan nūnāhaṃ anapaloketvā 'va Nigaṇṭhe[^181-8] taṃ Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkameyyaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhan'; ti. Atha kho Sīho senāpati pañcamattehi rathasatehi divādivassa Vesāliyā niyyāsi Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya. Yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi yānena gantvā yanā paccorohitvā pattiko 'va ārāmaṃ pāvisi. Atha kho Sīho senāpati yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Sīho senāpati Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sutaṃ me[^181-9] taṃ[^181-9] bhante: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo akiriyāya


<!-- PTS Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 182]*

dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Ye te bhante evam āhaṃsu: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo akiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti, kacci[^182-1] te bhante Bhagavato vuttavādino na ca[^182-2] Bhagavantaṃ abhūtena abbhācikkhanti dhammassa cānudhammaṃ[^182-3] vyākaronti na ca koci sahadhammiko vādānupāto[^182-4] gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati. Anabbhakkhātukāmā[^182-5] hi mayaṃ bhante Bhagavantan'; ti.

6. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo akiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: kiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo kiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: ucchedavādo samaṇo Gotamo ucchedāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: jegucchī samaṇo Gotamo jegucchitāya[^182-6] dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: venayiko samano Gotamo vinayāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: tapassī samaṇo Gotamo tapassitāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya apagabbho[^182-7] samaṇo Gotamo apagabbhatāya[^182-7] dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Atthi Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: assattho[^182-8] samaṇo Gotamo assāsāya[^182-9] dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti.

7. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo


<!-- PTS Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 183]*

akiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Ahaṃ hi Sīha akiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyaduccaritassa vacīduccaritassa manoduccaritassa, anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ akiriyaṃ vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: akiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo akiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: kiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo kiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Ahaṃ hi Sīha kiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyasucaritassa vacīsucaritassa manosucaritassa, anekavihitānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ kiriyaṃ vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: kiriyavādo samaṇo Gotamo kiriyāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: ucchedavādo samaṇo Gotamo ucchedāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Ahaṃ hi Sīha ucchedaṃ vadāmi rāgassa dosassa mohassa, anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ucchedaṃ vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: ucchedavādo samaṇo Gotamo ucchedāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: jegucchī samaṇo Gotamo jegucchitāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Ahaṃ hi Sīha jigucchāmi[^183-1] kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, jigucchāmi[^183-1] anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: jegucchī samaṇo Gotamo jegucchitāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: venayiko samaṇo Gotamo vinayāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Ahaṃ hi Sīha vinayāya dhammaṃ desemi rāgassa dosassa mohassa,


<!-- PTS Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 184]*

anekavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vinayāya dhammaṃ desemi. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: venayiko samaṇo Gotamo vinayāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: tapassī samaṇo Gotamo tapassitāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Tapanīyāhaṃ Sīha pāpake akusale dhamme vadāmi: kāyaduccaritaṃ vacīduccaritaṃ manoduccaritaṃ. Yassa kho Sīha tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā[^184-1] ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā[^184-2] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tam ahaṃ tapassī ti vadāmi. Tathāgatassa kho Sīha tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: tapassī samaṇo Gotamo tapassitāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: apagabbho[^184-3] samaṇo Gotamo apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Yassa kho Sīha āyatiṃ gabbhaseyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā[^184-4], tam ahaṃ apagabbho ti vadāmi. Tathāgatassa kho Sīha āyatiṃ gabbhaseyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: apagabbho samaṇo Gotamo apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti. Katamo ca Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: assattho[^184-5] samaṇo Gotamo assāsāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti? Ahaṃ hi Sīha


<!-- PTS Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 185]*

assattho[^185-1] paramena assāsena[^185-2] assāsāya[^185-3] dhammaṃ desemi, tena ca sāvake vinemi. Ayaṃ kho Sīha pariyāyo, yena maṃ pariyāyena sammā vadamāno vadeyya: assattho[^185-1] samaṇo Gotamo assāsāya dhammaṃ deseti, tena ca sāvake vinetī ti.

8. Evaṃ vutte Sīho senāpati Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantaṃ bhante abhikkantaṃ[^185-4] bhante . . . pe[^185-5] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhante Bhagavā dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. ‘Anuviccakāraṃ kho Sīha karohi, anuviccakāro[^185-6] tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ[^185-7] sādhu hotī' ti. ‘Iminā p'; ahaṃ[^185-8] bhante Bhagavato bhiyyosomattāya attamano abhiraddho, yaṃ maṃ Bhagavā evam āha: anuviccakāraṃ kho Sīha karohi, anuviccakāro tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hotī ti. Maṃ hi bhante aññatitthiyā sāvakaṃ labhitvā kevalakappaṃ Vesāliṃ[^185-9] paṭākaṃ[^185-10] parihareyyuṃ: Sīho amhākaṃ senāpati sāvakattaṃ upagato ti. Atha ca pana maṃ Bhagavā evam āha: anuviccakāraṃ kho Sīha karohi, anuviccakāro tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hotī ti. Esāhaṃ bhante dutiyam[^185-11] pi Bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ ca bhikkhusaṅghañ ca, upāsakaṃ maṃ bhante Bhagavā dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. ‘Dīgharattaṃ kho te Sīha Nigaṇṭhanaṃ[^185-12] opānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ, yena nesaṃ[^185-13] upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ[^185-14] dātabbaṃ maññeyyāsī'; ti. ‘Iminā p'; ahaṃ[^185-15] bhante Bhagavato bhiyyosomattāya attamano abhiraddho, yaṃ maṃ Bhagavā evam āha: dīgharattaṃ kho te[^185-16] Sīha Nigaṇṭhānaṃ[^185-12] opānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ, yena nesaṃ[^185-13] upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ[^185-14] dātabbaṃ[^185-17] maññeyyāsī ti. Sutam me[^185-18] taṃ[^185-18] bhante: samaṇo Gotamo evam āha


<!-- PTS Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 186]*

<<mayham eva dānaṃ[^186-1] dātabbaṃ, na[^186-2] aññesaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayham eva sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ[^186-3] dātabbaṃ, na[^186-2] aññesaṃ sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ[^186-4] dātabbaṃ; mayham eva dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ, na aññesaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ; mayham eva sāvakānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ, na aññesaṃ sāvakānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalan>> ti. Atha ca pana maṃ[^186-5] Bhagavā Nigaṇṭhesu[^186-6] pi dāne[^186-7] samādapeti, api ca bhante mayam ettha kālaṃ jānissāma. Esāhaṃ bhante tatiyam[^186-8] pi Bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ ca bhikkhusaṅghañ ca, upāsakaṃ maṃ bhante Bhagavā dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti.

9. Atha kho Bhagavā Sīhassa senāpatissa anupubbikathaṃ[^186-9] kathesi, seyyathīdaṃ dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ okāraṃ saṃkilesaṃ nekkhamme[^186-10] ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsesi. Yadā Bhagavā aññāsi[^186-11] Sīhaṃ senāpatiṃ kallacittaṃ muducittaṃ[^186-1] vinivaraṇacittaṃ[^186-12] udaggacittaṃ pasannacittaṃ, atha yā[^186-13] buddhānaṃ sāmukkaṃsikā dhammadesanā, taṃ pakāsesi[^186-14]: dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ nirodhaṃ maggaṃ. Seyyathā pi nāma suddhaṃ[^186-15] vatthaṃ[^186-15] apagatakālakaṃ[^186-16] samma[^186-17]-d-eva rajanaṃ[^186-18] paṭiggaṇheyya[^186-19], evam eva Sīhassa[^186-20] senāpatissa tasmiṃ yeva āsane virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi ‘yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ, sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman'; ti.

10. Atha kho Sīho senāpati diṭṭhadhammo pattadhammo[^186-21] viditadhammo pariyogāḷhadhammo tiṇṇavicikiccho vigatakathaṃkatho vesārajjappatto aparapaccayo Satthu sāsane Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘adhivāsetu me[^186-22] bhante[^186-23] Bhagavā


<!-- PTS Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 187]*

svātanāya[^187-1] bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghenā'; ti. Adhivāsesi Bhagavā tuṇhībhāvena. Atha kho Sīho senāpati Bhagavato adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi. Atha kho Sīho senāpati aññataraṃ purisaṃ āmantesi ‘gaccha tvaṃ[^187-2] ambho purisa pavattamaṃsaṃ jānāhī'; ti. Atha kho Sīho senāpati tassā rattiyā accayena sake nivesane paṇītaṃ khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyaṃ paṭiyādāpetvā[^187-3] Bhagavato kālaṃ ārocāpesi[^187-4] ‘kālo bhante, Sīhassa[^187-5] senāpatissa[^187-5] nivesane[^187-6] niṭṭhitaṃ bhattan'; ti.

11. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Sīhassa[^187-7] senāpatissa nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghena. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā Nigaṇṭhā[^187-8] Vesāliyaṃ rathiyāya[^187-9] rathiyaṃ[^187-10] siṅghāṭakena[^187-11] siṅghāṭakaṃ bāhā paggayha kandanti ‘ajja Sīheno senāpatinā thullaṃ[^187-12] pasuṃ vadhitvā samaṇassa Gotamassa bhattaṃ[^187-13] kataṃ, taṃ[^187-14] samaṇo Gotamo jānaṃ uddissa kataṃ[^187-15] maṃsaṃ[^187-15] paribhuñjati paṭicca kamman'; ti. Atha kho aññataro puriso yena Sīho senāpati ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Sīhassa senāpatissa upakaṇṇake ārocesi ‘yagghe[^187-16] bhante jāneyyāsi ete sambahulā Nigaṇṭhā[^187-8] Vesāliyaṃ rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakena siṅghāṭakaṃ bāhā[^187-17] paggayhakandanti: ajja Sīhena senāpatinā thullaṃ[^187-12] pasuṃ vadhitvā samaṇassa Gotamassa bhattaṃ[^187-13] kataṃ, taṃ[^187-18] samaṇo Gotamo jānaṃ[^187-19] uddissa kataṃ[^187-15] maṃsaṃ[^187-15] pari-


<!-- PTS Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 188]*

bhuñjati paṭicca kamman'; ti[^188-1]. ‘Alaṃ[^188-2] ayyo[^188-3], dīgharattaṃ hi te āyasmanto avaṇṇakāmā buddhassa avaṇṇakāmā dhammassa avaṇṇakāmā saṅghassa, na ca pana te[^188-4] āyasmanto jīranti[^188-5] taṃ[^188-6] Bhagavantaṃ asatā[^188-7] tucchā musā abhūtena abbhācikkhantā[^188-8], na ca mayaṃ jīvitahetu pi sañcicca pāṇaṃ jīvitā voropeyyāmā[^188-9]'; ti.

12. Atha kho Sīho senāpati buddhapamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītena khādaniyena bhojaniyena[^188-10] sahatthā santappesi sampavāresi. Atha kho Sīho senāpati Bhagavantaṃ[^188-11] bhuttāviṃ onītapattapāṇiṃ ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Sīhaṃ senāpatiṃ Bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassetvā samādapetvā samuttejetvā sampahaṃsetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmī ti. XIII.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhaddo[^188-12] assājāniyo rājāraho[^188-13] hoti rājabhoggo[^188-14], rañño aṅgan tveva[^188-15] saṃkhaṃ[^188-16] gacchati. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave rañño bhaddo assājāniyo ubhato sujāto hoti mātito ca pitito ca, yassaṃ disāyaṃ aññe pi bhaddā assājāniyā jāyanti, tassaṃ disāyaṃ jāto hoti; yaṃ kho pan' assa bhojanaṃ denti allaṃ vā sukkaṃ[^188-17] vā, taṃ sakkaccaṃ yeva[^188-10] paribhuñjati avikiranto[^188-18]; jegucchī hoti uccāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā abhinisīdituṃ vā abhinipajjituṃ vā; so rato


<!-- PTS Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 189]*

hoti sukhasaṃvāso[^189-1] na[^189-2] aññe asse ubbejetā[^189-3]; yāni kho pan'; assa honti[^189-4] sāṭheyyāni kūṭeyyāni[^189-5] jimheyyāni vaṅkeyyāni, tāni yathābhūtaṃ[^189-6] sārathissa āvikattā hoti, tesam assa sārathi abhinimmadanāya[^189-7] vāyamati; vāhī kho pana hoti, ‘kāmaṃ[^189-8] maññe[^189-8] assā vahantu vā mā vā, aham ettha[^189-9] vahissāmī'; ti cittaṃ uppādeti; gacchanto kho pana ujumaggen' eva gacchati; thāmavā hoti yāva[^189-10] jīvitamaraṇapariyādānā[^189-11] thāmaṃ[^189-12] upadaṃsetā[^189-13]. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhah'[^189-14] aṅgehi samannāgato rañño bhaddo assājāniyo rājāraho hoti rājabhoggo[^189-15], rañño aṅgan tveva saṃkhaṃ[^189-16] gacchati.

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi[^189-17] samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe[^189-18] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu; yaṃ kho pan'; assa bhojanaṃ denti[^189-19] lūkhaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, taṃ[^189-20] sakkaccaṃ yeva paribhuñjati avihaññamāno; jegucchī hoti, jigucchati[^189-21] kāyaduccaritena vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena, jigucchati[^189-22] pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā; so rato hoti sukhasaṃvāso[^189-23] na aññe bhikkhū ubbejetā[^189-24]; yāni kho pan'; assa honti[^189-25] sāṭheyyāni


<!-- PTS Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 190]*

kūṭeyyāni[^190-1] jimheyyāni vaṅkeyyāni, tāni yathābhūtaṃ āvikattā[^190-2] hoti Satthari vā viññūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu, tesam assa Satthā vā viññū vā sabrahmacārī abhinimmadanāya vāyamati; sikkhitā kho pana hoti[^190-3], ‘kāmaṃ[^190-4] maññe[^190-4] bhikkhū sikkhantu[^190-5] vā mā vā, aham ettha sikkhissāmī'; ti cittaṃ uppādeti; gacchanto kho pana ujumaggen'; eva gacchati, tatrāyaṃ ujumaggo, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^190-6] . . . sammāsamādhi; āraddhaviriyo viharati ‘kāmaṃ taco ca nahāru ca aṭṭhi[^190-7] ca[^190-7] avasissatu, sarīre upasussatu maṃsalohitaṃ, yan taṃ purisathāmena purisaviriyena purisaparakkamena pattabbaṃ[^190-8], na[^190-9] taṃ[^190-9] apāpuṇitvā[^190-10] viriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatī'; ti. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti . . . pe . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. XIV.

1. Aṭṭha ca[^190-11] bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅke[^190-12] desessāmi[^190-13] aṭṭha ca[^190-7] assadose aṭṭha ca[^190-11] purisakhaḷuṅke aṭṭha ca[^190-7] purisadose, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ[^190-14] manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katame ca bhikkhave aṭṭha assakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca assadosā? Idha bhikkhave ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī[^190-15] ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā pacchato paṭisakkati[^190-16],


<!-- PTS Page 191 -->
<a id="page-191"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 191]*

piṭṭhito rathaṃ paṭivatteti[^191-1]. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh' ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo assadoso.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā pacchā laṅghi[^191-2] pati kubbaraṃ hanti[^191-3] tidaṇḍaṃ[^191-4] bhañjati[^191-5]. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo assadoso.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā rathīsāya[^191-6] satthiṃ[^191-7] ussajjitvā rathīsaṃ yeva ajjhomaddati. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo assadoso.

5. Puna[^191-8] ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā ummaggaṃ gaṇhāti[^191-9], ubbaṭumaṃ rathaṃ karoti. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho assadoso.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā laṅgheti purimaṃ kāyaṃ, paggaṇhāti purime pāde. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo assadoso.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā anādiyitvā[^191-10] sārathiṃ[^191-11] anādiyitvā[^191-11] patodaṃ[^191-12] dantehi[^191-13] mukhādhānaṃ[^191-14]


<!-- PTS Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 192]*

viddhaṃsitvā[^192-1] yenakāmaṃ[^192-2] pakkamati. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭho assadoso.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā neva abhikkamati no paṭikkamati[^192-3], tatth'; eva {khīlaṭṭhāyi ṭhito} hoti. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo assadoso.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave idh'; ekacco assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā purime ca[^192-4] pāde saṃharitvā[^192-5] pacchime[^192-6] ca pāde saṃharitvā[^192-7] tatth' eva cattāro pāde abhinisīdati. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh' ekacco assakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamo assadoso. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha assakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca assadosā.

10. Katame ca bhikkhave attha purisakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca purisadosā? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhū[^192-8] bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno ‘na sarāmi[^192-9] na sarāmī'; ti asatiyā[^192-10] 'va nibbeṭheti[^192-11]. Seyyathā pi so[^192-12] bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā pacchato paṭisakkati[^192-13], piṭṭhito rathaṃ paṭivatteti[^192-14], tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo purisadoso.

11. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno


<!-- PTS Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 193]*

codakaṃ yeva paṭippharati[^193-1] ‘kiṃ nu kho tuyhaṃ[^193-2] bālassa avyattassa bhaṇitena, tvaṃ pi nāma bhaṇitabbaṃ maññasī' ti? Seyyathā pi so bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā pacchā laṅghi[^193-3] pati[^193-4] kubbaraṃ hanti[^193-5] tidaṇḍaṃ bhañjati[^193-6], tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh' ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave dutiyo purisadoso.

12. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno codakass'; eva paccāropeti ‘tvaṃ pi kho 'si itthannāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpanno, tvaṃ tāva paṭhamaṃ paṭikarohī'; ti. Seyyathā pi so bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā rathīsāya[^193-7] satthiṃ[^193-8] ussajjitvā rathīsaṃ[^193-9] yeva ajjhomaddati, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo purisadoso.

13. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno aññen'; aññaṃ[^193-10] paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmeti, kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti. Seyyathā pi so bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā ummaggaṃ gaṇhāti[^193-11], ubbaṭumaṃ rathaṃ karoti, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho purisadoso.

14. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno saṅghamajjhe bāhāvikkhepaṃ[^193-12] bhaṇati[^193-13]. Seyyathā pi so


<!-- PTS Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 194]*

bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā laṅgheti[^194-1] purimaṃ kāyaṃ, paggaṇhāti purime pāde, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave pañcamo purisadoso.

15. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno anādiyitvā saṅghaṃ anādiyitvā codakaṃ sāpattiko va[^194-2] yenakāmaṃ pakkamati. Seyyathā pi so bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko[^194-3] pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā anādiyitvā sārathiṃ[^194-4] anādiyitvā[^194-4] patodaṃ[^194-5] dantehi[^194-6] mukhādhānaṃ[^194-7] viddhaṃsitvā[^194-8] yenakāmaṃ pakkamati, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh' ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭho purisadoso.

16. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno[^194-9] ‘nevāhaṃ[^194-10] āpanno 'mhi[^194-11], na panāhaṃ āpanno 'mhī'; ti. So tuṇhībhāvena saṅghaṃ viheseti[^194-12]. Seyyathā pi so bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā neva[^194-13] abhikkamati no paṭikkamati[^194-14], tatth'; eva khīlaṭṭhāyiṭhito hoti, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti[^194-15]. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo purisadoso.

17. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno evam


<!-- PTS Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 195]*

āha: ‘kiṃ nu[^195-1] kho tumhe āyasmanto atibāḷhaṃ[^195-2] mayi[^195-3] vyāvaṭā[^195-4] yāva[^195-1] idānāhaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī[^195-5]'; ti? So sikkhaṃ[^195-6] paccakkhāya[^195-6] hīnāyāvattitvā[^195-5] evam āha: ‘idāni kho tumhe āyasmanto attamanā hothā' ti. Seyyathā pi so bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅko pehī ti vutto viddho samāno codito sārathinā purime ca[^195-7] pāde saṃharitvā pacchime ca[^195-8] pāde saṃharitvā[^195-9] tatth'; eva cattāro pāde abhinisīdati, tathūpamāhaṃ bhikkhave imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi. Evarūpo pi bhikkhave idh'; ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamo purisadoso. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha[^195-10] purisakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca[^195-11] purisadosā ti. XV.

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave malāni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

2. Asajjhāyamalā bhikkhave mantā, anuṭṭhānamalā bhikkhave gharā, malaṃ bhikkhave vaṇṇassa kosajjaṃ, pamādo bhikkhave rakkhato malaṃ, malaṃ bhikkhave itthiyā duccaritaṃ, maccheraṃ bhikkhave dadato malaṃ, malā bhikkhave pāpakā akusalā dhammā asmiṃ loke paramhi ca, tato ca[^195-12] bhikkhave malā[^195-13] malataraṃ, avijjā[^195-14] paramaṃ[^195-15] malaṃ[^195-16]. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha malānī ti. Asajjhāyamalā mantā anuṭṭhānamalā gharā malaṃ vaṇṇassa kosajjaṃ pamādo rakkhato malaṃ malitthiyā duccaritaṃ maccheraṃ dadato malaṃ malā ve pāpakā dhammā asmiṃ loke paramhi ca tato malā[^195-17] malataraṃ avijjā paramaṃ malan ti.

##### *[PTS Page 196]*

XVI.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu dūteyyaṃ gantum arahati. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sotā ca hoti sāvetā ca uggahetā ca dhāretā ca viññātā[^196-1] ca[^196-1] viññāpetā[^196-1] ca[^196-1] kusalo ca sahitāsahitassa no ca kalahakārako. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu dūteyyaṃ gantum arahati.

3. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato Sāriputto dūteyyaṃ gantum arahati. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave Sāriputto sotā ca hoti[^196-2] sāvetā ca uggahetā ca dhāretā ca viññātā ca viññāpetā ca kusalo ca sahitāsahitassa no ca kalahakārako. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato Sāriputto dūteyyaṃ gantum arahatī ti. Yo ve[^196-3] na byādhati[^196-4] patvā[^196-5] parisaṃ uggahavādinaṃ[^196-6] na ca hāpeti vacanaṃ na ca[^196-7] cchādeti[^196-8] sāsanaṃ asandiṭṭhañ[^196-9] ca[^196-9] bhaṇati pucchito na[^196-10] ca kuppati[^196-11]: sa[^196-12] ve[^196-12] tādisako bhikkhu dūteyyaṃ gantum arahatī ti. XVII.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave ākārehi itthi purisaṃ bandhati. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Rūpena[^196-13] bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati, hasitena bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati, bhaṇitena bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati, gītena[^196-14] bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ

##### *[PTS Page 197]*

bandhati, roṇṇena bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati, ākappena bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati, vanabhaṅgena bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati[^197-1], phassena bhikkhave itthi purisaṃ bandhati. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhah'[^197-2] ākārehi itthi purisaṃ bandhati. Tehi bhikkhave sattā subaddhā[^197-3] yeva pāsena[^197-4] baddhā ti[^197-5]. XVIII.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave ākārehi puriso itthiṃ bandhati. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Rūpena[^197-6] bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati, hasitena bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati, bhaṇitena bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati, gītena[^197-7] bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati, roṇṇena bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati, ākappena bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati, vanabhaṅgena bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ bandhati[^197-1], phassena bhikkhave puriso itthiṃ badhati. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhah'[^197-2] ākārehi puriso itthiṃ bandhati. Tehi bhikkhave sattā subaddhā[^197-3] yeva pāsena[^197-4] baddhā ti[^197-8]. XIX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Verañjāyaṃ viharati Naḷerupucimaṇḍamūle[^197-9]. Atha kho Pahārādo[^197-10] asurindo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ kho Pahārādaṃ[^197-10] asurindaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca ‘api[^197-11] pana


<!-- PTS Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 198]*

Pahārāda[^198-1] asurā mahāsamudde abhiramantī'; ti? Abhiramanti[^198-2] bhante asurā mahāsamudde ti. ‘Kati pana Pahārāda[^198-1] mahāsamudde acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā[^198-3] disvā[^198-3] asurā mahāsamudde abhiramantī'; ti? Aṭṭha[^198-4] bhante mahāsamudde acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti. Katame aṭṭha[^198-5]?

2. Mahāsamuddo bhante anupubbaninno anupubbapoṇo anupubbapabbhāro na āyataken'; eva papāto. Yam pi bhante mahāsamuddo anupubbaninno anupubbapoṇo anupubbapabbhāro na āyataken'; eva papāto: ayaṃ bhante mahāsamudde paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhante mahāsamuddo ṭhitadhammo velaṃ nātivattati. Yam pi bhante mahāsamuddo ṭhitadhammo velaṃ nātivattati: ayaṃ[^198-6] bhante mahāsamudde dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhante mahāsamuddo na matena kuṇapena saṃvasati[^198-7]; yaṃ hoti mahāsamudde mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippaṃ eva[^198-8] tīraṃ vāheti thalaṃ[^198-9] ussādeti[^198-10]. Yam[^198-11] pi bhante mahāsamuddo na matena kuṇapena saṃvasati[^198-7]; yaṃ hoti mahāsamudde mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippam eva[^198-8] tīraṃ vāheti thalaṃ[^198-9] ussādeti: ayaṃ[^198-6] bhante mahāsamudde tatiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhante yā kāci mahānadiyo, seyyathīdaṃ Gaṅgā Yamunā Aciravatī Sarabhū Mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ patvā[^198-12] jahanti purimāni nāmagottāni, mahā-


<!-- PTS Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 199]*

samuddo tveva[^199-1] saṃkhaṃ[^199-2] gacchanti. Yam[^199-3] pi bhante yā kāci mahānadiyo, seyyathīdaṃ Gaṅgā Yamunā Aciravatī Sarabhū Mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ patvā[^199-4] jahanti purimāni nāmagottāni, mahāsamuddo tveva saṃkhaṃ[^199-2] gacchanti: ayaṃ bhante mahāsamudde catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhante yā kāci[^199-5] loke savantiyo mahāsamuddaṃ appenti, yā kāci[^199-6] antalikkhā[^199-7] dhārā papatanti, na tena[^199-8] mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ[^199-9] vā pūrattaṃ[^199-10] vā[^199-11] paññāyati. Yam pi bhante yā kāci[^199-12] loke savantiyo mahāsamuddaṃ appenti, yā kāci[^199-13] antalikkhā[^199-7] dhārā papatanti, na tena[^199-8] mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ[^199-9] vā pūrattaṃ[^199-10] vā paññāyati: ayaṃ[^199-14] bhante mahāsamudde pañcamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhante mahāsamuddo ekaraso loṇaraso. Yam pi bhante mahāsamuddo ekaraso loṇaraso: ayaṃ[^199-15] bhante mahāsamudde chaṭṭho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhante mahāsamuddo bahuratano[^199-16] anekaratano, tatr'; imāni ratanāni, seyyathīdaṃ muttā maṇi veḷuriyo[^199-17] saṅkho silā[^199-18] pavāḷaṃ[^199-19] rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lohitaṅko[^199-20] masāragallaṃ[^199-21]. Yam pi bhante mahāsamuddo bahuratano[^199-16] anekaratano[^199-22], tatr'; imāni ratanāni, seyyathīdaṃ muttā maṇi veḷuriyo[^199-17] saṅkho silā[^199-18] pavāḷaṃ[^199-19] rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lohitaṅko[^199-20] masāragallaṃ: ayaṃ[^199-14] bhante

Puna ca paraṃ bhante mahāsamuddo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime[^200-1] bhūtā[^200-1] timitimiṅgalā[^200-2] timiramiṅgalā[^200-3] asurā nāgā gandhabbā; santi[^200-4] mahāsamudde yojanasatikā pi attabhāvā dviyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā tiyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā catuyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā pañcayojanasatikā pi attabhāvā. Yam pi bhante mahāsamuddo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ[^200-5] āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā[^200-6] timitimiṅgalā[^200-7] timiramiṅgalā[^200-8] asurā nāgā gandhabbā; santi[^200-9] mahāsamudde yojanasatikā pi attabhāvā dviyojanasatikā pi[^200-10] attabhāvā tiyojanasatikā[^200-11] pi attabhāvā[^200-12] catuyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā[^200-12] pañcayojanasatikā pi attabhāvā: ayaṃ[^200-13] bhante mahāsamudde aṭṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti. Ime kho bhante mahāsamudde aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā disvā asurā mahāsamudde abhiramanti[^200-14]; api pana bhante bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramantī[^200-15] ti[^200-15]? ‘Abhiramanti Pahārāda[^200-16] bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye'; ti. Kati pana bhante imasmiṃ dhammavinaye acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramantī ti[^200-10]?

10. ‘Aṭṭha Pahārāda[^200-16] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti. Katame aṭṭha?

11. Seyyathā pi Pahārādā[^200-17] mahāsamuddo anupubbaninno anupubbapoṇo anupubbapabbhāro na āyataken'[^200-18] eva pa-

##### *[PTS Page 201]*

pāto, evam eva kho Pahārāda[^201-1] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā na āyataken'; eva aññāpaṭivedho[^201-2]. Yam pi Pahārāda[^201-3] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā na āyataken'; eva aññāpaṭivedho[^201-2]: ayaṃ[^201-4] Pahārāda[^201-3] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti.

12. Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^201-3] mahāsamuddo ṭhitadhammo[^201-5] velaṃ nātivattati, evam eva kho Pahārāda[^201-3] yaṃ mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ paññattaṃ, taṃ[^201-6] mama[^201-6] sāvakā[^201-7] jīvitahetu[^201-8] pi nātikkamanti. Yam[^201-9] pi Pahārāda mayā[^201-10] sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ paññattaṃ, taṃ mama sāvakā[^201-11] jīvitahetu pi nātikkamanti: ayaṃ[^201-12] Pahārāda imasmiṃ dhammavinaye dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti.

13. Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^201-13] mahāsamuddo na matena kuṇapena saṃvasati; yaṃ hoti mahāsamudde mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippam eva[^201-14] tīraṃ vāheti[^201-15] thalaṃ ussādeti: evam eva kho Pahārāda[^201-13] yo so puggalo dussīlo pāpadhammo asucisaṃkassarasamācāro paṭicchannakammanto assamaṇo samaṇapaṭiñño abrahmacārī brahmacārīpaṭiñño antopūti avassuto kasambujāto[^201-16], na tena saṅgho saṃvasati, khippam eva naṃ sannipatitvā ukkhipati; kiñcāpi so hoti majjhe bhikkhusaṅghassa nisinno[^201-17], atha kho so ārakā 'va saṅghamhā saṅgho ca tena. Yam pi Pahārāda[^201-18] yo so puggalo dussīlo pāpadhammo asucisaṃkassarasamācāro paṭicchannakammanto assamaṇo samaṇapaṭiñño abrahmacārī brahmacārīpaṭiñño antopūti avassuto kasambujāto[^201-16],

Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^202-5] yā kāci mahānadiyo, seyyathīdaṃ Gaṅgā Yamunā Aciravatī Sarabhū Mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ patvā[^202-6] jahanti purimāni nāmagottāni, mahāsamuddo tveva saṃkhaṃ[^202-7] gacchanti: evam eva kho Pahārāda[^202-5] cattāro 'me vaṇṇā: khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā suddā, te Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā jahanti purimāni nāmagottāni, samaṇā[^202-8] Sakyaputtiyā[^202-9] tveva saṃkhaṃ[^202-10] gacchanti. Yam pi Pahārāda[^202-5] cattāro 'me vaṇṇā: khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā suddā, te Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā[^202-11] jahanti purimāni nāmagottāni, samaṇā[^202-12] Sakyaputtiyā[^202-9] tveva[^202-13] saṃkhaṃ[^202-10] gacchanti: ayam[^202-14] Pahārāda[^202-5] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti.

15. Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^202-5] yā kāci[^202-15] loke savantiyo mahāsamuddaṃ appeni, yā kāci[^202-16] antalikkhā[^202-17] dhārā papatanti, na tena mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ[^202-18] vā pūrattaṃ[^202-19] vā paññāyati: evam eva kho Pahārāda[^202-20] bahū ce pi bhikkhū anupādisesāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyanti, na tena nibbānadhātuyā ūnattaṃ[^202-18] vā pūrattaṃ[^202-19] vā paññāyati. Yam pi Pahārāda[^202-5] bahū ce pi bhikkhū anupādisesāya


<!-- PTS Page 203 -->

##### *[PTS Page 203]*

nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyanti, na tena nibbānadhātuyā ūnattaṃ[^203-1] vā pūrattaṃ[^203-2] vā paññāyati: ayaṃ[^203-3] Pahārāda imasmiṃ dhammavinaye pañcamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti.

16. Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^203-4] mahāsamuddo ekaraso loṇaraso, evam eva kho Pahārāda[^203-4] ayaṃ dhammavinayo ekaraso vimuttiraso. Yam pi Pahārāda[^203-4] ayaṃ dhammavinayo ekaraso vimuttiraso: ayaṃ Pahārāda[^203-4] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye chaṭṭho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti.

17. Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^203-4] mahāsamuddo bahuratano[^203-5] anekaratano, tatr'; imāni ratanāni, seyyathīdaṃ muttā maṇi veḷuriyo[^203-6] saṅkho silā[^203-7] pavāḷaṃ[^203-8] rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lohitaṅko[^203-9] masāragallaṃ: evam eva kho Pahārāda[^203-4] ayaṃ dhammavinayo bahuratano[^203-10] anekaratano, tatr'; imāni ratanāni, seyyathīdaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā, cattāro sammappadhānā[^203-11], cattāro iddhipādā, pañc'; indriyāni, pañca balāni, satta bojjhaṅgā, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo. Yam pi Pahārāda[^203-4] ayaṃ dhammavinayo bahuratano[^203-12] anekaratano, tatr'; imāni ratanāni, seyyathīdaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā, cattāro sammappadhānā[^203-11], cattāro iddhipādā, pañc'; indriyāni, pañca balāni, satta bojjhaṅgā, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo: ayaṃ[^203-3] Pahārāda[^203-4] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye sattamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti.

18. Seyyathā pi Pahārāda[^203-4] mahāsamuddo mahataṃ[^203-13] bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā[^203-14]: timitimiṅgalā[^203-15] timi-


<!-- PTS Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 204]*

ramiṅgalā[^204-1] asurā nāgā gandhabbā; santi[^204-2] mahāsamudde yojanasatikā pi attabhāvā dviyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā tiyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā catuyojanasatikā pi attabhāvā pañcayojanasatikā pi attabhāvā: evam eva kho Pahārāda[^204-3] ayaṃ dhammavinayo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā: sotāpanno sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sakadāgāmī[^204-4] sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, anāgāmī anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, arahā arahattāya paṭipanno. Yam pi Pahārāda[^204-3] ayaṃ dhammavinayo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā: sotāpanno sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sakadāgāmī sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, anāgāmī anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, arahā arahattāya paṭipanno: ayaṃ[^204-5] Pahārāda[^204-3] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye aṭṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti. Ime kho Pahārāda[^204-3] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramantī'; ti. XX.

1. Evam[^204-6] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbārāme[^204-7] Migāramātu pāsāde. Tena kho pana samayena Bhagavā tadahuposathe bhikkhusaṅghaparivuto nisinno hoti. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando abhikkantāya rattiyā nikkhante paṭhame yāme uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yena Bhagavā ten'; añjaliṃ'; panāmetvā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantā bhante ratti, nikkhanto paṭhamo yāmo, ciranisinno bhikkhusaṅgho, uddisatu[^204-8] bhante Bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimokkhan'; ti. Evaṃ vutte Bhagavā tuṇhī ahosi.

2. Dutiyam pi kho āyasmā Ānando abhikkantāya rattiyā nikkhante majjhime yāme uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yena Bhagavā ten'; añjaliṃ paṇāmetvā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantā bhante ratti, nikkhanto majjhimo yāmo, ciranisinno bhikkhusaṅgho, uddisatu[^205-1] bhante Bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimokkhan'; ti. Dutiyam pi kho Bhagavā tuṇhī ahosi.

3. Tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Ānando abhikkantāya rattiyā nikkhante pacchime yāme uddhaste[^205-2] aruṇe nandimukhiyā[^205-3] rattiyā uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yena Bhagavā ten'; añjaliṃ paṇāmetvā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantā bhante ratti, nikkhanto pacchimo yāmo, uddhastaṃ[^205-4] aruṇaṃ[^205-5] nandimukhī[^205-3] ratti, ciranisinno bhikkhusaṅgho, uddissatu[^205-1] bhante Bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimokkhan'; ti. ‘Aparisuddhā Ānanda parisā'; ti.

4. Atha kho āyasmato Mahāmoggallānassa etad ahosi ‘kiṃ[^205-6] nu kho Bhagavā puggalaṃ sandhāya evam āha: aparisuddhā Ānanda parisā'; ti? Atha kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno sabbāvantaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ cetasā ceto paricca manasākāsi. Addasā kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno taṃ puggalaṃ dussīlaṃ pāpadhammaṃ asucisaṃkassarasamācāraṃ paṭicchannakammantaṃ assamaṇaṃ samaṇapaṭiññaṃ abrahmacāriṃ brahmacārīpaṭiññaṃ antopūtiṃ avassutaṃ kasambujātaṃ[^205-7] majjhe bhikkhusaṅghassa[^205-8] nisinnaṃ; disvā[^205-9] uṭṭhāyāsanā yena so puggalo ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ puggalaṃ etad avoca ‘uṭṭheh'; āvuso, diṭṭho 'si Bhagavatā, natthi te[^205-10] bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāso'; ti. Evaṃ vutte so puggalo tuṇhī ahosi. Dutiyam pi kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno taṃ puggalaṃ etad avoca ‘uṭṭheh' āvuso, diṭṭho 'si Bhagavatā, natthi te[^205-10] bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāso'; ti. Dutiyam pi kho so puggalo tuṇhī ahosi. Tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno taṃ puggalaṃ

etad avoca ‘uṭṭheh'; āvuso, diṭṭho 'si Bhagavatā, natthi te[^206-1] bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāso'; ti. Tatiyam pi kho so puggalo tuṇhī ahosi.

5. Atha[^206-2] kho[^206-2] āyasmā Mahāmoggallāno taṃ puggalaṃ bāhāyaṃ[^206-3] gahetvā bahidvārakoṭṭhakā[^206-4] nikkhāmetvā sūcighaṭikaṃ datvā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘nikkhāmito so[^206-5] bhante puggalo mayā, parisuddhā parisā, uddisatu[^206-6] bhante Bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimokkhan'; ti. ‘Acchariyaṃ Moggallāna abbhutaṃ Moggallāna, yāva[^206-7] bāhāgahaṇā pi[^206-5] nāma[^206-5] so moghapuriso āgamissatī'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--

6. Tumhe 'va dāni[^206-8] bhikkhave uposathaṃ kareyyātha, pātimokkhaṃ uddiseyyātha[^206-9]. Na dānāhaṃ bhikkhave ajja-t-agge[^206-10] pātimokkhaṃ uddisissāmi[^206-11]. Aṭṭhānam etaṃ bhikkhave anavakāso, yaṃ Tathāgato aparisuddhāya parisāya pātimokkhaṃ uddiseyya.

Mahāsamuddo bhikkhave anupubbaninno anupubbapoṇo anupubbapabbhāro, na āyataken'; eva papāto[^206-12]. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave mahāsamuddo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā[^206-19] timitimiṅgalā[^206-20]
