##### *[PTS Page 208]*

satikā pi attabhāvā: evam eva kho bhikkhave ayaṃ[^208-1] dhammavinayo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā[^208-2]: sotāpanno sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno . . . pe[^208-3] . . . arahā arahattāya paṭipanno. Yam[^208-4] pi bhikkhave ayaṃ dhammavinayo mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāso, tatr'; ime bhūtā[^208-2]: sotāpanno sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno . . . pe[^208-5] . . . arahā arahattāya paṭipanno: ayaṃ bhikkhave imasmiṃ dhammavinaye aṭṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramanti. Ime kho bhikkhave imasmiṃ[^208-6] dhammavinaye[^208-6] aṭṭha[^208-7] acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, ye disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinaye abhiramantī ti. Mahāvaggo[^208-8] dutiyo[^208-7]. Tatr'[^208-9] uddānaṃ: Verañja-Sīho ājaññaṃ[^208-10] khaḷuṅkena[^208-11] malāni ca Dūteyyaṃ[^208-12] dve ca bandhanti[^208-13] Pahārāda[^208-14] uposatho ti. XXI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo[^208-15] ti. Bhadante ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave acchariyehi abbhutehi[^208-16] dhammehi samannāgataṃ Uggaṃ gahapatiṃ Vesālikaṃ dhārethā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 209 -->
<a id="page-209"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 209]*

Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvā[^209-1] Sugato uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi.

3. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Uggassa gahapatino Vesālikassa nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Atha kho Uggo gahapati Vesāliko yena so bhikkhu ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Uggaṃ gahapatiṃ Vesālikaṃ so bhikkhu etad avoca:--

4. Aṭṭhahi kho tvaṃ[^209-2] gahapati acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato. Katame te gahapati aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yehi tvaṃ samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato ti? ‘Na kho ahaṃ[^209-3] bhante jānāmi, katamehi ahaṃ[^209-4] aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato, api ca bhante ye me aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvijjanti, te[^209-5] suṇāhi[^209-6] sādhukaṃ manasikarohi[^209-7], bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ gahapatī'; ti kho so bhikkhu Uggassa gahapatino Vesālikassa paccassosi[^209-8]. Uggo gahapati Vesāliko[^209-9] etad avoca:--

5. Yadāhaṃ bhante Bhagavantaṃ paṭhamaṃ dūrato 'va addasaṃ, saha dassanen'; eva me bhante Bhagavato[^209-10] cittaṃ pasīdi. Ayaṃ kho[^209-11] me bhante paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati. So[^209-12] kho ahaṃ bhante pasannacitto Bhagavantaṃ payirupāsiṃ[^209-13]. Tassa me Bhagavā anupubbikathaṃ kathesi, seyyathīdaṃ dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ okāraṃ[^209-14] saṃkilesaṃ nekkhamme[^209-15] ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsesi.

6. Yadā maṃ Bhagavā aññāsi kallacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittam pasannacittaṃ, atha yā


<!-- PTS Page 210 -->
<a id="page-210"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 210]*

buddhānaṃ sāmukkaṃsikā dhammadesanā, taṃ pakāsesi: dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ nirodhaṃ maggaṃ. Seyyathā pi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakālakaṃ samma-d-eva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇheyya, evam eva kho me[^210-1] tasmiṃ yeva āsane virajaṃ vītamalaṃ[^210-2] dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi ‘yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ, sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman' ti. So kho ahaṃ bhante diṭṭhadhammo pattadhammo viditadhammo pariyogāḷhadhammo tiṇṇavicikiccho vigatakathaṃkatho vesārajjappatto aparappaccayo Satthu sāsane tatth'; eva buddhañ ca dhammañ ca saṅghañ ca saraṇaṃ agamāsiṃ[^210-3] brahmacariyapañcamāni ca sikkhāpadāni samādiyiṃ[^210-4]. Ayaṃ kho me bhante dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

7. Tassa mayhaṃ bhante catasso komāriyo pajāpatiyo ahesuṃ. Atha khvāhaṃ[^210-5] bhante yena tā pajāpatiyo ten' upasaṅkamiṃ, upasaṅkamitvā tā pajāpatiyo etad avocaṃ[^210-6] ‘mayā kho bhaginiyo brahmacariyapañcamāni sikkhāpadāni samādinnāni; yā icchati, idh'; eva[^210-7] bhoge ca[^210-8] bhuñjatu puññāni ca karotu sakāni vā ñātikulāni[^210-9] gacchatu, hoti vā pana purisādhippāyo, kassa[^210-10] vo dammī'; ti[^210-11]? Evaṃ vutte sā bhante jeṭṭhā pajāpati maṃ etad avoca ‘itthannāmassa maṃ[^210-1] ayya[^210-12] purisassa dehī'; ti. Atha khvāhaṃ[^210-13] bhante taṃ purisaṃ pakkosāpetvā vāmena hatthena pajāpatiṃ gahetvā dakkhiṇena hatthena bhiṅgāraṃ gahetvā tassa purisassa oṇojesiṃ[^210-14]. Komāriṃ[^210-15] kho panāhaṃ bhante dāraṃ pariccajanto nābhijānāmi cittassa aññathattaṃ[^210-16]. Ayaṃ kho me bhante tatiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.


<!-- PTS Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 211]*

8. Saṃvijjanti[^211-1] kho pana me bhante kule bhogā, te ca kho appaṭivibhattā sīlavantehi kalyāṇadhammehi. Ayaṃ kho me bhante catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

9. Yaṃ kho panāhaṃ bhante bhikkhuṃ payirupāsāmi, sakkaccaṃ yeva payirupāsāmi no asakkaccaṃ. Ayaṃ kho me bhante pañcamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

10. So ce[^211-2] me bhante[^211-3] āyasmā dhammaṃ deseti[^211-4], sakkaccaṃ yeva suṇāmi[^211-5] no asakkaccaṃ; no ce[^211-2] me[^211-6] āyasmā dhammaṃ deseti[^211-7], aham assa dhammaṃ desemi. Ayaṃ kho me bhante chaṭṭho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

11. Anacchariyaṃ[^211-8] kho pana maṃ[^211-9] bhante devatā upasaṅkamitvā ārocenti ‘svākkhāto[^211-10] gahapati Bhagavatā dhammo'; ti. Evaṃ vutte ahaṃ bhante tā[^211-11] devatā evaṃ vadāmi ‘vadeyyātha vā[^211-12] evaṃ[^211-11] kho tumhe devatā[^211-13] no vā[^211-14] vadeyyātha, atha kho svākkhāto[^211-10] Bhagavatā dhammo' ti. Na kho panāhaṃ bhante abhijānāmi tatonidānaṃ[^211-15] cittassa unnatiṃ[^211-16] ‘maṃ vā devatā upasaṅkamanti, ahaṃ vā devatāhi saddhiṃ[^211-11] sallapāmī'; ti. Ayaṃ kho me bhante sattamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

12. Yān'; imāni[^211-17] bhante Bhagavatā desitāni pañc'; orambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni, nāhaṃ tesaṃ[^211-18] kiñci attani[^211-19] appahīnaṃ samanupassāmi[^211-20]. Ayaṃ kho me bhante aṭṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.


<!-- PTS Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 212]*

Ime kho bhante aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvijjanti. Na ca kho ahaṃ jānāmi, katamehi pāhaṃ[^212-1] aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato ti.

13. Atha kho so bhikkhu Uggassa gahapatino Vesālikassa nivesane piṇḍapātaṃ gahetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi. Atha kho so bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so bhikkhu, yāvatako ahosi Uggena gahapatinā Vesālikena saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo, taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesi.

14. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhu, yathā taṃ Uggo gahapati Vesāliko sammā vyākaramāno vyākareyya, imeh'; eva kho bhikkhu aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Uggo gahapati[^212-2] Vesāliko mayā vyākato; imehi ca pana bhikkhu aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Uggaṃ gahapatiṃ Vesālikaṃ dhārethā ti. XXII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vajjīsu viharati Hatthigāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi . . . pe[^212-3] . . . [^212-2]. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Uggaṃ gahapatiṃ Hatthigāmakaṃ dhārethā ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvā[^212-4] Sugato uṭṭhāyāsanā[^212-5] vihāraṃ pāvisi.

3. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Uggassa gahapatino Hatthigāmakassa nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Atha kho Uggo gahapati Hatthigāmako yena so bhikkhu ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Uggaṃ gahapatiṃ Hatthigāmakaṃ so bhikkhu etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 213]*

4. Aṭṭhahi kho tvaṃ gahapati acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato. Katame te[^213-1] gahapati aṭṭha[^213-1] acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yehi tvaṃ samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato ti? ‘Na kho ahaṃ[^213-2] bhante jānāmi, katamehi ahaṃ[^213-3] aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato, api ca bhante ye me aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvijjanti, te[^213-4] suṇāhi[^213-4] sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ gahapatī'; ti kho so bhikkhu Uggassa gahapatino Hatthigāmakassa paccassosi. Uggo gahapati Hatthigāmako etad avoca:--

5. Yadāhaṃ bhante Nāgavane paricaranto[^213-5] Bhagavantaṃ paṭhamaṃ dūrato 'va addasaṃ, saha[^213-6] dassanen'; eva me bhante Bhagavato cittaṃ pasīdi surāmado ca pahīyi. Ayaṃ kho me bhante paṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati. So kho[^213-1] ahaṃ bhante pasannacitto Bhagavantaṃ payirupāsiṃ[^213-7]. Tassa me Bhagavā anupubbikathaṃ kathesi, seyyathīdaṃ dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ okāraṃ[^213-8] saṃkilesaṃ nekkhamme[^213-9] ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsesi.

6. Yadā maṃ Bhagavā aññāsi kallacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ[^213-10] udaggacittaṃ pasannacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmukkaṃsikā dhammadesanā, taṃ pakāsesi: dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ nirodhaṃ maggaṃ. Seyyathā pi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakālakaṃ samma-d-eva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇheyya, evam eva kho[^213-11] me[^213-12] tasmiṃ yeva āsane virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi ‘yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ, sabban taṃ nirodhamman'; ti. So kho ahaṃ bhante diṭṭhadhammo pattadhammo viditadhammo pariyogāḷhadhammo tiṇṇavicikiccho vigatakathaṃkatho vesārajjappatto aparappaccayo Satthu sāsane tatth'


<!-- PTS Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 214]*

eva buddhañ ca dhammañ ca saṅghañ ca saraṇaṃ agamāsiṃ[^214-1] brahmacariyapañcamāni ca[^214-2] sikkhāpadāni samādiyiṃ[^214-3]. Ayaṃ kho me bhante dutiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

7. Tassa mayhaṃ bhante catasso komāriyo pajāpatiyo ahesuṃ. Atha khvāhaṃ[^214-4] bhante yena tā pajāpatiyo ten' upasaṅkamiṃ, upasaṅkamitvā tā[^214-5] pajāpatiyo etad avocaṃ[^214-6] ‘mayā kho bhaginiyo brahmacariyapañcamāni[^214-7] sikkhāpadāni samādinnāni; yā icchati, idh'; eva[^214-8] bhoge ca[^214-9] bhuñjatu puññāni ca karotu sakāni vā ñātikulāni gacchatu, hoti[^214-10] vā pana purisādhippāyo, kassa vo[^214-11] dammī'; ti? Evaṃ vutte sā[^214-12] bhante jeṭṭhā pajāpati maṃ etad avoca ‘itthannāmassa maṃ ayya[^214-13] purisassa dehī'; ti. Atha khvāhaṃ[^214-14] bhante taṃ purisaṃ[^214-15] pakkosāpetvā vāmena[^214-16] hatthena[^214-16] pajāpatiṃ gahetvā dakkhiṇena hatthena bhiṅgāraṃ[^214-17] gahetvā tassa purisassa oṇojesiṃ[^214-18]. Komāriṃ[^214-19] kho panāhaṃ bhante dāraṃ pariccajanto nābhijānāmi cittassa aññathattaṃ. Ayaṃ kho me bhante tatiyo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

8. Saṃvijjanti[^214-20] kho pana me bhante kule bhogā, te ca[^214-21] kho appaṭivibhattā sīlavantehi kalyāṇadhammehi. Ayaṃ kho me[^214-5] bhante catuttho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.


<!-- PTS Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 215]*

9. Yaṃ kho panāhaṃ bhante bhikkhuṃ payirupāsāmi, sakkaccaṃ yeva payirupāsāmi no asakkaccaṃ. So ce[^215-1] me bhante āyasmā dhammaṃ deseti[^215-2], sakkaccaṃ yeva suṇāmi[^215-3] no asakkaccaṃ; no ce[^215-4] me āyasmā[^215-5] dhammaṃ deseti[^215-2], aham assa dhammaṃ desemi. Ayaṃ kho me bhante pañcamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

10. Anacchariyaṃ[^215-6] kho pana[^215-7] me bhante saṅghe nimantite devatā upasaṅkamitvā ārocenti ‘asuko gahapati bhikkhu ubhato bhāgavimutto, asuko paññāvimutto, asuko kāyasakkhī, asuko diṭṭhippatto, asuko saddhāvimutto, asuko[^215-8] dhammānusārī[^215-8], asuko saddhānusārī, asuko sīlavā[^215-7] kalyāṇadhammo, asuko dussīlo pāpadhammo'; ti[^215-9]. Saṅghaṃ[^215-10] kho panāhaṃ bhante parivisanto[^215-11] nābhijānāmi evaṃ[^215-12] cittaṃ uppādento ‘imassa vā thokaṃ demi imassa vā bahukan'; ti. Atha khvāhaṃ bhante samacitto 'va demi. Ayaṃ kho me bhante chaṭṭho acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.

11. Anacchariyaṃ[^215-13] kho pana maṃ[^215-14] bhante devatā upasaṅkamitvā ārocenti ‘svākkhāto bhante[^215-15] gahapati Bhagavatā dhammo'; ti. Evaṃ vutte ahaṃ bhante tā devatā evaṃ vadāmi[^215-16] ‘vadeyyātha vā evaṃ[^215-17] kho tumhe devatā[^215-18] no vā[^215-19] vadeyyātha, atha kho svākkhāto Bhagavatā dhammo'; ti. Na kho panāhaṃ bhante abhijānāmi tatonidānaṃ cittassa unnatiṃ[^215-20] ‘maṃ vā[^215-21] devatā upasaṅkamanti, ahaṃ vā devatāhi saddhiṃ sallapāmī'; ti. Ayaṃ kho me bhante sattamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati.


<!-- PTS Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 216]*

12. Sace kho panāhaṃ bhante Bhagavato[^216-1] paṭhamataraṃ kālaṃ kareyyaṃ, anacchariyaṃ[^216-2] kho pan'; etaṃ, yaṃ maṃ Bhagavā evaṃ vyākareyya ‘natthi taṃ saṃyojanaṃ[^216-3], yena[^216-3] saṃyojanena saṃyutto Uggo gahapati Hatthigāmako puna imaṃ lokaṃ āgaccheyyā'; ti. Ayaṃ kho me bhante aṭṭhamo acchariyo abbhuto dhammo saṃvijjati. Ime kho bhante aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvijjanti. Na ca kho ahaṃ jānāmi, katamehi pāhaṃ[^216-4] aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato ti.

13. Atha kho so bhikkhu Uggassa gahapatino Hatthigāmakassa nivesane piṇḍapātaṃ gahetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi. Atha kho so bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so bhikkhu, yāvatako ahosi Uggena gahapatinā Hatthigāmakena saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo, taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesi.

14. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhu, yathā taṃ Uggo gahapati Hatthigāmako sammā vyākaramāno vyākareyya, imeh'; eva[^216-5] kho bhikkhu aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Uggo gahapati Hatthigāmako mayā vyākato; imehi ca pana bhikkhu aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Uggaṃ gahapatiṃ Hatthigāmakaṃ dhārethā ti. XXIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Āḷaviyaṃ[^216-6] viharati Aggāḷave[^216-7] cetiye[^216-7]. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi . . . pe[^216-8] . . .

2. Sattahi bhikkhave acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ dhāretha. Katamehi[^217-1] sattahi[^217-1]?

3. Saddho bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, sīlavā bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, hirimā[^217-2] bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, ottāpī[^217-3] bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, bahussuto bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, cāgavā bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, paññavā bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako. Imehi kho bhikkhave sattahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ dhārethā ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvā[^217-4] Sugato uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi.

4. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Hatthakassa Āḷavakassa nivesanaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Atha kho Hatthako Āḷavako yena so bhikkhu ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ so bhikkhu etad avoca ‘sattahi kho tvaṃ āvuso acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato. Katamehi[^217-1] sattahi[^217-1]? Saddho bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako[^217-5], sīlavā . . .[^217-6] hirimā . . . ottāpī . . .[^217-7] bahussuto . . . cāgavā . . . paññavā bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako. Imehi kho tvaṃ āvuso sattahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato'; ti. ‘Kacci 'ttha[^217-8] bhante na koci gihī ahosi odātavasano'; ti? ‘Na kho 'ttha[^217-9] āvuso koci gihī ahosi odātavasano'; ti[^217-10]. ‘Sādhu bhante, yad ettha na koci gihī ahosi odātavasano'; ti.

5. Atha kho so bhikkhu Hatthakassa Āḷavakassa nivesane piṇḍapātaṃ gahetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi. Atha kho so bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Bhagavā

ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Atha kho bhante Hatthako Āḷavako yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ etad avocaṃ: sattahi kho tvaṃ āvuso acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato. Katamehi[^218-1] sattahi[^218-1]? Saddho bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako[^218-2], sīlavā[^218-3] . . .[^218-4] hirimā . . . ottāpī . . . bahussuto . . . cāgavā . . . paññavā bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako. Imehi kho tvaṃ āvuso sattahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgato Bhagavatā vyākato ti. Evaṃ vutte bhante Hatthako Āḷavako maṃ etad avoca: kacci 'ttha[^218-5] bhante na koci gihī ahosi odātavasano ti[^218-6]? Na kho 'ttha[^218-7] āvuso koci gihī ahosi odātavasano ti. Sādhu[^218-8] bhante, yad ettha na koci gihī ahosi odātavasano'; ti.

6. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhu[^218-9], appiccho so bhikkhu[^218-10] kulaputto sante yeva attani kusale dhamme na icchati parehi ñāyamāne[^218-11]. Tena hi tvaṃ bhikkhu iminā aṭṭhamena[^218-12] acchariyena abbhutena dhammena samannāgataṃ Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ dhārehi, yad idam[^218-13] appicchatāyā ti. XXIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Āḷaviyaṃ viharati Aggāḷave cetiye. Atha kho Hatthako Āḷavako pañcamattehi upā-


<!-- PTS Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 219]*

sakasatehi parivuto[^219-1] yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Mahatī kho tyāyaṃ[^219-2] Hatthaka parisā, kathaṃ pana tvaṃ Hatthaka imaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgaṇhāsī ti? Yān'; imāni bhante Bhagavatā desitāni cattāri saṃgahavatthūni, tehāhaṃ[^219-3] imaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgaṇhāmi. Ahaṃ bhante yaṃ jānāmi ‘ayaṃ dānena saṃgahetabbo'; ti, taṃ dānena saṃgaṇhāmi; yaṃ jānāmi ‘ayaṃ peyyavajjena[^219-4] saṃgahetabbo'; ti, taṃ peyyavajjena[^219-5] saṃgaṇhāmi; yaṃ jānāmi ‘ayaṃ atthacariyāya saṃgahetabbo'; ti, taṃ atthacariyāya saṃgaṇhāmi; yaṃ jānāmi ‘ayaṃ samānattatāya saṃgahetabbo[^219-6]'; ti[^219-6], taṃ[^219-6] samānattatāya[^219-6] saṃgaṇhāmi. Saṃvijjante[^219-7] kho pana me bhante kule bhogā, daliddassa[^219-8] kho no tathā sotabbaṃ[^219-9] maññantī[^219-10] ti.

3. Sādhu sādhu Hatthaka, yoni[^219-11] kho tyāhaṃ[^219-12] Hatthaka mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgahetuṃ. Ye[^219-13] hi keci[^219-14] Hatthaka atītamaddhānaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgahesuṃ, sabbe te imeh'; eva catūhi saṃgahavatthūhi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgahesuṃ; ye pi hi keci[^219-15] Hatthaka anāgatamaddhānaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgaṇhissanti, sabbe te imeh'; eva catūhi saṃgahavatthūhi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgaṇhissanti; ye pi hi keci[^219-15] Hatthaka etarahi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgaṇhanti, sabbe te imeh'; eva catūhi saṃgahavatthūhi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṃgaṇhantī ti.

4. Atha kho Hatthako Āḷavako Bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassito samādapito samuttejito sampahaṃsito uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā


<!-- PTS Page 220 -->

##### *[PTS Page 220]*

pakkāmi. Atha kho Bhagavā acirapakkante Hatthake Āḷavake bhikkhū āmantesi:--

5. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ dhāretha. Katamehi[^220-1] aṭṭhahi[^220-1]?

6. Saddho bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, sīlavā bhikkhave[^220-2] Hatthako[^220-3] Āḷavako[^220-3], hirimā bhikkhave[^220-2] Hatthako[^220-3] Āḷavako[^220-3], ottāpī bhikkhave[^220-2] Hatthako[^220-3] Āḷavako[^220-3], bahussuto bhikkhave[^220-2] Hatthako[^220-3] Āḷavako[^220-3], cāgavā bhikkhave[^220-2] Hatthako[^220-3] Āḷavako[^220-3], paññavā bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako, appiccho bhikkhave Hatthako Āḷavako. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi acchariyehi abbhutehi dhammehi samannāgataṃ Hatthakaṃ Āḷavakaṃ dhārethā ti. XXV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme. Atha kho Mahānāmo Sakko yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo Sakko Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kittāvatā nu kho bhante upāsako hotī'; ti? Yato[^220-4] kho Mahānāma buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti: ettāvatā[^220-5] kho Mahānāma upāsako hotī ti.

2. ‘Kittāvatā[^220-5] pana bhante upāsako sīlavā hotī'; ti? Yato[^220-5] kho Mahānāma upāsako pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti: ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako sīlavā hotī ti.

3. ‘Kittāvatā[^220-5] pana bhante upāsako attahitāya paṭipanno hoti no parahitāyā'; ti?


<!-- PTS Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 221]*

Yato[^221-1] kho Mahānāmo upāsako attanā[^221-2] saddhāsampanno hoti no paraṃ saddhāsampadāya samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] sīlasampanno hoti no paraṃ sīlasampadāya samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] cāgasampanno hoti no paraṃ cāgasampadāya samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] bhikkhūnaṃ dassanakāmo[^221-3] hoti no paraṃ bhikkhūnaṃ[^221-4] dassane samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] saddhammaṃ sotukāmo hoti no paraṃ saddhammasavane[^221-5] samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko[^221-6] hoti no paraṃ dhammadhāraṇāya[^221-7] samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] dhatānaṃ[^221-8] dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī[^221-9] hoti no paraṃ atthūpaparikkhāya samādapeti, attanā[^221-2] attham aññāya dhammam[^221-10] aññāya[^221-10] dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti no paraṃ[^221-11] dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā samādapeti: ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako attahitāya paṭipanno hoti no parahitāyā ti.

4. ‘Kittāvatā pana bhante upāsako attahitāya ca paṭipanno hoti parahitāya cā'; ti? Yato[^221-1] kho Mahānāma upāsako attanā ca saddhāsampanno hoti parañ ca saddhāsampadāya[^221-12] samādapeti, attanā ca sīlasampanno hoti parañ ca sīlasampadāya samādapeti, attanā ca cāgasampanno hoti parañ ca cāgasampadāya samādapeti, attanā ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassanakāmo hoti[^221-13] parañ ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassane samādapeti, attanā ca saddhammaṃ sotukāmo hoti parañ ca saddhammasavane samādapeti, attanā ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko[^221-6] hoti parañ ca dhammadhāraṇāya[^221-14] samādapeti, attanā ca dhatānaṃ[^221-15] dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī[^221-16] hoti parañ ca atthūpaparikkhāya samādapeti, attanā ca attham aññāya


<!-- PTS Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 222]*

dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti parañ ca[^222-1] dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā samādapeti: ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako attahitāya ca[^222-2] paṭipanno hoti parahitāya cā ti. XXVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Jīvakambavane. Atha kho Jīvako Komārabhacco yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Jīvako Komārabhacco Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kittāvatā nu[^222-3] kho[^222-4] bhante upāsako hotī'; ti? Yato[^222-5] kho Jīvaka buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti: ettāvatā kho Jīvaka upāsako hotī ti.

2. ‘Kittāvatā[^222-6] pana bhante upāsako sīlavā hotī ti'; ? Yato[^222-5] kho Jīvaka upāsako pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^222-7] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti: ettāvatā kho Jīvaka upāsako sīlavā hotī ti.

3. ‘Kittāvatā[^222-8] pana bhante upāsako attahitāya paṭipanno hoti no parahitāyā'; ti? Yato[^222-5] kho Jīvaka upāsako attanā[^222-9] saddhāsampanno hoti no paraṃ saddhāsampadāya samādapeti . . . pe[^222-7] . . . attanā[^222-10] attham aññāya dhammam[^222-11] aññāya[^222-11] dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti no paraṃ[^222-12] dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā samādapeti: ettāvatā kho Jīvaka upāsako attahitāya paṭipanno hoti no parahitāyā ti.

4. ‘Kittāvatā pana bhante upāsako attahitāya ca[^222-13] paṭipanno hoti parahitāya cā'; ti?


<!-- PTS Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 223]*

Yato[^223-1] kho Jīvaka upāsako attanā ca saddhāsampanno hoti parañ ca saddhāsampadāya samādapeti, attanā ca sīlasampanno hoti parañ ca sīlasampadāya samādapeti, attanā ca cāgasampanno hoti parañ ca cāgasampadāya samādapeti, attanā ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassanakāmo hoti parañ ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassane samādapeti, attanā ca saddhammaṃ sotukāmo hoti parañ ca saddhammasavane samādapeti, attanā ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti parañ ca dhammadhāraṇāya samādapeti, attanā ca dhatānaṃ[^223-2] dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī[^223-3] hoti parañ ca[^223-4] atthūpaparikkhāya samādapeti, attanā ca attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti parañ ca[^223-5] dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā samādapeti: ettāvatā kho Jīvaka upāsako attahitāya ca[^223-6] paṭipanno hoti parahitāya cā ti. XXVII.

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave balāni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

2. Roṇṇabalā[^223-7] bhikkhave dārakā, kodhabalā[^223-8] mātugāmā[^223-9], āvudhabalā corā, issariyabalā rājāno, ujjhattibalā[^223-10] bālā[^223-10], nijjhattibalā[^223-11] paṇḍitā, paṭisaṅkhānabalā bahussutā, khantibalā samaṇabrāhmaṇā. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha balānī ti. XXVIII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 224]*

Sāriputtaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca ‘kati[^224-1] nu kho Sāriputta khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balāni, yehi balehi[^224-2] samannāgato khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā'; ti?

2. Aṭṭha bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balāni, yehi balehi samannāgato khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me[^224-3] āsavā ti. Katamāni aṭṭha?

3. Idha bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aniccato sabbe saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aniccato sabbe saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā ti.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhā honti, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā ti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno vivekaninnaṃ cittaṃ hoti vivekapoṇaṃ vivekapabbhāraṃ vivekaṭṭhaṃ nekkhammābhirataṃ[^224-4] vyantibhūtaṃ sabbaso āsavaṭṭhāniyehi dhammehi. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno vivekaninnaṃ cittaṃ hoti vivekapoṇaṃ vivekapabbhāraṃ vivekaṭṭhaṃ nekkhammābhirataṃ[^224-5] vyantibhūtaṃ sabbaso āsavaṭṭhāniyehi dhammehi, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā ti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā. Yam pi bhante

##### *[PTS Page 225]*

khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno[^225-1] cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā ti.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā . . . pe[^225-2] . . . pañc'; indriyāni[^225-3] bhāvitāni[^225-4] honti[^225-4] subhāvitāni[^225-4] . . . satta bojjhaṅgā bhāvitā honti subhāvitā . . .[^225-5] ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo bhāvito hoti subhāvito. Yam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo bhāvito hoti subhāvito, idam pi bhante khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balaṃ hoti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā ti. Imāni kho bhante aṭṭha khīṇāsavassa bhikkhuno balāni, yehi balehi[^225-6] samannāgato khīṇāsavo bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti: khīṇā me āsavā ti. XXIX.

1. Khaṇakicco[^225-7] loko, khaṇakicco loko ti bhikkhave assutavā puthujjano bhāsati, no[^225-8] ca kho so jānāti khaṇaṃ vā akkhaṇaṃ vā. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave akkhaṇā asamayā brahmacariyavāsāya. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Idha bhikkhave Tathāgato ca loke uppanno hoti arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā[^225-9], dhammo ca desiyati opasamiko[^225-10] parinibbāyiko[^225-11] sambodhagāmī sugatappavedito, ayañ ca[^225-4] puggalo nirayaṃ upapanno[^225-12] hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo akkhaṇo asamayo brahmacariyavāsāya.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato ca loke[^226-1] uppanno hoti . . . pe[^226-2] . . . Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā[^226-3], dhammo ca desiyati opasamiko[^226-4] parinibbāyiko[^226-5] sambodhagāmī[^226-6] sugatappavedito, ayañ ca puggalo tiracchānayoniṃ[^226-7] upapanno[^226-8] hoti . . . pe[^226-9] . . . ayañ ca puggalo pittivisayaṃ upapanno[^226-10] hoti . . . pe[^226-9] . . . ayañ ca puggalo aññataraṃ[^226-11] dīghāyukaṃ devanikāyaṃ upapanno[^226-10] hoti . . . pe[^226-9] . . . ayañ ca puggalo paccantimesu janapadesu paccājāto[^226-12] hoti[^226-13] aviññātāresu milakkhesu[^226-14], yattha natthi gati bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ . . . pe[^226-15] . . . ayañ ca puggalo majjhimesu janapadesu paccājāto[^226-16] hoti, so ca hoti micchādiṭṭhiko viparītadassano ‘natthi dinnaṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ[^226-17] phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko, natthi paro[^226-18] loko[^226-18], natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā opapātikā, natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā[^226-19], ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti . . . pe[^226-15] . . . ayañ ca puggalo majjhimesu janapadesu paccājāto[^226-16] hoti, so[^226-17] ca[^226-17] hoti[^226-17] duppañño jaḷo eḷamūgo na paṭibalo[^226-20] subhāsitadubbhāsitassa attham aññātuṃ. Ayaṃ bhikkhave sattamo[^226-21] akkhaṇo[^226-21] asamayo brahmacariyavāsāya.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave Tathāgato ca[^226-22] loke anuppanno hoti arahaṃ sammāsambuddho . . . pe[^226-23] . . . Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā, dhammo ca na

##### *[PTS Page 227]*

desiyati opasamiko[^227-1] parinibbāyiko[^227-2] sambodhagāmī sugatappavedito, ayañ ca puggalo majjhimesu janapadesu paccājāto[^227-3] hoti, so ca hoti paññavā ajaḷo[^227-4] aneḷamūgo paṭibalo subhāsitadubbhāsitassa attham aññātuṃ. Ayaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamo akkhaṇo asamayo brahmacariyavāsāya. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha akkhaṇā asamayā brahmacariyavāsāya.

5. Eko 'va[^227-5] bhikkhave khaṇo ca samayo ca brahmacariyavāsāya. Katamo eko?

6. Idha bhikkhave Tathāgato ca[^227-6] loke uppanno hoti arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā, dhammo ca desiyati opasamiko[^227-1] parinibbāyiko[^227-2] sambodhagāmī sugatappavedito, ayañ ca puggalo majjhimesu janapadesu paccājāto[^227-7] hoti, so ca hoti paññavā ajaḷo[^227-8] aneḷamūgo paṭibalo subhāsitadubbhāsitassa attham aññātum. Ayaṃ bhikkhave eko 'va[^227-9] khaṇo ca samayo ca brahmacariyavāsāyā ti. Manussalābhaṃ[^227-10] laddhāna saddhamme suppavedite[^227-11] ye khaṇaṃ[^227-12] nādhigacchanti atināmenti[^227-13] te khaṇaṃ. Bahūhi akkhaṇā vuttā puggalass'; antarāyikā[^227-14]. Kadāci[^227-15] karahaci loke uppajjanti Tathāgatā, tayidaṃ[^227-16] sammukhībhūtaṃ[^227-17] yaṃ lokasmiṃ sudullabhaṃ manussapaṭilābho ca saddhammassa ca desanā, alaṃ vāyamituṃ tattha atthakāmena jantunā.


<!-- PTS Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 228]*

Kathaṃ vijaññā[^228-1] saddhammaṃ[^228-2] khaṇo[^228-3] ve[^228-4] mā upaccagā[^228-5]? Khaṇātītā hi socanti nirayamhi samappitā. Idha-m-eva[^228-6] naṃ[^228-7] virādheti[^228-8] saddhammassa niyāmataṃ[^228-9], vāṇijo[^228-10] va atītattho cirattaṃ[^228-11] anutapessati[^228-12]. Avijjānivuto[^228-13] poso[^228-14] saddhammaṃ aparādhiko[^228-15] jātimaraṇasaṃsāraṃ ciraṃ paccanubhossati[^228-16]. Ye ca laddhā manussattaṃ saddhamme suppavedite akaṃsu Satthu vacanaṃ karissanti karonti vā, khaṇaṃ paccavidhuṃ[^228-17] loke brahmacariyaṃ anuttaraṃ. Ye maggaṃ paṭipajjiṃsu Tathāgatappaveditaṃ[^228-18] ye saṃvarā cakkhumatā desitādiccabandhunā, tesu[^228-19] gutto sadā sato viharetha[^228-20] anavassuto. Sabbe[^228-21] anusaye chetvā Māradheyyasarānuge[^228-22] te[^228-23] ve[^228-23] pāragatā[^228-23] loke ye pattā āsavakkhayan ti. XXX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Bhaggesu viharati Suṃsumāragire[^228-24] Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Anuruddho Cetīsu viharati Pācīnavaṃsadāye[^228-25]. Atha kho āyasmato Anuruddhassa rahogatassa patisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi ‘appicchassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo mahicchassa; santuṭṭhassāyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 229]*

dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asantuṭṭhassa; pavivittassāyaṃ[^229-1] dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo saṅgaṇikārāmassa; āraddhaviriyassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo kusītassa; upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo muṭṭhassatissa; samāhitassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asamāhitassa; paññavato ayaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo duppaññassā'; ti.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmato Anuruddhassa cetasā ceto parivitakkam aññāya seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^229-2] vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^229-3], evam eva Bhaggesu Suṃsumāragire Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye antarahito Cetīsu Pācīnavaṃsadāye āyasmato Anuruddhassa sammukhe[^229-4] pāturahosi: Nisīdi Bhagavā paññatte āsane. Āyasmā pi kho Anuruddho Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Anuruddhaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

3. Sādhu sādhu Anuruddha, sādhu kho tvaṃ Anuruddha satta mahāpurisavitakke[^229-5] vitakkesi: appicchassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo mahicchassa; santuṭṭhassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asantuṭṭhassa; pavivittassāyaṃ[^229-6] dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo saṅgaṇikārāmassa; āraddhaviriyassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo kusītassa; upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo muṭṭhassatissa; samāhitassāyaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asamāhitassa; paññavato ayaṃ dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo duppaññassā ti. Tena hi tvaṃ Anuruddha imam pi[^229-7] aṭṭhamaṃ mahāpurisavitakkaṃ vitakkehi[^229-8]: nippapañcārāmassāyaṃ dhammo nippapañcaratino, nāyaṃ dhammo papañcārāmassa papañcaratino ti.

4. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi[^229-9], tato tvaṃ Anuruddha, yāva-d-eva ākaṅkhissasi, vivicc'; eva[^229-10] kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dham-


<!-- PTS Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 230]*

mehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ[^230-1] jhānaṃ[^230-1] upasampajja viharissasi.

5. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi[^230-2], tato[^230-3] tvaṃ Anuruddha, yāva-d-eva ākaṅkhissasi, vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā[^230-4] ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharissasi.

6. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi[^230-2], tato tvaṃ Anuruddha, yāva-d-eva ākaṅkhissasi, pītiyā ca virāgā[^230-5] upekhako[^230-6] ca viharissasi sato ca[^230-7] sampajāno, sukhañ ca kāyena paṭisaṃvedissasi[^230-8], yan taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upekhako satimā sukhavihārī'; ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharissasi.

7. Yato[^230-9] kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi, tato tvaṃ Anuruddha, yāva-d-eva ākaṅkhissasi, sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubb'; eva somanassadomanassānaṃ[^230-10] aṭṭhaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharissasi.

8. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime ca[^230-11] aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi imesañ ca[^230-12] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^230-13] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tato tuyhaṃ Anuruddha seyyathā pi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nānārattānaṃ dussānaṃ dussakaraṇḍako[^230-14] pūro[^230-15], evam eva te paṃsukūlacīvaraṃ[^230-16] khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharato ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.


<!-- PTS Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 231]*

9. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi imesañ ca[^231-1] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi[^231-2] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tato tuyhaṃ Anuruddha seyyathā pi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā sālīnaṃ[^231-3] odano[^231-4] vicitakāḷako anekasūpo anekavyañjano, evam eva te piṇḍiyālopabhojanaṃ[^231-5] khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharato ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.

10. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime ca[^231-1] aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi imesañ ca[^231-1] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tato tuyhaṃ Anuruddha seyyathā pi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā kūṭāgāraṃ ullittāvalittaṃ[^231-6] nivātaṃ phusitaggaḷaṃ[^231-7] pihitavātapānaṃ, evam eva te[^231-8] rukkhamūlasenāsanaṃ[^231-9] khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharato ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.

11. Yato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime ca[^231-10] aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi imesañ ca[^231-1] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tato tuyhaṃ Anuruddha seyyathā pi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā pallaṅko gonakatthato[^231-11] paṭikatthato[^231-12] paṭalikatthato[^231-13] kadalimigapavarapaccattharaṇo[^231-14] sa-uttaracchado ubhato lohitakūpadhāno, evam eva te tiṇasantharakasayanāsanaṃ[^231-15] khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharato ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya nibbānassa.


<!-- PTS Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 232]*

12. ḥYato kho tvaṃ Anuruddha ime ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakke vitakkessasi imesañ ca[^232-1] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tato tuyhaṃ Anuruddha seyyathā pi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nānābhesajjāni, seyyathīdaṃ[^232-1] sappi navanītaṃ telaṃ madhu phānitaṃ[^232-2], evam eva te pūtimuttabhesajjaṃ[^232-3] khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharato ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya okkamanāya[^232-4] nibbānassa.

13. Tena hi tvaṃ Anuruddha āyatikam pi vassāvāsaṃ idh'; eva Cetīsu Pācīnavaṃsadāye vihareyyāsī ti. Evaṃ bhante ti kho āyasmā Anuruddho Bhagavato paccassosi. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Anuruddhaṃ iminā ovādena ovaditvā seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^232-5] vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^232-6], evam eva Cetīsu Pācīnavaṃsadāye antarahito Bhaggesu Suṃsumāragire Bhesakaḷāvane Migadāye pāturahosi. Nisīdi Bhagavā paññatte āsane. Nisajja kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--

14. Aṭṭha[^232-7] vo[^232-7] bhikkhave mahāpurisavitakke desessāmi[^232-8], taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ[^232-9] manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. Evam bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:-- Katame ca bhikkhave aṭṭha mahāpurisavitakkā?

15. Appicchassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo mahicchassa; santuṭṭhassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asantuṭṭhassa; pavivittassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo saṅgaṇikārāmassa; āraddhaviriyassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo kusītassa; upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo muṭṭhassatissa; samāhitassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asamāhitassa; paññavato ayaṃ[^232-10] bhikkhave dhammo,


<!-- PTS Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 233]*

nāyaṃ dhammo duppaññassa; nippapañcārāmassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo nippapañcaratino, nāyaṃ dhammo papañcārāmassa papañcaratino.

16. Appicchassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo mahicchassā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

17. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu appiccho samāno ‘appiccho ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti na icchati, santuṭṭho samāno ‘santuṭṭho ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti na icchati, pavivitto samāno ‘pavivitto ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti na icchati, āraddhaviriyo samāno ‘āraddhaviriyo ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti na icchati, upaṭṭhitasati samāno ‘upaṭṭhitasatī ti maṃ jāneyyun ti na icchati, samāhito samāno ‘samāhito ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti na icchati, paññavā samāno ‘paññavā ti maṃ jāneyyun'; ti na icchati, nippapañcārāmo samāno ‘nippapañcārāmo ti maṃ jāneyyun' ti na icchati. Appicchassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo mahicchassā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

18. Santuṭṭhassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asantuṭṭhassā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

19. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu santuṭṭho hoti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena. Santuṭṭhassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asantuṭṭhassā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

20. Pavivittassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo saṅgaṇikārāmassā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

21. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno pavivittassa viharato bhavanti upasaṅkamitāro[^233-1] bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo upāsakā upāsikāyo rājāno rājamahāmattā titthiyā titthiyasāvakā. Tatra bhikkhu vivekaninnena[^233-2] cittena vivekapoṇena vivekapabbhārena vivekaṭṭhena nekkhammābhiratena[^233-3] aññadatthu uyyojanikapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ[^233-4] yeva kathaṃ kattā hoti. Pavi-


<!-- PTS Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 234]*

vittassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo saṅgaṇikārāmassā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

22. Āraddhaviriyassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo kusītassā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

23. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Āraddhaviriyassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo kusītassā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

24. Upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo muṭṭhassatissā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c' etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

25. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu satimā hoti paramena satinepakkena samannāgato cirakatam pi cirabhāsitam pi saritā anussaritā[^234-1]. Upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo muṭṭhassatissā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

26. Samāhitassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asamāhitassā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

27. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^234-2] . . . catutthaṃ[^234-3] jhānaṃ[^234-3] upasampajja viharati. Samāhitassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo asamāhitassā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

28. Paññavato ayaṃ[^234-4] bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo duppaññassā ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paticca vuttaṃ?

29. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu paññavā hoti udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Paññavato ayaṃ[^234-4] bhikkhave dhammo, nāyaṃ dhammo duppaññassā ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 235]*

30. Nippapañcārāmassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo nippapañcaratino, nāyaṃ dhammo papañcārāmassa papañcaratino ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

31. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno[^235-1] papañcanirodhe cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati. Nippapañcārāmassāyaṃ bhikkhave dhammo nippapañcaratino, nāyaṃ dhammo papañcārāmassa papañcaratino ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttan ti.

32. Atha kho āyasmā Anuruddho āyatikam pi vassāvāsaṃ tatth'; eva Cetīsu Pācīnavaṃsadāye vihāsi. Atha kho āyasmā Anuruddho eko vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto na cirass'; eva yass'; atthāya kulaputtā sammad-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā'; ti abbhaññāsi, aññataro ca panāyasmā Anuruddho arahataṃ ahosi. Atha kho āyasmā Anuruddho arahattappatto tāyaṃ velāyaṃ imā gāthāyo abhāsi: Mama saṅkappam aññāya Satthā loke anuttaro manomayena kāyena iddhiyā {upasaṅkami,} yathā[^235-2] me ahu[^235-3] saṅkappo tato uttari desayi nippapañcarato buddho nippapañcaṃ adesayi. Tassāhaṃ[^235-4] dhammam aññāya vihāsiṃ[^235-5] sāsane rato tisso vijjā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa[^235-6] sāsanan ti. Gahapativaggo tatiyo. Tatr'[^235-7] uddānaṃ[^235-8]: Dve Uggā dve ca Hatthakā Mahānāmena Jīvako Dve balā akkhaṇā vuttā Anuruddhena te dasā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 236]*

XXXI[^236-1].

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave dānāni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

2. Āsajja dānaṃ deti, bhayā dānaṃ deti, ‘adāsi me'; ti dānaṃ deti, ‘dassati me'; ti dānaṃ deti, ‘sāhu[^236-2] dānan'; ti dānaṃ deti, ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, ime na pacanti, na[^236-3] arahāmi[^236-3] pacanto apacantānaṃ[^236-4] adātun[^236-5]'; ti dānaṃ deti, ‘imaṃ me[^236-6] dānaṃ dadato kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggacchatī'; ti dānaṃ deti, cittālaṅkāracittaparikkhāratthaṃ dānaṃ deti. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha dānānī ti[^236-7]. XXXII. Saddhā hiriyaṃ kusalañ ca dānaṃ Dhammā ete sappurisānuyātā[^236-8] Etaṃ hi maggaṃ diviyaṃ[^236-9] vadanti Etena hi gacchati devalokan ti. XXXIII.

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave dānavatthūni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

2. Chandā dānaṃ deti, dosā dānaṃ deti, mohā dānaṃ deti, bhayā dānaṃ deti, ‘dinnapubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahehi[^236-10], na arahāmi[^236-11] porāṇaṃ[^236-12] kulavaṃsaṃ hāpetun' ti dānaṃ deti, ‘imāhaṃ dānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjissāmī[^236-13]'; ti dānaṃ deti, ‘imaṃ me dānaṃ dadato cittaṃ pasīdati,


<!-- PTS Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 237]*

attamanatā[^237-1] somanassaṃ upajāyatī'; ti dānaṃ deti, cittālaṅkāracittaparikkhāratthaṃ dānaṃ deti. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha dānavatthūnī ti. XXXIV.

1. Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgate bhikkhave khette bījaṃ vuttaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hoti na mahassādaṃ na phātiseyyaṃ[^237-2]. Kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgate[^237-3]?

2. Idha bhikkhave khettaṃ unnāmininnāmi ca hoti, pāsāṇasakkharillañ[^237-4] ca hoti, ūsarañ[^237-5] ca hoti, na ca gambhīrasitaṃ hoti, na āyasampannaṃ hoti, na apāyasampannaṃ hoti, na mātikāsampannaṃ[^237-6] hoti, na mariyādasampannaṃ hoti. Evaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgate bhikkhave khette bījaṃ vuttaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hoti na mahassādaṃ na phātiseyyaṃ[^237-7]. Evam eva kho bhikkhave aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatesu samaṇabrāhmaṇesu dānaṃ[^237-8] dinnaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hoti na mahānisaṃsaṃ na mahājutikaṃ na mahāvipphāraṃ. Kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatesu[^237-9]?

3. Idha bhikkhave samaṇabrāhmaṇā micchādiṭṭhikā honti micchāsaṅkappā micchāvācā micchākammantā micchā-ājīvā micchāvāyāmā micchāsatino micchāsamādhino. Evaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatesu bhikkhave samaṇabrāhmaṇesu dānaṃ[^237-8] dinnaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hoti na mahānisaṃsaṃ na mahājutikaṃ na mahāvipphāraṃ.

4. Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgate[^237-10] bhikkhave khette bījaṃ vuttaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahassādaṃ[^237-11] phātiseyyaṃ[^237-7]. Kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgate[^237-12]?

5. Idha bhikkhave khettaṃ anunnāmininnāmi ca hoti, apāsāṇasakkharillañ[^237-4] ca hoti, anūsarañ[^237-13] ca hoti, gambhī-


<!-- PTS Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 238]*

rasitaṃ hoti, āyasampannaṃ hoti, apāyasampannaṃ hoti, mātikāsampannaṃ hoti, mariyādasampannaṃ hoti. Evaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgate bhikkhave khette bījaṃ vuttaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahassādaṃ phātiseyyaṃ[^238-1]. Evaṃ eva kho bhikkhave aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatesu samaṇabrāhmaṇesu dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsaṃ mahājutikaṃ mahāvipphāraṃ. Kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatesu[^238-2]?

6. Idha bhikkhave samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammādiṭṭhikā honti sammāsaṅkappā sammāvācā sammākammantā sammā-ājīvā sammāvāyāmā sammāsatino sammāsamādhino. Evaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatesu bhikkhave samaṇabrāhmaṇesu dānaṃ dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ hoti mahānisaṃsaṃ mahājutikaṃ mahāvipphāran ti. Yathā pi khette sampanne pavuttā bījasampadā deve sampādayantamhi[^238-3] hoti dhaññassa[^238-4] sampadā. Anīti sampadā hoti virūḷhi bhavati sampadā vepullaṃ[^238-5] sampadā hoti phalaṃ[^238-6] ve hoti sampadā. Evaṃ sampannasīlesu dinnā[^238-7] bhojanasampadā sampadānaṃ upaneti sampannaṃ hi 'ssa taṃ kataṃ. Tasmā sampadam ākaṅkhī[^238-8] sampannattho[^238-9] 'dha[^238-9] puggalo sampannapaññe[^238-10] sevetha[^238-11], evaṃ ijjhanti sampadā. Vijjācaraṇasampanno laddhā[^238-12] cittassa sampadaṃ[^238-13] karoti kammasampadaṃ labhati c'[^238-14] atthasampadaṃ[^238-15]. Lokaṃ ñatvā yathābhūtaṃ pappuyya[^238-16] diṭṭhisampadaṃ maggasampadam āgamma yāti sampannamānaso.


<!-- PTS Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 239]*

Odhunitvā malaṃ sabbaṃ patvā nibbānasampadaṃ muccati[^239-1] sabbadukkhehi, sā hoti sabbasampadā ti. XXXV.

1. Aṭṭh'; imā[^239-2] bhikkhave dānūpapattiyo. Katamā[^239-3] aṭṭha?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco dānaṃ deti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ[^239-4] yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ[^239-5]. So yaṃ deti taṃ paccāsiṃsati[^239-6]. So passati khattiyamahāsāle vā brāhmaṇamahāsāle vā gahapatimahāsāle[^239-7] vā[^239-7] pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappite samaṅgibhūte paricārayamāne. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘aho vatāhaṃ[^239-8] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā sahavyataṃ upapajjeyyan[^239-9]'; ti. So taṃ cittaṃ dahati[^239-10] taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti taṃ cittaṃ bhāveti. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīne 'dhimuttaṃ[^239-11] uttariṃ[^239-12] abhāvitaṃ[^239-13] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā sahavyataṃ upapajjati[^239-9]. Tañ ca kho sīlavato vadāmi, no dussīlassa. Ijjhati bhikkhave sīlavato cetopaṇidhi visuddhattā[^239-14].

3. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco dānaṃ deti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So yaṃ deti taṃ paccāsiṃsati. Tassa sutaṃ hoti ‘Cātummahārājikā[^239-15]


<!-- PTS Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 240]*

devā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavanto[^240-1] sukhabahulā'; ti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘aho vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ[^240-2] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyyan[^240-3]'; ti. So taṃ cittaṃ dahati[^240-4] taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti taṃ cittaṃ bhāveti. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīne 'dhimuttaṃ[^240-5] uttariṃ abhāvitaṃ[^240-6] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati[^240-3]. Tañ ca kho sīlavato vadāmi, no dussīlassa. Ijjhati bhikkhave sīlavato cetopaṇidhi visuddhattā[^240-7].

4. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco dānaṃ deti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So yaṃ[^240-8] deti taṃ paccāsiṃsati. Tassa sutaṃ hoti ‘Tāvatiṃsā devā . . . pe[^240-9] . . . Yāmā devā . . . Tusitā[^240-10] devā . . . Nimmānaratī devā . . . Paranimmitavasavattī[^240-11] devā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavanto[^240-12] sukhabahulā'; ti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘aho vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ[^240-13] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyyan[^240-3]'; ti. So taṃ cittaṃ dahati[^240-4] taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti taṃ cittaṃ bhāveti. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīne'; dhimuttaṃ[^240-14] uttariṃ abhāvitaṃ[^240-15] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati[^240-3]. Tañ ca kho sīlavato vadāmi, no dussīlassa. Ijjhati bhikkhave sīlavato cetopaṇidhi visuddhattā[^240-16].

5. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco dānaṃ deti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So yaṃ deti taṃ paccāsiṃsati. Tassa sutaṃ hoti ‘Brahmakāyikā devā


<!-- PTS Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 241]*

dīghāyukā vaṇṇavanto sukhabahulā'; ti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘aho vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Brahmakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyyan[^241-1]'; ti. So taṃ cittaṃ dahati[^241-2] taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti taṃ cittaṃ bhāveti. Tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīne 'dhimuttaṃ[^241-3] uttariṃ abhāvitaṃ[^241-4] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Brahmakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati[^241-1]. Tañ ca kho sīlavato vadāmi, no dussīlassa, vītarāgassa, no sarāgassa[^241-5]. Ijjhati bhikkhave sīlavato cetopaṇidhi vītarāgattā. Imā[^241-6] kho bhikkhave aṭṭha dānūpapattiyo[^241-7] ti. XXXVI.

1. Tīṇ'; imāni bhikkhave puññakiriyavatthūni[^241-8]. Katamāni tīṇi?

2. Dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ[^241-9], sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ.

3. Idha bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ parittaṃ kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ parittaṃ kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manussasobhaggaṃ[^241-10] upapajjati[^241-11].

4. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ mattaso kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ mattaso kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So[^241-12] kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manussasobhaggaṃ[^241-13] upapajjati.

5. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puñña-


<!-- PTS Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 242]*

kiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Tatra[^242-1] bhikkhave cattāro mahārājāno[^242-2] dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā Cātummahārājike deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti: dibbena āyunā, dibbena vaṇṇena, dibbena sukhena, dibbena yasena, dibbena ādhipateyyena[^242-3], dibbehi rūpehi, dibbehi saddehi, dibbehi gandhehi, dibbehi rasehi, dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

6. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ[^242-4] adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Tatra[^242-1] bhikkhave Sakko devānam indo dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā Tāvatiṃse deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhāti: dibbena āyunā . . . pe[^242-5] . . . dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

7. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Yāmānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Tatra bhikkhave Suyāmo[^242-6] devaputto dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā Yāme deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhāti: dibbena āyunā . . . pe[^242-5] . . . dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

8. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Tusitānaṃ[^242-7] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati.


<!-- PTS Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 243]*

Tatra bhikkhave Santusito[^243-1] devaputto dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā Tusite[^243-2] deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhāti: dibbena āyunā . . . pe[^243-3] . . . dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi[^243-4].

9. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ[^243-5] kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ[^243-5] kataṃ hoti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Tatra bhikkhave Sunimmito[^243-6] devaputto dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā Nimmānaratī deva dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhāti: dibbena āyunā . . . pe[^243-7] . . . dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

10. Idha pana bhikkhave ekaccassa dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti, sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ adhimattaṃ kataṃ hoti[^243-4], bhāvanāmayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Paranimmitanvasavattīnaṃ Devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Tatra bhikkhave Vasavattī[^243-8] devaputto dānamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puññakiriyavatthuṃ atirekaṃ karitvā Paranimmitavasavattī deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhāti: dibbena āyunā, dibbena vaṇṇena, dibbena sukhena, dibbena yasena, dibbena ādhipateyyena[^243-9], dibbehi rūpehi, dibbehi saddehi, dibbehi gandhehi, dibbehi rasehi, dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi. Imāni kho bhikkhave tīṇi puññakiriyavatthūnī ti. XXXVII.

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave sappurisadānāni. Katamāni aṭṭha?


<!-- PTS Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 244]*

2. Suciṃ[^244-1] deti, paṇītaṃ deti, kālena deti, kappiyaṃ deti, viceyya[^244-2] deti, abhiṇhaṃ deti, dadaṃ[^244-3] cittaṃ pasādeti[^244-4], datvā attamano hoti. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha sappurisadānānī ti. Suciṃ paṇītaṃ kālena kappiyaṃ pānabhojanaṃ[^244-5] abhiṇhaṃ dadāti dānāni[^244-6] sukhette[^244-7] brahmacārisu[^244-8] na[^244-9] ca[^244-9] vippaṭisāri 'ssa[^244-10] cajitvā āmisaṃ bahuṃ. Evaṃ dinnāni dānāni vaṇṇayanti vipassino, evaṃ yajitvā medhāvi saddho muttena cetasā avyāpajjhaṃ sukhaṃ lokaṃ paṇḍito upapajjatī ti. XXXVIII.

1. Sappuriso bhikkhave kule jāyamāno bahuno janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti: mātāpitunnaṃ[^244-11] atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, puttadārassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, dāsakammakaraporisassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, mittāmaccānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, pubbapetānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, rañño atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, devatānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti.

2. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave mahāmegho sabbasassāni sampādento bahuno janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya[^244-12] hoti, evam eva kho bhikkhave sappuriso kule jāyamāno bahuno janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti: mātāpitunnaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, puttadārassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, dāsakammakaraporisassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, mittāmaccānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, pubbapetānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, rañño atthāya hitāya sukhā-


<!-- PTS Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 245]*

ya hoti, devatānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hoti, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hotī ti. Bahunnaṃ[^245-1] vata atthāya sappañño gharam āvasaṃ mātaraṃ pitaraṃ pubbe rattindivam atandito pūjeti saha dhammena pubbe katam anussaraṃ anāgāre pabbajite apace[^245-2] brahmacārayo[^245-3] niviṭṭhasaddho pūjeti ñatvā dhamme ca pesalo[^245-4] rañño hito devahito ñātīnaṃ sakhinaṃ[^245-5] hito sabbesaṃ sa[^245-6] hito hoti saddhamme suppatiṭṭhito vineyya maccheramalaṃ sa lokaṃ bhajate[^245-7] sivan ti. XXXIX.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave puññābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sovaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamo puññābhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro sovaggiko sukhavipāko saggasaṃvattaniko iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti. Ayaṃ[^245-8] bhikkhave dutiyo puññābhisando . . . pe[^245-9] . . . saṃvattati.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave ariyasāvako saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti. Ayaṃ bhikkhave tatiyo puññābhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro sovaggiko sukhavipāko saggasaṃvattaniko iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati[^245-10].


<!-- PTS Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 246]*

5. Pañc'; imāni bhikkhave dānāni mahādānāni aggaññāni rattaññāni vaṃsaññāni porāṇāni asaṃkiṇṇāni asaṃkiṇṇapubbāni na[^246-1] saṃkīyanti[^246-2] na saṃkīyissanti[^246-3] appaṭikuṭṭhāni samaṇehi brāhmaṇehi viññūhi. Katamāni pañca?

6. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti. Pāṇātipātā paṭivirato bhikkhave ariyasāvako aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ deti averaṃ deti avyāpajjhaṃ deti; aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ[^246-1] abhayaṃ datvā averaṃ[^246-1] datvā[^246-1] avyāpajjhaṃ datvā aparimāṇassa abhayassa averassa avyāpajjhassa bhāgī hoti. Idaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamaṃ dānaṃ mahādānaṃ aggaññaṃ rattaññaṃ vaṃsaññaṃ porāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ na saṃkīyati[^246-4] na saṃkīyissati[^246-3] appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇehi brāhmaṇehi viññūhi. Ayaṃ bhikkhave catuttho puññābhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro sovaggiko[^246-5] sukhavipāko saggasaṃvattaniko iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati.

7. Puna ca paraṃ[^246-5] bhikkhave ariyasāvako adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^246-6] . . . kāmesu micchācāraṃ pahāya kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^246-7] . . . musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^246-7] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti. Surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato bhikkhave ariyasāvako aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ deti averaṃ deti avyāpajjhaṃ deti; aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ datvā averaṃ datvā avyāpajjhaṃ datvā aparimāṇassa abhayassa averassa avyāpajjhassa bhāgī hoti. Idaṃ bhikkhave pañcamaṃ dānaṃ[^246-8] mahādānaṃ aggaññaṃ rattaññaṃ[^246-1] vaṃsaññaṃ[^246-1] porāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ na saṃkīyati[^246-4] na[^246-1] saṃkīyissati[^246-1] appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇehi brāhmaṇehi viññūhi. Ayaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamo puññābhisando kusalā-


<!-- PTS Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 247]*

bhisando sukhassāhāro sovaggiko sukhavipāko saggasaṃvattaniko iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati. Ime[^247-1] kho bhikkhave aṭṭha puññābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sovaggikā sukhavipākā saggasavattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantī ti. XL.

1. Pāṇātipāto bhikkhave āsevito bhāvito bahulīkato nirayasaṃvattaniko[^247-2] tiracchānayonisaṃvattaniko pittivisayasaṃvattaniko[^247-3]. Yo[^247-4] sabbalahuso[^247-5] pāṇātipātassa vipāko manussabhūtassa appāyukasaṃvattaniko hoti.

2. Adinnādānaṃ bhikkhave āsevitaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ nirayasaṃvattanikaṃ tiracchānayonisaṃvattanikaṃ[^247-6] pittivisayasaṃvattanikaṃ[^247-7]. Yo[^247-4] sabbalahuso adinnādānassa vipāko manussabhūtassa bhogavyasanasaṃvattaniko hoti.

3. Kāmesu micchācāro bhikkhave āsevito bhāvito bahulīkato nirayasaṃvattaniko tiracchānayonisaṃvattaniko pittivisayasaṃvattaniko. Yo[^247-4] sabbalahuso kāmesu micchācārassa vipāko manussabhūtassa sapattaverasaṃvattaniko[^247-8] hoti.

4. Musāvādo bhikkhave āsevito bhāvito bahulīkato nirayasaṃvattaniko tiracchānayonisaṃvattaniko pittivisayasaṃvattaniko. Yo[^247-4] sabbalahuso musāvādassa vipāko manussabhūtassa abhūtabbhakkhānasaṃvattaniko[^247-9] hoti.

5. Pisuṇā bhikkhave vācā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nirayasaṃvattanikā tiracchānayonisaṃvattanikā pittivisayasaṃvattanikā. Yo sabbalahuso pisuṇāya[^247-10] vācāya vipāko manussabhūtassa mittehi bhedanasaṃvattaniko[^247-11] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 248]*

6. Pharusā bhikkhave vācā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nirayasaṃvattanikā tiracchānayonisaṃvattanikā pittivisayasaṃvattanikā. Yo[^248-1] sabbalahuso pharusāya[^248-2] vācāya vipāko manussabhūtassa[^248-3] amanāpasaddasaṃvattaniko hoti.

7. Samphappalāpo bhikkhave āsevito bhāvito bahulīkato nirayasaṃvattaniko tiracchānayonisaṃvattaniko[^248-4] pittivisayasaṃvattaniko. Yo sabbalahuso samphappalāpassa vipāko manussabhūtassa anādeyyavācāsaṃvattaniko hoti.

8. Surāmerayapānaṃ bhikkhave āsevitaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ nirayasaṃvattanikaṃ tiracchānayonisaṃvattanikaṃ pittivisayasaṃvattanikaṃ. Yo sabbalahuso surāmerayapānassa vipāko manussabhūtassa ummattakasaṃvattaniko hotī ti. Dānavaggo catuttho. Tatr'[^248-5] uddānaṃ: Dve dānāni vatthuñ ca khettaṃ dānūpapatti kiriyaṃ Dve sappurisā sabbalahuso abhisando ca vattatī ti. XLI[^248-6].

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^248-7] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato bhikkhave uposatho upavuttho[^248-8] mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro. Kathaṃ upavuttho ca bhikkhave aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro?


<!-- PTS Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 249]*

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā lajjī dayāpannā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^249-1] viharanti, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^249-1] viharāmi, iminā pi aṅgena[^249-2] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā paṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^249-3].

4. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭiviratā[^249-4] dinnādāyī dinnapāṭikaṅkhī[^249-5] athenena sucibhūtena attanā viharanti, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato dinnādāyī dinnapāṭikaṅkhī[^249-6] athenena sucibhūtena attanā viharāmi, iminā pi aṅgena[^249-2] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā dutiyena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^249-3].

5. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī[^249-7] ārācārī[^249-8] viratā methunā gāmadhammā, ahaṃ p' ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī ārācārī[^249-9] virato methunā gāmadhammā, iminā pi aṅgena[^249-10] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā tatiyena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^249-3].

6. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratā saccavādī[^249-11] saccasandhā[^249-12] ṭhetā[^249-13] paccayikā[^249-14] avisaṃvādakā lokassa, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ musāvādaṃ[^249-15] pahāya[^249-15] musāvādā paṭivirato


<!-- PTS Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 250]*

saccavādī saccasandho[^250-1] ṭheto[^250-2] paccayiko[^250-3] avisaṃvādako[^250-4] lokassa, iminā pi aṅgena[^250-5] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā catutthena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^250-6].

7. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā[^250-7], ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato, iminā pi aṅgena[^250-8] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā pañcamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^250-6].

8. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto ekabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhojanā, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ ekabhattiko rattūparato virato vikālabhojanā[^250-9], iminā pi aṅgena[^250-8] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā chaṭṭhena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^250-6].

9. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto[^250-10] naccagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilepanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ naccagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilepanadhāraṇamaṇḍa- navibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭivirato, iminā pi aṅgena[^250-11] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā sattamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^250-6].

10. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ[^250-12] pahāya[^250-12] uccāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcaseyyaṃ kappenti mañcake vā tiṇasanthārake[^250-13] vā, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uccāsayanamahāsayanā paṭivirato nīcaseyyaṃ kappemi mañcake vā tiṇasanthārake[^250-14] vā, iminā pi aṅgena[^250-8] arahataṃ


<!-- PTS Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 251]*

anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā aṭṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti. Evaṃ upavuttho kho bhikkhave aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro ti. XLII.

1. Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato bhikkhave uposatho upavuttho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro. Kathaṃ upavuttho ca[^251-1] bhikkhave aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro?

2. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā lajjī dayāpannā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^251-2] viharanti, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi, iminā pi aṅgena[^251-3] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā paṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti . . . pe[^251-4] . . .

3. ‘Yāvajīvaṃ arahanto uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uccāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcaseyyaṃ kappenti mañcake vā tiṇasanthārake[^251-5] vā, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uccāsayanamahāsayanā paṭivirato nīcaseyyaṃ kappemi mañcake[^251-6] vā[^251-1] tiṇasanthārake[^251-7] vā, iminā pi aṅgena[^251-8] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī' ti. Iminā[^251-9] aṭṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti. Evaṃ upavuttho kho bhikkhave aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahā-


<!-- PTS Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 252]*

vipphāro. Kīva[^252-1] mahapphalo hoti kīva mahānisaṃso kīva mahājutiko kīva mahāvipphāro?

4. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave yo[^252-2] imesaṃ soḷasannaṃ mahājanapadānaṃ pahūtasattaratanānaṃ[^252-3] issarādhipaccaṃ rajjaṃ kāreyya, seyyathīdaṃ Aṅgānaṃ Magadhānaṃ Kāsīnaṃ Kosalānaṃ Vajjīnaṃ Mallānaṃ Cetīnaṃ[^252-4] Vaṃsānaṃ Kurūnaṃ Pañcālānaṃ Macchānaṃ[^252-5] Sūrasenānaṃ Assakānaṃ Avantīnaṃ Gandhārānaṃ Kambojānaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatassa uposathassa etaṃ[^252-6] kalaṃ nāgghati[^252-7] soḷasiṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Kapaṇaṃ bhikkhave mānusakaṃ[^252-8] rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ[^252-9] upanidhāya.

5. Yāni bhikkhave mānusakāni[^252-10] paññāsa[^252-11] vassāni, Cātummahārājikānaṃ[^252-12] devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo[^252-13] saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni pañca vassasatāni Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ bhikkhave vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya[^252-14]. Idaṃ kho pana[^252-15] me[^252-15] taṃ[^252-15] bhikkhave sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ[^252-16] rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

6. Yaṃ bhikkhave mānusakaṃ[^252-17] vassasataṃ[^252-18], Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo[^252-19] saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbaṃ vassasahassaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devā-


<!-- PTS Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 253]*

naṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ bhikkhave vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. Idaṃ kho pana[^253-1] me[^253-1] taṃ[^253-1] bhikkhave sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ[^253-2] rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

7. Yāni bhikkhave mānusakāni dve vassasatāni, Yāmānaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni dve vassasahassāni Yāmānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ bhikkhave vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Yāmānaṃ[^253-3] devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. Idaṃ kho pana[^253-4] me[^253-4] taṃ[^253-4] bhikkhave sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

8. Yāni bhikkhave mānusakāni cattāri vassasatāni, Tusitānaṃ[^253-5] devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni cattāri vassasahassāni Tusitānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ bhikkhave vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Tusitānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. Idaṃ kho pana[^253-1] me[^253-1] taṃ[^253-1] bhikkhave sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

9. Yāni bhikkhave mānusakāni[^253-6] aṭṭha vassasatāni, Nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni aṭṭha vassasahassāni Nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ bhikkhave vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā


<!-- PTS Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 254]*

aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. Idaṃ kho pana[^254-1] me[^254-1] taṃ[^254-1] bhikkhave sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ[^254-2] rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

10. Yāni bhikkhave mānusakāni[^254-3] soḷasa vassasatāni, Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni soḷasa vassasahassāni Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ bhikkhave vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. Idaṃ kho pana[^254-4] me[^254-4] taṃ[^254-4] bhikkhave sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāyā ti. Pāṇaṃ na haññe[^254-5] na cādinnam[^254-6] ādiye musā na bhāse na[^254-7] ca majjapo siyā abrahmacariyā virameyya methunā rattiṃ na bhuñjeyya vikālabhojanaṃ[^254-8] mālaṃ na dhāraye[^254-9] na ca gandham ācare[^254-10] mañce chamāyaṃ vasayetha santhate: etaṃ hi aṭṭhaṅgikam āh'; uposathaṃ buddhena dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ. Cando ca[^254-11] suriyo[^254-12] ca[^254-13] ubho sudassanā obhāsayaṃ[^254-14] anupariyanti[^254-15] yāvatā tamonudā te pana antalikkhagā[^254-16] nabhe pabhāsanti disā virocanā,


<!-- PTS Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 255]*

etasmiṃ[^255-1] yaṃ vijjati[^255-2] antare[^255-2] dhanaṃ muttā maṇi[^255-3] veḷuriyañ ca bhaddakaṃ siṅgī suvaṇṇaṃ atha vā pi kañcanaṃ[^255-4] yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakan[^255-5] ti vuccati, aṭṭhaṅgupetassa uposathassa kalam pi te nānubhavanti soḷasiṃ candappabhā tāragaṇā va[^255-6] sabbe. Tasmā hi nārī ca naro ca sīlavā aṭṭhaṅgupetaṃ upavass'; uposathaṃ puññāni katvāna sukhudrayāni[^255-7] aninditā saggam upenti ṭhānan ti. XLIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbārāme Migāramātupāsāde. Atha kho Visākhā Migāramātā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Visākhaṃ Migāramātaraṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato Visākhe uposatho upavuttho mahapphalo hoti[^255-8] mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro. Kathaṃ upavuttho ca[^255-9] Visākhe aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro?

3. Idha Visākhe ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā lajjī dayāpannā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^255-10] viharanti, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi, iminā pi aṅgena[^255-11] arahataṃ anu-

karomi, uposatho ca[^256-1] me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā[^256-4] aṭṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti[^256-5]. Evaṃ upavuttho kho Visākhe aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro. Kīva[^256-6] mahapphalo hoti kīva mahānisaṃso kīva mahājutiko kīva mahāvipphāro?

4. Seyyathā pi Visākhe yo imesaṃ soḷasannaṃ mahājanapadānaṃ pahūtasattaratanānaṃ[^256-7] issarādhipaccaṃ rajjaṃ kāreyya, seyyathīdaṃ Aṅgānaṃ Magadhānaṃ Kāsīnaṃ Kosalānaṃ Vajjīnaṃ Mallānaṃ Cetīnaṃ[^256-8] Vaṃsānaṃ Kurūnaṃ Pañcālānaṃ Macchānaṃ Sūrasenānaṃ Assakānaṃ Avantīnaṃ Gandhārānaṃ Kambojānaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatassa uposathassa etaṃ[^256-9] kalaṃ nāgghati[^256-10] soḷasiṃ. Yāni Visākhe mānusakāni paññāsa[^256-11] vassāni, Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni pañca vassasatāni Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ Visākhe[^256-1] vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ

upapajjeyya. idaṃ kho pana[^257-1] me[^257-1] taṃ[^257-1] Visākhe sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

6. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ Visākhe vijjati, yaṃ idh' ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. 7. Yāni Visākhe mānusakāni dve vassasatāni . . . pe[^257-2] . . . cattāri vassasatāni . . . pe[^257-3] . . . aṭṭha vassasatāni . . . pe[^257-3] . . . soḷasa vassasatāni Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni soḷasa vassasahassāni Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Pāṇaṃ na haññe[^257-5] na cādinnam[^257-6] ādiye musā na bhāse na ca majjapo siyā abrahmacariyā virameyya methunā rattiṃ na bhuñjeyya vikālabhojanaṃ mālaṃ na dhāraye[^257-7] na ca gandham ācare[^257-8]
