##### *[PTS Page 258]*

mañce chamāyaṃ vasayetha santhate[^258-1]: etaṃ hi aṭṭhaṅgikam āh'; uposathaṃ buddhena dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ. Cando ca[^258-2] suriyo[^258-3] ca ubho sudassanā obhāsayaṃ[^258-4] anupariyanti[^258-5] yāvatā tamonudā te pana antalikkhagā[^258-6] nabhe pabhāsanti[^258-7] disā virocanā, etasmiṃ[^258-8] yaṃ vijjati antare dhanaṃ muttā maṇi veḷuriyañ ca bhaddakaṃ siṅgī suvaṇṇaṃ atha vā pi kañcanaṃ[^258-9] yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakan[^258-10] ti vuccati, aṭṭhaṅgupetassa uposathassa kalam pi te nānubhanti soḷasiṃ candappabhā tāragaṇā va[^258-11] sabbe. Tasmā hi nārī ca naro ca sīlavā aṭṭhaṅgupetaṃ upavass'; uposathaṃ puññāni katvāna sukhudrayāni[^258-12] aninditā saggam upenti ṭhānan ti. XLIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho Vāseṭṭho upāsako yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Vāseṭṭhaṃ upāsakaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:-- Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato Vāseṭṭha uposatho upavuttho mahapphalo hoti . . . pe[^258-13] . . . aninditā saggam[^258-14] upenti ṭhānan ti.

2. Evaṃ vutte Vāseṭṭho upāsako Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 259]*

Piyā me bhante ñātisālohitā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, piyānam pi me assa ñātisālohitānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya. Sabbe ce pi bhante khattiyā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, sabbesam pi'; ssa[^259-1] khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya. Sabbe ce pi bhante brāhmaṇā . . . pe[^259-2] . . . vessā . . . suddā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, sabbesam pi'; ssa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā ti.

3. Evam[^259-3] etaṃ Vāseṭṭha. Sabbe[^259-4] ce[^259-4] pi Vāseṭṭha khattiyā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, sabbesam pi'; ssa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya. Sabbe ce pi Vāseṭṭha brāhmaṇā . . . pe[^259-5] . . . vessā . . . suddā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, sabbesam pi'; ssa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya. Sadevako ce[^259-6] pi[^259-6] Vāseṭṭha loko samārako sabrahmako sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā[^259-7] pajāya[^259-7] sadevamanussāya[^259-7] aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, sadevakassa lokassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya. Ime ce pi Vāseṭṭha mahāsālā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavaseyyuṃ, imesam pi'; ssa mahāsālānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya, sace ceteyyuṃ[^259-8], ko pana vādo manussabhūtassā ti. XLV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Bojjhā[^259-9] upāsikā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Bojjhaṃ upāsikaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato Bojjhe uposatho upavuttho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro.


<!-- PTS Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 260]*

Kathaṃ upavuttho ca[^260-1] Bojjhe aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro?

3. Idha Bojjhe ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā lajjī dayāpannā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^260-2] viharanti, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^260-2] viharāmi, iminā pi aṅgena[^260-3] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā paṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti . . . pe[^260-4] . . . ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uccāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcaseyyaṃ kappenti mañcake vā tiṇasanthārake[^260-5] vā, ahaṃ p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uccāsayanamahāsayānā paṭivirato nīcaseyyaṃ kappemi mañcake vā tiṇasanthārake vā, iminā pi aṅgena[^260-6] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā[^260-7] aṭṭhamena aṅgena samannāgato hoti. Evaṃ upavuttho kho Bojjhe aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro. Kīva[^260-8] mahapphalo hoti kīva mahānisaṃso kīva mahāvipphāro?

4. Seyyathā pi Bojjhe yo imesaṃ soḷasannaṃ mahājanapadānaṃ pahūtasattaratanānaṃ[^260-9] issarādhipaccaṃ rajjaṃ kāreyya[^260-10], seyyathīdaṃ Aṅgānaṃ Magadhānaṃ Kāsīnaṃ Kosalānaṃ Vajjīnaṃ Mallānaṃ Cetīnaṃ[^260-11] Vaṃsānaṃ Kurūnaṃ Pañcālānaṃ Macchānaṃ Sūrasenānaṃ Assakā-


<!-- PTS Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 261]*

naṃ Avantīnaṃ Gandhārānaṃ Kambojānaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgatassa uposathassa etaṃ[^261-1] kalaṃ nāgghati[^261-2] soḷasiṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Kapaṇaṃ Bojjhe mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

5. Yāni Bojjhe mānusakāni paññāsa vassāni, Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni pañca vassasatāni Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamānaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan' etaṃ Bojjhe vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. Idaṃ kho pana[^261-3] me[^261-3] taṃ[^261-3] Bojjhe sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāya.

6. Yaṃ Bojjhe mānusakaṃ vassasataṃ . . . pe[^261-4] . . . yāni Bojjhe mānusakāni dve vassasatāni . . . pe[^261-5] . . . cattāri vassasatāni . . . pe[^261-6] . . . aṭṭha vassasatāni . . . pe[^261-6] . . . soḷasa vassasatāni, Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo, tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso, tena māsena dvādasa māsiyo saṃvaccharo, tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni soḷasa vassasahassāni Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ Bojjhe vijjati, yaṃ idh'; ekacco itthi vā puriso vā aṭṭhaṅgasamannāgataṃ uposathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjeyya. idaṃ kho pana[^261-3] me[^261-3] taṃ[^261-3] Bojjhe sandhāya bhāsitaṃ: kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ rajjaṃ dibbasukhaṃ upanidhāyā ti. pāṇaṃ na haññe[^261-7] na cādinnam[^261-8] ādiye musā na bhāse na ca majjapo siyā


<!-- PTS Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 262]*

abrahmacariyā virameyya methunā rattiṃ na bhuñjeyya vikālabhojanaṃ mālaṃ na dhāraye[^262-1] na ca gandham ācare[^262-2] mañce chamāyaṃ vasayetha santhate: etaṃ hi aṭṭhaṅgikam āh'; uposathaṃ buddhena dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ. Cando ca[^262-3] suriyo[^262-4] ca ubho sudassanā obhāsayaṃ[^262-5] anupariyanti[^262-6] yāvatā tamonudā te pana antalikkhagā[^262-7] nabhe pabhāsanti disā virocanā, etasmiṃ[^262-8] yaṃ vijjati antare dhanaṃ muttā maṇi[^262-9] veḷuriyañ ca bhaddakaṃ siṅgī suvaṇṇaṃ atha vā pi kañcanaṃ[^262-10] yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakan[^262-11] ti vuccati, aṭṭhaṅgupetassa uposathassa kalam pi te nānubhavanti soḷasiṃ candappabhā tāragaṇā va[^262-12] sabbe. Tasmā hi nārī ca naro ca sīlavā aṭṭhaṅgupetaṃ upavass'; uposathaṃ puññāni katvāna sukhudrayāni[^262-13] aninditā saggam upenti ṭhānan ti. XLVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Anuruddho divāvihāraṃ gato hoti paṭisallīno. Atha kho sambahulā manāpakāyikā devatā yenāyasmā Anuruddho ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Anuruddhaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho tā devatā āyasmantaṃ Anuruddhaṃ etad avocuṃ ‘mayaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 263]*

bhante Anuruddha manāpakāyikā nāma devatā[^263-1] tīsu ṭhānesu issariyaṃ kārema vasaṃ vattema: mayaṃ bhante Anuruddha yādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ākaṅkhāma, tādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ṭhānaso paṭibhāma; yādisakaṃ saraṃ[^263-2] ādaṅkhāma, tādisakaṃ saraṃ[^263-2] ṭhānaso paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ākaṅkhāma, tādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ṭhānaso paṭilabhāma; mayaṃ bhante Anuruddha manāpakāyikā nāma devatā imesu tīsu ṭhānesu issariyaṃ kārema vasaṃ vattenā'; ti.

2. Atha kho āyasmato Anuruddhassa etad ahosi ‘aho vat'; imā devatā sabbā 'va[^263-3] nīlā assu nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṃkārā'; ti. Atha kho tā devatā āyasmato Anuruddhassa[^263-4] cittam aññāya sabbā 'va[^263-5] nīlā ahesuṃ nīlavaṇṇā[^263-6] nīlavatthā nīlālaṃkārā[^263-7]. Atha kho āyasmato Anuruddhassa etad ahosi ‘aho vat'; imā[^263-8] devatā sabbā 'va[^263-3] pītā assu[^263-9] . . . pe[^263-10] . . . sabbā 'va[^263-3] lohitakā[^263-11] assu[^263-9] . . . sabbā 'va[^263-3] odātā[^263-12] assu[^263-9] odātavaṇṇā odātavatthā odātālaṃkārā' ti. Atha kho tā devatā āyasmato Anuruddhassa cittam aññāya sabbā 'va[^263-13] odātā ahesuṃ odātavaṇṇā odātavatthā odātālaṃkārā[^263-14]. Atha kho tā devatā ekā 'va[^263-15] gāyi, ekā 'va[^263-16] nacci, ekā 'va[^263-17] accharikaṃ[^263-18] vādesi. Seyyathā pi nāma pañcaṅgikassa turiyassa suvinītassa suppaṭippatāḷitassa[^263-10] kusalehi susamannāhatassa[^263-20] saddo hoti vaggu ca rajaniyo ca kamaniyo[^263-21] ca pemaniyo[^263-22] ca[^263-6] madaniyo[^263-23] ca[^263-23]

##### *[PTS Page 264]*

evam eva tāsaṃ devatānaṃ alaṃkārānaṃ[^264-1] saddo hoti vaggu ca rajaniyo ca kamaniyo[^264-2] ca[^264-2] pemaniyo[^264-3] ca madaniyo[^264-4] ca. Atha kho āyasmā Anuruddho indriyāni[^264-5] okkhipi. Atha kho tā devatā ‘na khv[^264-6] ayyo[^264-6] Anuruddho sādiyatī'; ti tatth' ev'; antaradhāyiṃsu.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Anuruddho sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallāṇā vuṭṭhito yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Anuruddho Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: Idhāhaṃ bhante divāvihāraṃ gato homi[^264-7] paṭisallīno. Atha kho bhante sambahulā manāpakāyikā devatā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhante tā devatā maṃ etad avocuṃ ‘mayaṃ bhante Anuruddha manāpakāyikā nāma devatā tīsu ṭhānesu issariyaṃ kārema vasaṃ vattema: mayaṃ bhante Anuruddha yādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ākaṅkhāma, tādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ṭhānaso paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ saraṃ[^264-8] ākaṅkhāma, tādisakaṃ saraṃ ṭhānaso paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ākaṅkhāma, tādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ṭhānaso paṭilabhāma; mayaṃ bhante Anuruddha manāpakāyikā nāma devatā imesu tīsu ṭhānesu issariyaṃ kārema vasaṃ vattemā'; ti. Tassa mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi ‘aho vat'; imā devatā sabbā 'va[^264-9] nīlā[^264-10] assu nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṃkārā'; ti. Atha kho bhante tā devatā mama cittam aññāya sabbā 'va[^264-9] nīlā ahesuṃ nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṃkārā. Tassa mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi ‘aho vat'; imā devatā sabbā 'va[^264-9] pītā assu[^264-11] . . . pe[^264-12] . . . sabbā[^264-13] 'va[^264-9] lohitakā assu[^264-14] . . . pe[^264-15] . . . sabbā 'va[^264-9] odātā[^264-16] assu[^264-11] odātavaṇṇā odātavatthā odātā-


<!-- PTS Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 265]*

laṃkārā'; ti. Atha kho bhante tā devatā mama cittam aññāya sabbā 'va odātā ahesuṃ odātavaṇṇā odātavatthā odātālaṃkārā[^265-1]. Atha kho bhante tā devatā ekā 'va[^265-2] gāyi[^265-2], ekā 'va[^265-3] nacci[^265-3], ekā 'va[^265-4] accharikaṃ[^265-5] vādesi. Seyyathā pi nāma pañcaṅgikassa turiyassa suvinītassa suppaṭippatāḷitassa[^265-6] kusalehi susamannāhatassa[^265-7] saddo hoti vaggu ca rajaniyo ca kamaniyo ca pemaniyo[^265-8] ca[^265-8] madaniyo[^265-9] ca, evam eva tāsaṃ devatānaṃ alaṃkārānaṃ saddo hoti vaggu ca rajaniyo ca kamaniyo ca pemaniyo[^265-10] ca[^265-10] madaniyo[^265-11] ca[^265-11]. Atha khv āhaṃ[^265-12] bhante indriyāni okkhipiṃ. Atha kho bhante tā devatā ‘na khv[^265-13] ayyo Anuruddho sādiyatī' ti tatth'; ev'; antaradhāyiṃsu. Katihi nu kho bhante dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjatī[^265-14] ti?

4. Aṭṭhahi kho Anuruddha dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati[^265-14]. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

5. idha[^265-15] Anuruddha mātugāmo[^265-16] yassa[^265-16] mātāpitaro bhattuno denti atthakāmā hitesino anukampakā anukampaṃ upādāya, tassa hoti pubbuṭṭhāyinī[^265-17] pacchānipātinī kiṃkārapaṭissāvinī manāpacārinī piyavādinī. Ye te[^265-18] bhattu garuno honti ‘mātā'; ti vā ‘pitā'; ti vā ‘samaṇabrāhmaṇā'; ti vā, te sakkaroti garukaroti māneti pūjeti abbhāgate ca āsanodakena paṭipūjeti. Ye te bhattu abbhantarā kammantā ‘uṇṇā'; ti vā ‘kappāsā'; ti vā, tattha dakkhā hoti analasā tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya samannāgatā alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ. Yo[^265-19] so[^265-19] bhattu abbantaro[^265-20] anto-


<!-- PTS Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 266]*

jano[^266-1] ‘dāsā'; ti vā ‘pessā'[^266-2] ti vā ‘kammakarā'; ti vā, tesaṃ katañ ca katato[^266-3] jānāti akatañ ca akatato[^266-4] jānāti gilānakānañ[^266-5] ca balābalaṃ jānāti, khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyañ c'; assa paccaṃsena[^266-6] saṃvibhajati. Yaṃ[^266-7] bhattā āharati dhanaṃ vā dhaññaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā, taṃ ārakkhena guttiyā sampādeti, tattha ca hoti adhuttī athenī asoṇḍī avināsikā. Upāsikā kho pana hoti buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā. Sīlavatī kho pana hoti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā adinnādānā paṭiviratā kāmesu micchācārā paṭiviratā musāvādā paṭiviratā surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā. Cāgavatī kho pana hoti vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā[^266-8] payatapāṇī vossaggaratā yācayogā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā. Imehi kho[^266-9] Anuruddha aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjatī[^266-10] ti. Yo naṃ bharati sabbadā niccaṃ ātāpi[^266-11] ussuko taṃ sabbakāmaharaṃ[^266-12] posaṃ bhattāraṃ nātimaññati. Na cāpi sotthi bhattāraṃ issāvādena rosaye[^266-13] bhattuñ ca garuno sabbe paṭipūjeti paṇḍitā. Uṭṭhāhikā[^266-14] analasā saṅgahitaparijjanā[^266-15] bhattu manāpaṃ carati sambhataṃ anurakkhati. Yā evaṃ vattati nārī bhattu chandavasānugā[^266-16] manāpā[^266-17] nāma[^266-17] te[^266-17] devā yattha sā upapajjatī ti.

XLVII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbārāme Migāramātupāsāde. Atha kho Visākhā Migāramātā . . . pe[^267-1] . . . Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Visākhā Migāramātaraṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

3. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo yassa mātāpitaro bhattuno denti atthakāmā hitesino[^267-4] anukampakā[^267-4] anukampaṃ upādāya, tassa hoti pubbuṭṭhāyinī pacchānipātinī kiṃkārapaṭissāvinī[^267-5] manāpacārinī piyavādinī . . . pe[^267-6] . . . Cāgavatī kho pana hoti vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā[^267-7] payatapāṇī vossaggaratā yācayogā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā. Imehi kho Visākhe aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjatī[^267-3] ti. Yo naṃ bharati sabbadā niccaṃ ātāpi ussuko taṃ sabbakāmaharaṃ[^267-8] posaṃ bhattāraṃ nātimaññati. Na cāpi sotthi bhattāraṃ issāvādena rosaye[^267-9] bhattuñ ca garuno sabbe paṭipūjeti paṇḍitā. Uṭṭhāhikā[^267-10] analasā saṅgahitaparijjanā bhattu manāpaṃ carati sambhataṃ anurakkhati. Yā evaṃ vattati nārī bhattu chandavasānugā[^267-11] manāpā[^267-12] nāma te devā yattha sā upapajjatī ti.

XLVIII.

Yo so bhattu abbhantaro antojano ‘dāsā' ti vā ‘pessā'; ti vā ‘kammakarā'; ti vā, tesaṃ katañ ca katato jānāti akatañ ca akatato jānāti gilānakānañ ca balābalaṃ jānāti, khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyañ c'; assa paccaṃsena[^268-7] saṃvibhajati. Yaṃ bhattā āharati dhanaṃ vā dhaññaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā, taṃ ārakkhena guttiyā sampādeti, tattha ca hoti adhuttī athenī asoṇḍī avināsikā. Upāsikā kho pana hoti buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā. Sīlavatī kho pana hoti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā . . . pe[^268-8] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā[^268-9]. Cāgavatī kho pana hoti vigatamalamaccherena

##### *[PTS Page 269]*

cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā[^269-1] payatapāṇī vossaggaratā yācayogā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā. Imehi kho Nakulamāte aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjatī[^269-2] ti. Yo naṃ bharati sabbadā niccaṃ ātāpi ussuko taṃ sabbakāmaharaṃ[^269-3] posaṃ bhattāraṃ nātimaññati. Na cāpi sotthi bhattāraṃ issāvādena rosaye bhattuñ ca garuno sabbe paṭipūjeti paṇḍitā. Uṭṭhāhikā[^269-4] analasā saṅgahitaparijjanā bhattu manāpaṃ carati sambhataṃ anurakkhati. Yā evaṃ vattati nārī bhattu chandavasānugā[^269-5] manāpā nāma te devā yattha sā upapajjatī[^269-6] ti. XLIX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbārāme Migāramātupāsāde. Atha kho Visākhā Migāramātā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami[^269-7], upasaṅkamitvā . . . pe[^269-8] . . . Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Visākhaṃ Migāramātaraṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Catūhi kho Visākhe dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo idhalokavijayāya paṭipanno hoti, {ayaṃsa}[^269-9] loko āraddho hoti. Katamehi catūhi?

3. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo susaṃvihitakammanto hoti saṅgahitaparijjano, bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo susaṃvihitakammanto hoti?

4. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo ye te bhattu abbhantarā kammantā ‘uṇṇā'; ti vā ‘kappāsā'; ti vā, tattha dakkhā hoti analasā tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya samannāgatā alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo susaṃ-


<!-- PTS Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 270]*

vihitakammanto hoti. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo saṅgahitaparijjano hoti?

5. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo yo[^270-1] so[^270-1] bhattu abbhantaro[^270-2] antojano[^270-3] ‘dāsā'; ti vā ‘pessā'; ti vā ‘kammakarā'; ti vā, tesaṃ katañ ca katato jānāti akatañ ca akatato jānāti gilānakānañ ca balābalaṃ jānāti, khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyañ c'; assa paccaṃsena[^270-4] saṃvibhajati. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo saṅgahitaparijjano hoti. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo bhattu manāpaṃ carati?

6. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo yaṃ bhattu[^270-5] amanāpasaṃkhātaṃ, taṃ jīvitahetu pi na[^270-6] ajjhācarati[^270-6]. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo bhattu manāpaṃ carati. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo sambhataṃ anurakkhati?

7. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo yaṃ bhattā āharati dhanaṃ vā dhaññaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā, taṃ ārakkhena guttiyā sampādeti, tattha ca hoti adhuttī athenī asoṇḍī avināsikā. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo sambhataṃ anurakkhati. Imehi kho Visākhe catūhi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo idhalokavijayāya paṭipanno hoti, {ayaṃsa}[^270-7] loko āraddho hoti.

8. Catūhi kho Visākhe dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo dhalokavijayāya paṭipanno hoti, parassa[^270-8] loko āraddho hoti. Katamehi catūhi?

9. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo saddhāsampanno hoti, sīlasampanno hoti, cāgasampanno hoti, paññāsampanno hoti. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo saddhāsampanno hoti?

10. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo saddho hoti, saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhiṃ ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo saddhāsampanno hoti. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo sīlasampanno hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 271 -->

##### *[PTS Page 271]*

11. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^271-1] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo sīlasampanno hoti. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo cāgasampanno hoti?

12. Idha Visakhe mātugāmo vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā[^271-2] payatapāṇī vossaggaratā yācayogā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā. Evaṃ kho[^271-3] Visākhe mātugāmo cāgasampanno hoti. Kathañ ca Visākhe mātugāmo paññāsampanno hoti?

13. Idha Visākhe mātugāmo paññavā hoti[^271-4] udayatthagāminiyā[^271-5] paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Evaṃ kho Visākhe mātugāmo paññāsampanno hoti. Imehi kho Visākhe catūhi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo paralokavijayāya paṭipanno hoti, parassa[^271-6] loko[^271-6] āraddho hotī ti. Susaṃvihitakammantā[^271-7] saṅgahitaparijjanā bhattu manāpaṃ carati sambhataṃ anurakkhati. Saddhāsīlena sampannā vadaññū vītamaccharā niccaṃ maggaṃ visodheti sotthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ. Icc'; ete aṭṭha dhammā ca[^271-8] yassā vijjanti nāriyā tam pi sīlavatiṃ āhu dhammaṭṭhaṃ saccavādiniṃ. Soḷasākārasampannā aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgatā tādisī sīlavatī upāsikā upapajjati[^271-9] devalokaṃ manāpan ti. L.

LI[^274-1].

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme. Atha kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sādhu bhante labheyya mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ[^274-2] pabbajjan'; ti. ‘Alaṃ Gotamī, mā te rucci mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjā'; ti.

2. Dutiyam pi kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sādhu bhante labheyya mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan' ti. ‘Alaṃ Gotamī, mā te rucci mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjā'; ti.

3. Tatiyam pi kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sādhu bhante labheyya mātugāmo Tathāgatappevedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan' ti. ‘Alaṃ Gotamī, mā te rucci mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjā' ti. Atha kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī ‘na Bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā[^274-3] Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi.

4. Atha kho Bhagavā Kapilavatthusmiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yena Vesālī tena cārikaṃ pakkāmi, anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena Vesālī tad avasari. Tatra sudaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane[^274-4] Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī kese chedāpetvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā sambahulāhi Sākiyānīhi[^274-5] saddhiṃ yena


<!-- PTS Page 275 -->
<a id="page-275"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 275]*

Vesālī tena pakkāmi, anupubbena yena Vesālī Mahāvanaṃ Kūṭāgārasālā ten'; upasaṅkami. Atha kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī sunehi[^275-1] pādehi rajokiṇṇena gattena dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake aṭṭhāsi. Addasā kho āyasmā Ānando Mahāpajāpatiṃ Gotamiṃ sunehi pādehi rajokiṇṇena gattena dukkhiṃ dummanaṃ assumukhiṃ rudamānaṃ bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhitaṃ, disvā[^275-2] Mahāpajāpatiṃ Gotamiṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu tvaṃ Gotamī sunehi pādehi rajokiṇṇena gattena dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhitā'; ti? ‘Tathā hi pana bhante Ānanda Bhagavā na[^275-3] anujānāti mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti. ‘Tena hi Gotamī idh'; eva tāva hohi, yāvāhaṃ Bhagavantaṃ[^275-4] yācāmi mātugāmassa Thatāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti.

5. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘esā bhante Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī sunehi pādehi rajokiṇṇena gattena dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhitā <<na[^275-5] Bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan>> ti. Sādhu bhante labheyya mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti. ‘Alaṃ Ānanda, mā te rucci mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjā'; ti. Dutiyam pi kho . . . pe[^275-6] . . . tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sādhu bhante labheyya mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagariyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti. ‘Alaṃ Ānanda, mā te rucci mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjā'; ti.

6. Atha kho āyasmato Ānandassa etad ahosi ‘na Bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjaṃ[^276-1], yan nūnāhaṃ aññena pi[^276-2] pariyāyena Bhagavantaṃ yāceyyaṃ mātugāmassa Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘bhabbo nu kho bhante mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sotāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaphalaṃ vā sacchikātun'; ti? ‘Bhabbo Ānanda mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sotāpattiphalam pi sakadāgāmiphalam pi anāgāmiphalam pi arahattaphalam pi sacchikātun'; ti. ‘Sace bhante bhabbo mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sotāpattiphalaṃ pi . . . pe[^276-3] . . . arahattaphalam pi sacchikātuṃ, bahupakārā[^276-4] bhante Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī Bhagavato mātucchā āpādikā posikā[^276-5] Bhagavantaṃ janettiyā kālakatāya thaññaṃ pāyesi, sādhu bhante labheyya mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjan'; ti.

7. ‘Sace Ānanda Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī aṭṭha garudhamme paṭigaṇhāti, sā 'v'; assā[^276-6] hotu upasampadā. Vassasatūpasampannāya bhikkhuniyā tadahūpasampannassa bhikkhuno abhivādanaṃ paccuṭṭhānaṃ añjalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ[^276-7] kattabbaṃ, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Na[^276-2] bhikkhuniyā[^276-8] abhikkhuke[^276-9] āvāse vassaṃ upagantabbaṃ, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Anvaḍḍhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghato[^276-10] uposathapucchakañ[^276-11] ca[^276-11] ovādūpasaṅkamanañ[^276-12]


<!-- PTS Page 277 -->
<a id="page-277"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 277]*

ca pariyesitabbaṃ[^277-1], ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Vassaṃ vutthāya[^277-2] bhikkhuniyā[^277-3] ubhatosaṅghe tīhi ṭhānehi[^277-4] pavāretabbaṃ[^277-5] diṭṭhena[^277-6] sutena parisaṅkāya, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Garudhammaṃ ajjhāpannāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatosaṅghe pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Dve vassāni chasu dhammesu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya ubhatosaṅghe upasampadā pariyesitabbā, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Na[^277-7] kenaci pariyāyena bhikkhuniyā bhikkhu akkositabbo[^277-8] paribhāsitabbo[^277-8], ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Ajja-t-agge Ānanda[^277-9] ovaṭo[^277-10] bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapatho, anovaṭo[^277-11] bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapatho, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Sace Ānanda Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī ime aṭṭha garudhamme paṭigaṇhāti, sā 'v'; assā[^277-12] hotu upasampadā'; ti.

8. Vassasatūpasampannāya bhikkhuniyā tadahūpasampannassa bhikkhuno abhivādanaṃ paccuṭṭhānaṃ añjalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ[^277-4] kattabbaṃ, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo . . . pe[^277-15] . . . ajja-t-agge ovaṭo[^277-16] bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapatho,

anovaṭo[^278-1] bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapatho, ayam pi dhammo sakkatvā garukatvā mānetvā pūjetvā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyo. Sace kho tvaṃ Gotamī ime aṭṭha garudhamme paṭigaṇheyyāsi, sā 'va te bhavissati upasampadā' ti. ‘Seyyathā pi bhante Ānanda itthi vā puriso vā daharo[^278-2] yuvā maṇḍanakajātiyo[^278-3] sīsaṃ nahāto[^278-4] uppalamālaṃ vā vassikamālaṃ[^278-5] vā[^278-5] adhimuttakamālaṃ[^278-6] vā labhitvā[^278-7] ubhohi hatthehi paṭiggahetvā[^278-8] uttamaṅge sirasmiṃ patiṭṭhāpeyya[^278-9], evam eva kho ahaṃ bhante ime aṭṭha garudhamme paṭigaṇhāmi yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīye'; ti.

9. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘paṭiggahitā bhante Mahāpajāpatiyā Gotamiyā aṭṭha garudhammā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyā'; ti. ‘Sace Ānanda nālabhissa[^278-10] mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjaṃ, ciraṭṭhitikaṃ Ānanda brahmacariyaṃ[^278-11] abhavissa, vassasahassam eva[^278-12] saddhammo tiṭṭheyya. Yato[^278-13] ca kho Ānanda mātugāmo Tathāgatappavedite dhammavinaye agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito, na dāni[^278-14] Ānanda brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ bhavissati, pañc'; eva dāni Ānanda vassasatāni saddhammo ṭhassati. Seyyathā pi Ānanda yāni kānici kulāni bahukitthikāni[^278-15] appapurisakāni[^278-16], tāni suppadhaṃsiyāni honti corehi kumbhatthenakehi[^278-17], evam eva kho Ānanda yasmiṃ dhammavinaye labhati mātugāmo agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti. Seyyathā pi Ānanda sampanne sālikhette


<!-- PTS Page 279 -->
<a id="page-279"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 279]*

setaṭṭhikā nāma rogajāti[^279-1] nipatati[^279-2], evaṃ taṃ sālikhettaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti, evam eva kho Ānanda yasmiṃ dhammavinaye labhati mātugāmo agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti. Seyyathā pi Ānanda sampanne ucchukhette mañjiṭṭhikā[^279-3] nāma rogajāti[^279-1] nipatati[^279-2], evaṃ taṃ ucchukhettaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti, evam eva kho Ānanda yasmiṃ dhammavinaye labhati mātugāmo agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajjaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hoti. Seyyathā pi Ānanda puriso mahato taḷākassa paṭigacc'[^279-4] eva āliṃ[^279-5] bandheyya yāva-deva udakassa anatikkamanāya, evam eva kho Ānanda mayā paṭigacc'[^279-4] eva bhikkhunīnaṃ aṭṭha garudhammā paññattā yāvajīvaṃ anatikkamanīyā'; ti. LII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘katīhi nu kho bhante dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhikkhuno'; vādako sammannitabbo'; ti? Aṭṭhahi kho Ānanda dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhikkhuno'; vādako sammannibabbo. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Idh'; Ānanda bhikkhu sīlavā hoti . . . pe[^279-6] . . . samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu, bahussuto hoti . . . pe[^279-6] . . . diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, ubhayāni kho pan'[^279-7] assa[^279-7] pātimokkhāni vitthārena svāgatāni honti suvibhattāni suppavattīni[^279-8] suvinicchitāni suttaso[^279-9] anuvyañjanaso[^279-10], kalyāṇavāco hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriya vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya[^279-11] anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā, paṭibalo


<!-- PTS Page 280 -->
<a id="page-280"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 280]*

hoti bhikkhunīsaṅghaṃ[^280-1] dhammiyā kathāya sandassetuṃ samādapetuṃ samuttejetuṃ sampahaṃsetuṃ, yebhuyyena bhikkhunīnaṃ piyo hoti manāpo, na kho pan'; etaṃ[^280-2] Bhagavantaṃ uddissa pabbajitāya kāsāyavatthanivasanāya[^280-3] garudhammaṃ ajjhāpannapubbo hoti, vīsativasso vā hoti atirekavīsativasso vā. Imehi kho Ānanda aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu bhikkhuno'; vādako sammannitabbo ti. LIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ atthāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho Mahāpajāpatī Gotamī Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sādhu me[^280-4] bhante Bhagavā saṃkhittena dhammaṃ desetu, yam[^280-5] ahaṃ[^280-5] Bhagavato dhammaṃ sutvā ekā[^280-6] vūpakaṭṭhā appamattā ātāpinī pahitattā vihareyyan'; ti.

2. Ye kho tvaṃ Gotamī dhamme jāneyyāsi ‘ime dhammā sarāgāya saṃvattanti no virāgāya, saṃyogāya saṃvattanti no visaṃyogāya, ācayāya saṃvattanti no apacayāya, mahicchatāya saṃvattanti no appicchatāya, asantuṭṭhiyā saṃvattanti no santuṭṭhiyā, saṅgaṇikāya saṃvattanti no pavivekāya, kosajjāya saṃvattanti no viriyārambhāya, dubbharatāya saṃvattanti no subharatāyā'; ti. Ekaṃsena Gotamī dhāreyyāsi ‘n'; eso dhammo, n'; eso vinayo, n'; etaṃ Satthu sāsanan'; ti.

3. Ye ca kho tvaṃ Gotamī dhamme jāneyyāsi ‘ime dhammā virāgāya saṃvattanti no sarāgāya, visaṃyogāya saṃvattanti no saṃyogāya, apacayāya saṃvattanti no ācayāya, appicchatāya saṃvattanti no mahicchatāya, santuṭṭhiyā saṃvattanti no asantuṭṭhiyā, pavivekāya saṃvattanti no


<!-- PTS Page 281 -->
<a id="page-281"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 281]*

saṅgaṇikāya, viriyārambhāya saṃvattanti no kosajjāya, subharatāya saṃvattanti no dubbharatāyā'; ti. Ekaṃsena Gotamī dhāreyyāsi ‘eso dhammo, eso vinayo, etaṃ Satthu sāsanan'; ti. LIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Koḷiyesu viharati Kakkarapattaṃ[^281-1] nāma Koḷiyānaṃ nigamo. Atha[^281-2] kho Dīghajānu Koḷiyaputto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Dīghajānu Koḷiyaputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘mayaṃ bhante gihī kāmabhogī[^281-3] puttasambādhasayanaṃ[^281-4] ajjhāvasāma kāsikacandanaṃ paccanubhoma mālāgandhavilepanaṃ dhārayāma jātarūparajataṃ sādiyāma, tesaṃ no bhante Bhagavā amhākaṃ tathā dhammaṃ desetu, ye amhākaṃ assa dhammā[^281-5] diṭṭhadhammahitāya diṭṭhadhammasukhāya samparāyahitāya[^281-6] samparāyasukhāyā'; ti.

2. Cattāro 'me Byagghapajja dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya. Katame cattāro?

3. Uṭṭhānasampadā ārakkhasampadā kalyāṇamittatā samajīvitā[^281-7]. Katamā ca Byagghapajja uṭṭhānasampadā[^281-8]?

4. Idha[^281-8] Byagghapajja[^281-8] kulaputto yena kammaṭṭhānena jīvikaṃ[^281-9] kappeti yadi kasiyā yadi vaṇijjāya[^281-10] yadi gorakkhena yadi issatthena yadi rājaporisena[^281-11] yadi sippaññatarena, tattha dakkho hoti analaso tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya samannāgato alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ[^281-12]. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja uṭṭhānasampadā. Katamā ca Byagghapajja ārakkhasampadā[^281-13]?

5. Idha[^281-13] Byagghapajja[^281-13] kulaputtassa bhogā honti


<!-- PTS Page 282 -->
<a id="page-282"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 282]*

uṭṭhānaviriyādhigatā bāhābalaparicitā[^282-1] sedāvakkhittā[^282-2] dhammikā dhammaladdhā, te ārakkhena guttiyā sampādeti ‘kinti me ime bhoge neva rājāno hareyyuṃ, na corā hareyyuṃ, na aggi ḍaheyya, na udakaṃ vaheyya[^282-3], na appiyā dāyādā hareyyun'; ti? Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja ārakkhasampadā. Katamā ca Byagghapajja kalyāṇamittatā?

6. Idha Byagghapajja kulaputto yasmiṃ[^282-4] gāme vā nigame vā paṭivasati, tattha[^282-5] ye te honti gahapati vā gahapatiputtā vā daharā vā vuddhasīlino vuddhā[^282-6] vā[^282-6] vuddhasīlino[^282-6] saddhāsampannā sīlasampannā cāgasampannā paññāsampannā, tehi saddhiṃ[^282-7] santiṭṭhati sallapati sākacchaṃ samāpajjati; yathārūpānaṃ saddhāsampannānaṃ saddhāsampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ sīlasampannānaṃ sīlasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ cāgasampannānaṃ cāgasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ paññāsampannānaṃ paññāsampadaṃ anusikkhati. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja kalyāṇamittatā. Katamā ca Byagghapajja samajīvitā[^282-8]?

7. Idha Byagghapajja kulaputto āyañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā samaṃ[^282-9] jīvikaṃ[^282-10] kappeti na[^282-11] accogāḷhaṃ[^282-12] na[^282-12] atihīnaṃ[^282-12] ‘evaṃ me āyo vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya thassatī'; ti. Seyyathā pi Byagghapajja tulādhāro vā tulādhārantevāsī vā tulaṃ[^282-13] paggahetvā[^282-14] jānāti ‘ettakena vā onataṃ[^282-15] ettakena vā unnatan'[^282-16] ti, evam eva kho Byagghapajja kulaputto āyañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ[^282-6] ca[^282-6] bhogānaṃ[^282-6] viditvā[^282-6] samaṃ[^282-17] jīvikaṃ[^282-10] kappeti na[^282-11] accogāḷhaṃ[^282-11] na[^282-12] atihīnaṃ[^282-12] ‘evaṃ me āyo vayaṃ pariyādāya


<!-- PTS Page 283 -->
<a id="page-283"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 283]*

ṭhassati[^283-1], na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti. Sacāyaṃ Byagghapajja kulaputto appāyo samāno uḷāraṃ jīvikaṃ[^283-2] kappeti, tassa bhavanti vattāro ‘udumbarakhādikaṃ[^283-3] 'vāyaṃ[^283-4] kulaputto bhoge khādatī'; ti. Sace panāyaṃ Byagghapajja kulaputto mahāyo[^283-5] samāno kasiraṃ jīvikaṃ[^283-2] kappeti, tassa bhavanti vattāro ‘ajaddhumārikaṃ[^283-6] 'vāyaṃ[^283-7] kulaputto marissatī'; ti. Yato ca khvāyaṃ[^283-8] Byagghapajja kulaputto āyañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā samaṃ[^283-9] jīvikaṃ[^283-2] kappeti na[^283-10] accogāḷhaṃ[^283-10] na[^283-11] atihīnaṃ[^283-11] ‘evaṃ me āyo vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja samajīvitā[^283-12].

8. Evaṃ samuppannānaṃ Byagghapajja bhogānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni honti: itthidhutto hoti[^283-13], surādhutto, akkhadhutto, pāpamitto pāpasahāyo pāpasampavaṅko. Seyyathā pi Byagghapajja mahato taḷākassa cattāri c'; eva āyamukhāni cattāri ca apāyamukhāni, tassa puriso yāni c'; eva āyamukhāni tāni pidaheyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni vivareyya, devo ca na sammā dhāraṃ anuppaveccheyya[^283-14]; evaṃ hi tassa Byagghapajja mahato taḷākassa parihāni[^283-15] yeva pāṭikaṅkhā no vuddhi: evam eva kho Byagghapajja evaṃ samuppannānaṃ bhogānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni honti: itthidhutto hoti[^283-16], surādhutto, akkhadhutto, pāpamitto pāpasahāyo pāpasampavaṅko.

9. Evaṃ samuppannānaṃ Byagghapajja bhogānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni honti: na-itthidhutto hoti[^283-17], na-surādhutto, na-


<!-- PTS Page 284 -->
<a id="page-284"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 284]*

akkhadhutto, kalyāṇamitto kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Seyyathā pi Byagghapajja mahato taḷākassa cattāri c'; eva āyamukhāni cattāri ca[^284-1] apāyamukhāni, tassa puriso yāni c'; eva āyamukhāni tāni vivareyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni pidaheyya, devo ca sammā dhāraṃ anuppaveccheyya[^284-2]; evaṃ hi tassa Byagghapajja[^284-3] mahato taḷākassa vuddhi yeva pāṭikaṅkhā no parihāni: evam eva kho Byagghapajja evaṃ samuppannānaṃ bhogānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni honti: na-itthidhutto hoti[^284-4], na-surādhutto, naakkhadhutto, kalyāṇamitto kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Ime kho Byagghapajja[^284-3] cattāro dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya.

10. Cattāro 'me[^284-5] Byagghapajja dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāya[^284-6]. Katame cattāro?

11. Saddhāsampadā sīlasampadā cāgasampadā paññāsampadā. Katamā ca Byagghapajja saddhāsampadā?

12. Idha Byagghapajja kulaputto saddho hoti, saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhiṃ ‘iti pi so Bhagavā . . . pe[^284-7] . . . Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja saddhāsampadā. Katamā ca Byagghapajja sīlasampadā?

13. Idha Byagghapajja[^284-3] kulaputto pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^284-7] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja[^284-3] sīlasampadā. Katamā ca Byagghapajja cāgasampadā?

14. Idha Byagghapajja kulaputto vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgo payatapāṇi vossaggarato yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja cāgasampadā. Katamā ca[^284-8] Byagghapajja paññāsampadā?


<!-- PTS Page 285 -->
<a id="page-285"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 285]*

15. Idha Byagghapajja[^285-1] kulaputto paññavā hoti, udayatthagāminiyā[^285-2] paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Ayaṃ vuccati Byagghapajja[^285-1] paññāsampadā. Ime kho Byagghapajja[^285-1] cattāro dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāyā ti. Uṭṭhātā kammadheyyesu[^285-3] appamatto vidhānavā samaṃ[^285-4] kappeti jīvikaṃ[^285-5] sambhataṃ anurakkhati, saddho sīlena sampanno vadaññū[^285-6] vītamaccharo[^285-6] niccaṃ maggaṃ visodheti sotthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ. Icc'; ete aṭṭha dhammā ca[^285-7] saddhassa gharam esino akkhātā saccanāmena ubhayattha sukhāvahā, diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya samparāyasukhāya ca: evam etaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ cāgo puññaṃ pavaḍḍhatī[^285-8] ti. LV.

1. Atha kho Ujjayo[^285-9] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Ujjayo brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘mayam assu[^285-10] bho Gotama pavāsaṃ gantukāmā, tesan no bhavaṃ Gotamo amhākaṃ tathā[^285-11] dhammaṃ desetu, ye amhākaṃ assu[^285-12] dhammā diṭṭhadhammahitāya diṭṭhadhammasukhāya samparāyahitāya samparāyasukhāyā'; ti.

2. Cattāro 'me[^285-13] brāhmaṇa dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya. Katame cattāro?


<!-- PTS Page 286 -->
<a id="page-286"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 286]*

3. Uṭṭhānasampadā ārakkhasampadā kalyāṇamittatā samajīvitā[^286-1]. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa uṭṭhānasampadā?

4. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto yena kammaṭṭhānena jīvikaṃ[^286-2] kappeti yadi kasiyā yadi vaṇijjāya yadi gorakkhena yadi issatthena yadi rājaporisena yadi sippaññatarena, tattha dakkho hoti analaso tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya samannāgato alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ[^286-3]. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa uṭṭhānasampadā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa ārakkhasampadā?

5. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputtassa bhogā honti uṭṭhānaviriyādhigatā bāhābalaparicitā[^286-4] sedāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā, te ārakkhena guttiyā sampādeti ‘kinti me[^286-5] ime bhoge neva rājāno hareyyuṃ, na corā hareyyuṃ, na aggi ḍaheyya, na udakaṃ vaheyya[^286-6], na appiyā dāyādā hareyyun'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa ārakkhasampadā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa kalyāṇamittatā?

6. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto yasmiṃ gāme vā nigame vā paṭivasati, tattha[^286-7] ye te honti gahapati vā gahapatiputtā vā daharā vā vuddhasīlino[^286-8] vuddhā[^286-9] vā[^286-9] vuddhasīlino[^286-5] saddhāsampannā sīlasampannā[^286-5] cāgasampannā paññāsampannā, tehi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhati sallapati sākacchaṃ samāpajjati; yathārūpānaṃ saddhāsampannānaṃ saddhāsampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ sīlasampannānaṃ sīlasampadaṃ anusikkhati[^286-10], yathārūpānaṃ cāgasampannānaṃ cāgasampadaṃ anusikkhati[^286-10], yathārūpānaṃ paññāsampannānaṃ paññāsampadaṃ anusikkhati. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa kalyāṇamittatā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa samajīvitā[^286-11]?

7. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto āyañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā samaṃ[^286-12] jīvikaṃ[^286-2] kappeti


<!-- PTS Page 287 -->
<a id="page-287"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 287]*

na[^287-1] accogāḷhaṃ[^287-1] na[^287-2] atihīnaṃ[^287-2] ‘evaṃ me[^287-3] āyo vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī' ti. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa tulādhāro vā tulādhārantevāsī vā tulaṃ[^287-4] paggahetvā jānāti ‘ettakena vā onataṃ[^287-5] ettakena vā unnatan'[^287-6] ti, evam eva kho brāhmaṇa kulaputto āyañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā samaṃ[^287-7] jīvikaṃ[^287-8] kappeti na accogāḷhaṃ na[^287-2] atihīnaṃ[^287-2] ‘evaṃ me āyo vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti. Sacāyaṃ brāhmaṇa kulaputto appāyo samāno uḷāraṃ jīvikaṃ[^287-8] kappeti, tassa bhavanti vattāro ‘udumbarakhādikaṃ[^287-9] 'vāyaṃ[^287-10] kulaputto bhoge khādatī'; ti. Sace panāyaṃ brāhmaṇa kulaputto mahāyo samāno kasiraṃ jīvikaṃ[^287-8] kappeti, tassa bhavanti vattāro ‘ajaddhumārikaṃ[^287-11] 'vāyaṃ[^287-12] kulaputto marissatī'; to. Yato ca khvāyaṃ[^287-13] brāhmaṇa kulaputto āyañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ ca bhogānaṃ viditvā[^287-14] samaṃ[^287-15] jīvikaṃ[^287-8] kappeti na accogāḷhaṃ na atihīnaṃ ‘evaṃ me āyo vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa samajīvitā[^287-16].

8. Evaṃ samuppannānaṃ brāhmaṇa bhogānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni honti: itthidhutto hoti[^287-17], surādhutto, akkhadhutto, pāpamitto pāpasahāyo pāpasampavaṅko. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa mahato taḷākassa cattāri c'; eva āyamukhāni cattāri ca apāyamukhāni, tassa puriso yāni c'; eva āyamukhāni tāni pidaheyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni vivareyya, devo ca na sammā dhāraṃ anuppaveccheyya[^287-18]; evaṃ hi tassa


<!-- PTS Page 288 -->
<a id="page-288"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 288]*

brāhmaṇa mahato taḷākassa parihāni[^288-1] yeva pāṭikaṅkhā no vuddhi: evam eva kho brāhmaṇa evaṃ samuppannānaṃ bhogānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni honti: itthidhutto hoti[^288-2], surādhutto, akkhadhutto, pāpamitto pāpasahāyo pāpasampavaṅko.

9. Evaṃ samuppannānaṃ brāhmaṇa bhogānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni honti: na-itthidhutto hoti[^288-2], na-surādhutto, naakkhadhutto, kalyāṇamitto kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇa mahato taḷākassa cattāri c'; eva āyamukhāni cattāri ca apāyamukhāni, tassa puriso yāni c'; eva āyamukhāni tāni vivareyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni pidaheyya, devo ca sammā dhāraṃ anuppaveccheyya[^288-3]; evaṃ hi tassa brāhmaṇa mahato taḷākassa vuddhi yeva pāṭikaṅkhā no parihāni: evam eva kho brāhmaṇa evaṃ samuppannānaṃ bhogānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni honti: na-itthidhutto hoti[^288-2] . . . pe[^288-4] . . . kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Ime kho brāhmaṇa cattāro dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya.

10. Cattāro 'me brāhmaṇa dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāya. Katame cattāro?

11. Saddhāsampadā sīlasampadā cāgasampadā paññāsampadā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa saddhāsampadā?

12. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto saddho hoti, saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhiṃ ‘iti pi so Bhagavā . . . pe[^288-5] . . . Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa saddhāsampadā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa sīlasampadā?

13. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^288-5] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa sīlasampadā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa cāgasampadā?


<!-- PTS Page 289 -->
<a id="page-289"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 289]*

14. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati . . . pe[^289-1] . . . yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa cāgasampadā. Katamā ca brāhmaṇa paññāsampadā?

15. Idha brāhmaṇa kulaputto paññavā hoti . . . pe[^289-2] . . . sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇa paññāsampadā. Ime kho brāhmaṇa cattāro dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāyā ti. Uṭṭhātā[^289-3] kammadheyyesu appamatto vidhānavā samaṃ kappeti jīvikaṃ[^289-4] sambhataṃ anurakkhati, saddho sīlena sampanno vadaññū vītamaccharo niccaṃ maggaṃ visodheti sotthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ. Icc'; ete aṭṭha dhammā ca[^289-5] saddhassa gharam esino akkhātā saccanāmena ubhayattha sukhāvahā, diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya samparāyasukhāya ca: evam etaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ cāgo puññaṃ pavaḍḍhatī ti. LVI.

1. ‘Bhayan'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘dukkhan'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘rogo' ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘gaṇḍo'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘sallan'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘saṅgo'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘paṅko'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ, ‘gabbho'; ti bhikkhave kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ.

2. Kasmā ca bhikkhave ‘bhayan'; ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ? Yasmā[^289-5] ca[^289-5] kāmarāgarattāyaṃ[^289-6] bhikkhave chandarāgavinibaddho[^289-7] diṭṭhadhammikā pi bhayā na parimuccati,


<!-- PTS Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 290]*

samparāyikā pi bhayā na parimuccati, tasmā ‘bhayan'; ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ.

3. Kasmā ca bhikkhave ‘dukkhan'; ti . . .[^290-1] ‘rogo'; ti . . . ‘gaṇḍo'; ti . . . ‘sallan'; ti . . . ‘saṅgo'; ti . . . ‘paṅko'; ti . . . ‘gabbho'; ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanaṃ? Yasmā[^290-2] ca[^290-2] kāmarāgarattāyaṃ[^290-3] bhikkhave chandarāgavinibaddho[^290-4] diṭṭhadhammikā pi gabbhā na parimuccati, samparāyikā pi gabbhā na parimuccati, tasmā ‘gabbho'; ti kāmānam etaṃ adhivacanan ti. Bhayaṃ dukkhañ ca rogo[^290-5] ca gaṇḍo[^290-2] sallañ[^290-2] ca[^290-2] saṅgo ca[^290-2] paṅko[^290-6] gabbho[^290-7] ca[^290-7] ubhayaṃ[^290-8]. Ete kāmā pavuccanti[^290-9] yattha satto puthujjano otiṇṇo sātarūpena puna gabbhāya gacchati yato ca bhikkhu ātāpi sampajaññaṃ[^290-10] na[^290-11] riñcati[^290-12]. So imaṃ palipathaṃ duggaṃ atikkamma tathāvidho pajaṃ jātijarūpetaṃ[^290-13] phandamānaṃ avekkhatī ti. LVII.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti . . . pe[^290-14] . . . samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu, bahussuto hoti . . . pe[^290-15] . . . diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo[^290-16] kalyāṇasampavaṅko, sammādiṭṭhiko hoti sammā-

dassanena samannāgato, catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^291-1] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^291-2] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena . . . pe[^291-2] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajānāti, āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^291-3] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. 1. Aṭṭh'; ime[^292-7] bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā[^292-8] añjalikaraṇīyā[^292-8] anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Sotāpanno sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sakadāgāmī sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, anāgāmī anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, arahā arahattāya paṭipanno. Yajamānānaṃ manussānaṃ puññapekhānapāṇinaṃ[^292-12] karotaṃ[^292-13] opadhikaṃ puññaṃ saṅghe dinnaṃ mahapphalan ti. LX.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā . . . pe[^292-14] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Sotāpanno sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno[^293-1], sakadāgāmī sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno[^293-2], anāgāmī anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, arahā arahattāya paṭipanno. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha puggalā āhuneyyā . . . pe[^293-3] . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. Cattāro ca paṭipannā cattāro ca phale ṭhitā: esa saṅgho samukkaṭṭho[^293-4] sattānaṃ aṭṭha puggalā. Yajamānānaṃ manussānaṃ puññapekhānapāṇinaṃ karotaṃ[^293-5] opadhikaṃ puññaṃ ettha[^293-6] dinnaṃ mahapphalan ti. Sa-ādhānavaggo[^293-7] chaṭṭho[^293-8]. Tatr'; uddānam[^293-9]: Gotamī ovādaṃ saṃkhittaṃ Dīghajānuñ ca Ujjayo[^293-10] Bhayā dve āhuneyyā ca[^293-11] dve ca aṭṭhapuggalā ti. LXI[^293-12].

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno pavivittassa viharato nirāyattavuttino icchā uppajjati lābhāya. So uṭṭhahati ghaṭati[^293-13] vāyamati lābhāya. Tassa[^293-14] uṭṭhahato ghaṭato vāyamato lābhāya[^293-14] lābho n'; uppajjati. So tena alābhena socati kilamati paridevati urattāḷiṃ[^293-15] kandati sammohaṃ āpajjati. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu iccho viharati lābhāya,


<!-- PTS Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 294]*

uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya[^294-1], na ca lābhī socicca[^294-2] paridevicca[^294-2] cuto ca saddhammā.

3. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno pavivittassa viharato nirāyattavuttino icchā uppajjati lābhāya. So uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya. Tassa uṭṭhahato ghaṭato vāyamato lābhāya lābho uppajjati. So tena lābhena majjati[^294-3] pamajjati madāpamādam[^294-4] āpajjati. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu iccho viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca madī[^294-5] ca[^294-5] pamādī[^294-3] ca[^294-3] cuto ca saddhammā.

4. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno pavivittassa viharato nirāyattavuttino icchā uppajjati lābhāya. So na uṭṭhahati na[^294-3] ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya. Tassa anuṭṭhahato aghaṭato avāyamato lābhāya lābho n'; uppajjati. So tena alābhena socati kilamati paridevati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammohaṃ āpajjati. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu iccho viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī[^294-6] socicca[^294-2] paridevicca[^294-2] cuto ca saddhammā.

5. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno pavivittassa viharato nirāyattavuttino icchā[^294-7] uppajjati lābhāya, so na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭāti na vāyamati lābhāya. Tassa anuṭṭhahato aghaṭato avāyamato lābhāya lābho uppajjati. So tena lābhena majjati pamajjati madāpamādam āpajjati. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu iccho viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca madī ca pamādī ca cuto ca saddhammā.

6. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno pavivittassa viharato nirāyattavuttino icchā uppajjati lābhāya. So uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya. Tassa uṭṭhahato ghaṭato vāya-

Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu iccho viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, na[^295-2] ca[^295-3] lābhī na[^295-4] ca[^295-4] socī[^295-4] na ca paridevī accuto ca saddhammā.

Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu iccho viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya, lābhicca[^295-6] na ca madī[^295-8] na ca pamādī[^295-8] accuto ca saddhammā. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmin ti.


<!-- PTS Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 296]*

LXII.

1. Chahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano alaṃ paresaṃ. Katamehi chahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko[^296-1] hoti, dhatānañ[^296-2] ca dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī[^296-3] hoti, attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^296-4] hoti, kalyāṇavāco ca[^296-5] hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā, sandassako ca hoti samādapako samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave chahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano alaṃ paresaṃ.

3. Pañcahi bhikkhave dhammehi sammannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano alaṃ paresaṃ. Katamehi pañcahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na h'; eva kho khippanisanti ca hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī[^296-6] hoti, attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^296-4] hoti, kalyāṇavāco[^296-7] ca[^296-4] hoti . . . pe[^296-8] . . . atthassa viññāpaniyā, sandassako[^296-9] ca hoti samādapako[^296-10] samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacārīnaṃ Imehi kho bhikkhave pañcahi[^296-11] dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano alaṃ[^296-12] paresaṃ.

5. Catūhi[^296-13] bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^296-14] paresaṃ. Katamehi catūhi?

6. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko


<!-- PTS Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 297]*

hoti, dhātānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^297-1] hoti, no[^297-2] ca[^297-2] kalyāṇavāco hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā, no ca sandassako hoti samādapako samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave catūhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^297-3] paresaṃ.

7. Catūhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^297-4] attano. Katamehi catūhi?

8. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hoti[^297-5] kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, no ca[^297-2] dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, no[^297-6] ca attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti, kalyāṇavāco[^297-7] ca hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo . . . pe[^297-8] . . . atthassa viññāpaniyā, sandassako ca hoti . . . pe[^297-9] . . . sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave catūhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^297-10] attano.

9. Tīhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^297-11] paresaṃ. Katamehi tīhi?

10. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na[^297-12] h'; eva kho khippanisanti ca hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti[^297-13], dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti[^297-13], attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^297-14] hoti, no ca kalyāṇavāco hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya

16. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu na h'; eva kho khippanisanti ca[^298-9] hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, no ca dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthupa-


<!-- PTS Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 299]*

parikkhī hoti, no ca attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti, kalyāṇavāco ca hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā, sandassako ca hoti samādapako[^299-1] samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacarīnaṃ. Imehi kho bhikkhave dvīhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no attano ti. LXIII.

1. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami[^299-2] . . . pe[^299-3] . . . Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘sādhu me bhante Bhagavā saṃkhittena dhammaṃ desetu, yam ahaṃ Bhagavato dhammaṃ sutvā eko vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto vihareyyan'; ti. Evam eva pan'; idh'; ekacce moghapurisā mamañ[^299-4] ñeva[^299-4] ajjhesanti, dhamme ca bhāsite mamañ ñeva anubandhitabbaṃ maññantī ti. ‘Desetu me[^299-5] bhante Bhagavā saṃkhittena dhammaṃ, desetu Sugato saṃkhittena dhammaṃ, app eva nāmāhaṃ[^299-6] Bhagavato bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājāneyyaṃ[^299-7], app eva nāmāhaṃ[^299-8] Bhagavato bhāsitassa dāyado[^299-9] assan'; ti.

2. Tasmā ti ha te bhikkhu evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Ajjhattaṃ[^299-10] me[^299-10] cittaṃ ṭhitaṃ bhavissati susaṇṭhitaṃ, na c'[^299-11] uppannā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassantī ti. Evaṃ hi te bhikkhu sikkhitabbaṃ.

3. Yato kho te bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ ṭhitaṃ[^299-5] hoti susaṇṭhitaṃ, na c'[^299-12] uppannā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, tato te bhikkhu evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:--


<!-- PTS Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 300]*

Mettā me cetovimutti bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā[^300-1] vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā ti. Evaṃ hi te bhikkhu sikkhitabbaṃ.

4. Yato kho te bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti bahulīkato[^300-2], tato tvaṃ bhikkhu imaṃ samādhiṃ savitakkam pi savicāraṃ bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi vicāramattaṃ[^300-3] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi[^300-4] avicāraṃ[^300-5] bhāveyyāsi, sappītikam[^300-1] pi[^300-1] bhāveyyāsi[^300-5], nippītikam pi bhāveyyāsi, sātasahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi, upekhāsahagatam[^300-6] pi bhāveyyāsi. Yato kho te[^300-7] bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti subhāvito, tato te[^300-8] bhikkhu evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Karuṇā me cetovimutti . . .[^300-9]muditā me cetovimutti . . .[^300-10] upekhā me cetovimutti bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā ti. Evaṃ hi t e bhikkhu sikkhitabbaṃ.

5. Yato kho te bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti[^300-11] bahulīkato, tato tvaṃ bhikkhu imaṃ samādhiṃ savitakkam pi savicāraṃ[^300-12] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi vicāramattaṃ[^300-12] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi avicāraṃ[^300-12] bhāveyyāsi, sappītikam pi bhāveyyāsi, nippītikam pi bhāveyyāsi, sātasahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi, upekhāsahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi. Yato kho te bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti subhāvito, tato[^300-1] te[^300-1] bhikkhu evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Kāye kāyānupassī viharissāmi[^300-13], ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassan ti. Evaṃ hi te bhikkhu sikkhitabbaṃ.

6. Yato kho te bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti bahulīkato, tato tvaṃ bhikkhu imaṃ samādhiṃ savitakkam pi savicāraṃ[^300-5] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi vicāra-


<!-- PTS Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 301]*

mattaṃ[^301-1] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi avicāraṃ[^301-2] bhāveyyāsi, sappītikam pi bhāveyyāsi, nippītikam[^301-3] pi[^301-3] bhāveyyāsi[^301-3], sātasahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi, upekhāsahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi. Yato kho te bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti subhāvito, tato te bhikkhu evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Vedanāsu[^301-4] . . .[^301-5] citte . . .[^301-6] dhammesu dhammānupassī viharissāmi, ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassan ti. Evaṃ hi te bhikkhu sikkhitabbaṃ.

7. Yato kho te bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti bahulīkato, tato tvaṃ bhikkhu imaṃ samādhiṃ savitakkam pi savicāraṃ[^301-7] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam[^301-8] pi vicāramattaṃ[^301-9] bhāveyyāsi, avitakkam pi avicāraṃ[^301-7] bhāveyyāsi, sappītikam pi bhāveyyāsi, nippītikam pi bhāveyyāsi, sātasahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi, upekhāsahagatam pi bhāveyyāsi. Yato kho te[^301-10] bhikkhu ayaṃ samādhi evaṃ bhāvito hoti subhāvito, tato tvaṃ[^301-11] bhikkhu yena yen'; eva gacchasi[^301-12], phāsu yeva gacchasi[^301-12], yattha yattha ṭhassasi, phāsu yeva ṭhassasi, yattha yattha nisīdissasi, phāsu[^301-10] yeva[^301-10] nisīdissasi[^301-10], yattha yattha seyyaṃ kappessasi[^301-13], phāsu yeva seyyaṃ kappessasī[^301-14] ti.

8. Atha kho so[^301-15] bhikkhu Bhagavatā iminā ovādena ovadito[^301-16] uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi. Atha kho so bhikkhu eko vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto na cirass'; eva yass'; atthāya kulaputtā samma-d-eva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ[^301-17] pabbajanti, tad anuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭh' eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi,


<!-- PTS Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 302]*

‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā'; ti abbhaññāsi[^302-1], aññataro ca[^302-2] pana[^302-2] so bhikkhu arahataṃ ahosī ti. LXIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Gayāyaṃ[^302-3] viharati Gāyāsīse. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi[^302-4]:-- Bhikkhavo ti[^302-5]. Bhadante ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Pubbāhaṃ bhikkhave sambodhā[^302-6] anabhisambuddho bodhisatto 'va[^302-7] samāno obhāsaṃ hi[^302-8] kho sañjānāmi, no ca rūpāni passāmi. Tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhave etad ahosi ‘sace kho ahaṃ obhāsañ c'; eva sañjāneyyaṃ rūpāni ca[^302-9] passeyyaṃ, evam me idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā'; ti. So kho ahaṃ bhikkhave aparena samayena appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto obhāsañ c'; eva sañjānāmi rūpāni ca[^302-10] passāmi, no ca kho tāhi[^302-11] devatāhi[^302-11] saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi[^302-12] sallapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpajjāmi.

3. Tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhave etad ahosi ‘sace kho ahaṃ obhāsañ c'; eva sañjāneyyaṃ rūpāni ca passeyyaṃ tāhi ca devatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭheyyaṃ sallapeyyaṃ sākacchaṃ samāpajjeyyaṃ, evam me[^302-13] idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā'; ti. So kho ahaṃ bhikkhave aparena samayena appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto obhāsañ c'; eva sañjānāmi rūpāni ca passāmi tāhi ca[^302-9] devatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sallapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpajjāmi, no ca kho tā devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā devanikāyā'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 303]*

4. Tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhave etad ahosi ‘sace kho ahaṃ obhāsañ c'; eva sañjāneyyaṃ rūpāni ca passeyyaṃ tāhi ca[^303-1] devatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭheyyaṃ sallapeyyaṃ sākacchaṃ samāpajjeyyaṃ tā ca[^303-2] devatā jāneyyaṃ <<imā devatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā devanikāyā>> ti, evam me idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā'; ti. So kho ahaṃ bhikkhave aparena samayena appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto obhāsañ c'; eva sañjānāmi rūpāni ca passāmi tāhi ca[^303-3] devatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sallapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpajjāmi tā ca devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā[^303-4] amukamhā vā amukamhā vā devanikāyā'; ti, no ca kho tā devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā imassa kammassa vipākena ito cutā tattha[^303-5] upapannā[^303-5]'; ti tā ca[^303-2] devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā imassa kammassa vipākena ito cutā tattha upapannā'; ti, no[^303-6] ca kho tā devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā[^303-7] evamāhārā evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvediniyo[^303-8]'; ti tā ca[^303-9] devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā[^303-10] evamāhārā evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvediniyo'; ti, no ca kho tā devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā evaṃdīghāyukā evaṃciraṭṭhitikā'; ti tā ca[^303-3] devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā evaṃdīghāyukā evaṃciraṭṭhitikā'; ti, no ca kho tā devatā jānāmi ‘yadi vā[^303-11] me imāhi devatāhi saddhiṃ sannivutthapubbaṃ[^303-12] yadi vā na sannivutthapubban'; ti.

5. Tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhave etad ahosi ‘sace kho ahaṃ obhāsañ c'; eva sañjāneyyaṃ rūpāni ca passeyyaṃ tāhi ca[^303-13] devatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭheyyaṃ sallapeyyaṃ sākacchaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 304]*

samāpajjeyyaṃ, tā ca[^304-1] devatā jāneyyaṃ <<imā devatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā devanikāyā>> ti tā ca[^304-2] devatā jāneyyaṃ <<imā devatā imassa kammassa vipākena ito cutā tattha upapannā>> ti tā ca[^304-2] devatā jāneyyaṃ <<imā devatā evamāhārā evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvediniyo>> ti tā ca[^304-3] devatā jāneyyaṃ <<imā devatā evaṃdīghāyukā evaṃciraṭṭhitikā>> ti tā ca[^304-4] devatā jāneyyaṃ <<yadi vā me imāhi devatāhi saddhiṃ sannivutthapubbaṃ yadi vā na sannivutthapubban>> ti, evam me idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā'; ti. So kho ahaṃ bhikkhave aparena samayena appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto obhāsañ c'; eva sañjānāmi rūpāni ca passāmi tāhi ca[^304-5] devatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sallapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpajjāmi tā ca[^304-6] devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā devanikāyā'; ti tā ca devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā imassa kammassa vipākena ito cutā tattha upapannā'; ti tā ca[^304-7] devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā evamāhārā evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvediniyo'; ti tā[^304-8] ca[^304-9] devatā jānāmi ‘imā devatā evaṃdīghāyukā evaṃciraṭṭhitikā'; ti tā ca devatā jānāmi ‘yadi vā me tāhi devatāhi saddhiṃ sannivutthapubbaṃ yadi vā na sannivutthapubban'; ti.

6. Yāva[^304-10] kīvañ[^304-10] ca[^304-11] me[^304-11] bhikkhave evaṃ aṭṭhaparivaṭṭhaṃ[^304-12] adhidevañāṇadassanaṃ na suvisuddhaṃ ahosi, neva tāvāhaṃ bhikkhave sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambuddho[^304-13] paccaññāsiṃ[^304-14]. Yato ca kho me bhikkhave evaṃ aṭṭhaparivaṭṭaṃ[^304-12] adhidevañāṇadassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ ahosi, athāhaṃ bhikkhave


<!-- PTS Page 305 -->
<a id="page-305"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 305]*

sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambuddho[^305-1] paccaññāsiṃ[^305-2]. Ñāṇañ ca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi ‘akuppā me cetovimutti[^305-3], ayam antimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavo'; ti. LXV.

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave abhibhāyatanāni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

2. Ajjhattaṃ rūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ.

3. Ajjhattaṃ rūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ dutiyaṃ[^305-4] abhibhāyatanaṃ.

4. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ.

5. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ.

6. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ pañcamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ.

7. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 306 -->
<a id="page-306"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 306]*

8. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati lohitakāni lohitakavaṇṇāni lohitakanidassanāni lohitakanibhāsāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ.

9. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko bahiddhā rūpāni passati odātāni odātavaṇṇāni odātanidassanāni odātanibhāsāni ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī'; ti evaṃsaññī hoti. Idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha abhibhāyatanānī ti. LXVI.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave vimokhā[^306-1]. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Rūpī rūpāni passati, ayaṃ paṭhamo vimokho.

3. Ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī eko[^306-2] bahiddhā rūpāni passati, ayaṃ dutiyo vimokho.

4. Subhan t'; eva adhimutto hoti, ayaṃ tatiyo vimokho.

5. Sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, ayaṃ catuttho vimokho.

6. Sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, ayaṃ pañcamo vimokho.

7. Sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, ayaṃ chaṭṭho vimokho.

8. Sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, ayaṃ sattamo vimokho.

9. Sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, ayaṃ aṭṭhamo vimokho. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha vimokhā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 307 -->
<a id="page-307"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 307]*

LXVII.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave anariyavohārā. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Adiṭṭhe diṭṭhavāditā, asute sutavāditā, amute mutavāditā, aviññāte viññātavāditā, diṭṭhe[^307-1] adiṭṭhavāditā, sute asutavāditā, mute amutavāditā, viññāte aviññātavāditā. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha anariyavohārā ti. LXVIII.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave ariyavohārā. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Adiṭṭhe adiṭṭhavāditā, asute asutavāditā, amute amutavāditā, aviññāte aviññātavāditā[^307-2], diṭṭhe diṭṭhavāditā, sute sutavāditā, mute mutavāditā, viññāte viññātavāditā. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha ariyavohārā ti. LXIX.

1. Aṭṭh'; imā bhikkhave parisā. Katamā aṭṭha?

2. Khattiyaparisā, brāhmaṇaparisā, gahapatiparisā, samaṇaparisā, Cātummahārājikaparisā[^307-3], Tāvatiṃsaparisā, Māraparisā, Brahmaparisā.

3. Abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave anekasataṃ khattiyaparisaṃ upasaṅkamitvā[^307-4]. Tatra pi mayā sannisinnapubbañ c'; eva sallapitapubbañ ca sākacchā ca samāpannapubbā, tattha yādisako tesaṃ vaṇṇo hoti, tādisako[^307-5] mayhaṃ[^307-5] vaṇṇo[^307-5] hoti[^307-5], yādisako tesaṃ saro hoti, tādisako mayhaṃ saro hoti, dhammiyā ca[^307-6] kathāya sandassemi samādapemi samuttejemi sampahaṃsemi, bhāsamānañ ca maṃ na[^307-6] jānanti ‘ko nu kho ayaṃ bhāsati devo vā manusso vā'; ti? Dhammiyā[^307-7] ca[^307-8] kathāya sandassetvā samādapetvā samuttejetvā sampahaṃsetvā antaradhāyāmi, antarahitañ ca maṃ na jānanti ‘ko nu kho ayaṃ antarahito devo vā manusso vā'; ti?


<!-- PTS Page 308 -->
<a id="page-308"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 308]*

4. Abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave anekasataṃ brāhmaṇaparisaṃ . . .[^308-1] gahapatiparisaṃ . . . samaṇaparisaṃ . . . Cātummahārājikaparisaṃ . . . Tāvatiṃsaparisaṃ . . . Māraparisaṃ[^308-2] . . . Brahmaparisaṃ upasaṅkamitvā[^308-3]. Tatra pi[^308-4] mayā sannisinnapubbañ c'; eva sallapitapubbañ ca sākacchā ca samāpannapubbā, tattha yādisako tesaṃ vaṇṇo hoti, tādisako mayhaṃ vaṇṇo hoti, yādisako tesaṃ saro hoti, tādisako mayhaṃ saro hoti, dhammiyā ca[^308-5] kathāya sandassemi samādapemi samuttejemi sampahaṃsemi, bhāsamānañ ca maṃ na[^308-6] jānanti ‘ko nu kho ayaṃ bhāsati devo vā manusso vā'; ti? Dhammiyā ca[^308-7] kathāya sandassetvā samādapetvā samuttejetvā sampahaṃsetvā antaradhāyāmi, antarahitañ ca maṃ na jānanti ‘ko nu kho ayaṃ antarahito devo vā manusso vā'; ti? Imā kho bhikkhave aṭṭha parisā ti. LXX.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Vesāliṃ[^308-8] piṇḍāya pāvisi. Vesāliyaṃ[^308-9] piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi:--

2. Gaṇhāhi Ānanda nisīdanaṃ, yena Cāpālacetiyaṃ[^308-10] ten'; upasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paṭissutvā[^308-11] nisīdanaṃ ādāya Bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhito piṭṭhito anubandhi.

3. Atha kho Bhagavā yena Cāpālacetiyaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Nisajja kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi:--


<!-- PTS Page 309 -->
<a id="page-309"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 309]*

4. Ramaṇīyā Ānanda Vesālī, ramaṇīyaṃ Udenacetiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ Gotamakacetiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ Bahuputtakacetiyaṃ, ramanīyaṃ Sattambacetiyaṃ[^309-1], ramanīyaṃ Sārandadacetiyaṃ[^309-2], ramaṇīyaṃ Cāpālacetiyaṃ. Yassa kassaci Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, ākaṅkhamāno so Ānanda kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā. Tathāgatassa kho Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, ākaṅkhamāno Ānanda Tathāgato kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā ti. Evam pi kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavatā oḷārike nimitte kayiramāne[^309-3] oḷārike obhāse kayiramāne nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na[^309-4] Bhagavantaṃ yāci ‘tiṭṭhatu bhante Bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu bhante Sugato kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lokānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānan'; ti, yathā taṃ Mārena pariyuṭṭhitacitto.

5. Dutiyam pi kho Bhagavā . . . Tatiyam pi kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi:--

6. Ramaṇīyā Ānanda Vesālī, ramaṇīyaṃ Udenacetiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ Gotamakacetiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ Bahuputtakacetiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ Sattambacetiyaṃ[^309-5], ramaṇīyaṃ Sārandadacetiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ Cāpālacetiyaṃ. Yassa kassaci Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā[^309-6] ākaṅkhamāno so Ānanda kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā. Tathāgatassa kho Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā[^309-7] yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, ākaṅkhamāno Ānanda Tathāgato kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā ti. Evam pi kho āyasmā Ānando Bha-


<!-- PTS Page 310 -->
<a id="page-310"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 310]*

gavatā oḷārike nimitte kayiramāne oḷārike obhāse kayiramāne nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na[^310-1] Bhagavantaṃ yāci ‘tiṭṭhatu bhante[^310-2] Sugato kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lokānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānan' ti, yathā taṃ Mārena pariyuṭṭhitacitto.

7. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi ‘gaccha tvaṃ Ānanda, yassa dāni kālaṃ maññasī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā Bhagavato avidūre aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle nisīdi.

8. Atha kho Māro pāpimā acirapakkante āyasmante Ānande Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu Sugato, parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato, bhāsitā kho pan'; esā bhante Bhagavatā vācā ((na tāvāhaṃ pāpima parinibbāyissāmi, yāva me bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacārino sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahetvā ācikkhissanti desissanti[^310-3] paññāpessanti paṭṭhapessanti[^310-4] vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti[^310-5], uppannaṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā[^310-6] sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desissantī)) ti. Etarahi bhante bhikkhū Bhagavato sāvakā viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacārino sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahetvā ācikkhanti desenti paññāpenti paṭṭhapenti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaronti, uppannaṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā[^310-6] sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desenti. Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu Sugato, parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato, bhāsitā kho pan'; esā bhante Bhagavatā vācā ((na tāvāhaṃ pāpima parinibbāyissāmi, yāva me bhikkhuniyo na sāvikā bhavissanti . . .[^310-7] yāva[^310-8] me upāsakā na
